NIMA MATARSA CE
             NA
HABIBA ABUBAKAR IMAM
  ( YAR GIDAN IMAM )
 BABI NA TALATIN DA BAKWAI
           *************
    Durmi kauye ne me ruko da al'ada da kaucema duk wani abu da zai zamanto sanadiyyar zubewar mutuncinsu. Hakan yasa suke taka tsan tsan da rayuwar yayansu gami da killacesu , duk da kasancewar talauci da ràshin wadataccen ilimi agarin to bai hana iyaye tsayawa akan tarbiyyar yayansu ba. Musamman da garin kowa ya San kowa domin a wancan lokacin mutanen garin babu yawa.
   Mahaifin Hajara mallam sule Yana daya daga cikin irin wadannan mutanen da suke kafe da idanunsu akan yayansu tare da kokarin tabbatar da tarbiyya akansu.

Duk da Hajara itace kadai Allah ya bar masa acikin Jerin yayan da matarsa daya tilo zainabu ta Haifa masa guda biyar, duk sun rasu ita kadaice tayi saura hakan yasa ya dauki kauna da so na duniya ya Dora Mata, talaka ne sosai domin bai aje ba baiba wani ajiya ba  duk da gidansa babu yawa to daker ake Samun abinda zaaci .

 Noma yake yi Wanda kafin wata shekarar ta zagayo ya Gama kakkabar da komai babu dai tayi kaka gida acikin rayuwarsa hatta gidan da suke ciki ba nashi bane haya yake yi, tunda shima baida wasu yan' uwa Kuma babu abinda ya mallaka daga gadon mahaifinsa tunda shima Bai da Shi.
 
   Rayuwar Hajara ta cigaba ba yabo ba fallasa babu karatun boko sai dai makarantar allo Wanda itace kadai ta taimaketa arayuwa.zainabu kuwa babu wata Sana'a sai dai dan aikatau shima din Saita samu, hakan yasa ake zaune babu Mai taimakon wani. Rana tsaka Allah ya dorama mallam sule lalurar ciwon kafa daji ya kama masa kafa tun Yana jurewa ya na dauriya ya dingisa ya fita yadanyi buga bugarsa har abu ya Fara fassakara domin yadda kafar ta kumbura tayi suntum ga azabar ciwo mara musaltuwa dole ya tsuguna agida ana maganin gargajiya shima din sai Allah yasa an samu Wanda zai iya hakurin bada bashi . Sannu ahankali itama daya kafar ta kama ruwa mai wàri na fita daga jikinsu. Awannan lokaci babu wani tashin hankalin da Hajara da mahaifiyarta suka taba shiga sama da wannan arayuwarsu, ciwo ya samu da talauci haka zainabu take fita aikatau ita da Hajara domin Samun abinda zaa ci da Wanda zaayi magani.

Abin takaici Kuma babu mai taimaka musu domin kowa ta Kansa yake Masu Dan zuwa gaida Shi ne suke dan bada kudin da bazai isa ayi komai ba. Rayuwa fa tayi ma gidan mallam sule zafi dole tasa yar gonar tasa ta rufin asiri ya daga ya sayar domin yin magani. Alokacin ne aka basu shawarar zuwa babban asibiti a jigawa domin a duba Shi.
  Aminin malllam sule mallam kallah shine yayi masa rakiya zuwa general hospital na jigawa. Gwajin farko aka gano cancer ce ta kamashi Kuma harta Fara yaduwa ajikinsa . Take suka bashi gado aka cigaba da gwaje gwaje . Mallam kallah shine ya Komama zainabu da labarin an kwantar dashi don haka itace ya kamata ta je ta zauna dashi.

Hakan yasa sauran Yan kudin gonar ta tattara washegari Mallam kallah yayi musu jagora da zainabu da Hajara zuwa jigawa. Aranar Shi ya dawo gida su Kuma suka cigaba da zaman jinyar Mallam sule.kullum cikin bukatar kudin magani ake ga abinda zasu ci kullum haka suke Zama da yunwa ko Kuma idan masu jinya sun rage abinci su Basu su ci. Zaman asibiti dai Bana kare ba domin duk kudin hannunsu sun gama kakkabewa, hakan yasa  zainabu take tura Hajara wajen asibiti tana Dan nema musu taimako.

Wata Mata da take jinyar danta Haj lami idonta nakan su zainabu sosai take tausaya musu domin ta fahimci suna cikin tsananin talauci , sau tari duk abincin da aka kawo Mata tana duba ta Basu tana Kuma Kula kwarai da hankali da nutsuwar irinta Hajara .cikin kankanin lokaci Haj lami ta Saba da zainabu ita kanta zainabun bata kyashin gaya Mata halin kuncin da suke ciki Basu da mataimaki sai Allah. Wata ranar laraba ne Haj lami ta dubi zainabu tace " gaskiya Mijin ki Yana jinya Mai wahala ,ba zaku iya da asibitin nan ba" zainabu ta numfasa tace " gamu gatan kowa Allah, ai Haj idan ba Dama tattarawa zamuyi mu koma gida kawai" Haj lami tace " to ammafa ciwon nan yayi yawa ajikinsa bazai yuwu ace wai Ku koma gida ahaka ba" tace " to ya zamuyi babu ai bata gwaninta" Haj lami ta gyara zamanta tace " da babu damuwa da nayi miki wani taimako" zainabu tace " ta yaya zaa damu da taimako ai abinda Muke nema Kenan don Allah hajiya ina jinki". Haj lami tace " tunda yarinyarki tana da nutsuwa akwai wata Haj me kudi ce sosai mijinta ya rasu ya barta da ya daya gashi baa Dade ba ta Kara aure shine take ta neman me aiki , shine Naga ko Zan Kai Mata itane ta Fara Mata aiki kafin asallameku kinga anan zata samu abinda zaku ci da Kuma kudin aikinta don Zan CE kullum ne zaa dinga bata albashinta basai wata ba kinga kafin asallame Ku akalla kun Sami abubuwan da zaku rage wahala da wannan fita neman taimakon da take yi awaje ai ina ganin gara wannan din".

Zainabu ta dubi Haj lami cike da godiya da jindadi tace " aiko idan kika min haka kin Gama min komai Kuma kin taimakeni ba Kuma Zan taba mantawa ba ". Haj lami tace " to shikenan tunda kin amince Bari nayi Mata magana". Haj lami ta dakko waya ta danna ta Kira Haj ta Dade tana yi Mata bayani kafin ta aje wayar sannan ta dubi zainabu tace " kinga ta amince , tace anjima idan ta zo gaida yarona zata ganta Saita tafi da ita" zainabu ta jinjina Kai tace " Alhamdulillah nagode Haj Allah ya saka da alheri".

  suna cikin tattaunawa Hajara ta shigo, Zainabu ta dubeta tace " kin Dan Samo wani abun ko?" Ta girgiza Kai tace " babu abinda na Samo Inna magaji ne dai yasa na dawo ciki" zainabu ta ja hannunta ta zaunar da ita , cikin nutsuwa ta shiga Mata bayanin komai , sannan Hajara ta numfasa tace " babu komai Inna in dai hakan zai saku farinciki babu komai zanje nayi aikin" zainabu tace " Allah yayi Miki albarka kinji Hajara", ita kanta Haj lami ta gamsu da hankalin Hajara Shi yasa take son ta taimakesu.

  Bayan magriba hajiyar ta iso da ita da mijinta, sun duba jikin Dan Haj lami da Kuma Mallam sule .hajiyar ta Dade tana kallon Hajara sosai irin Mai aikin da take so kenan me jini ajika da zata iya ko wane irin aiki ba tare da Ganda ba .shima mijin nata idonsa nakan Hajara. Haj lami ta Kara gabatar da zainabu da Kuma dalilin son yarta tayi aiki agunsu .Haj ta dubi me jinyar tace " Allah ya bashi lafiya ba bu komai ko yaushe aka sallamesu zasu iya tafiya da yarsu idan Kuma zasu barta ta cigaba da aikin to duk babu damuwa zamu fi bukatar hakan ma". Daga bisani ta dauki kudi masu yawa taba zainabu .

 Mallam sule na kallo ba don ransa ya so ba aka tafi da Hajara.sai bayan tafiyarsu ne ya kasa hakuri ya dubi zainabu yace " yanzu da kika bada yarinyar nan zainabu kinsan matar nan ne kinsan duk inda take ?Baki San komai ba akansu Amma kin bada  yarki an tafi da ita" zainabu tace " naji azuciyata Haj lami ba zata cutar da ni ba domin bata da kamar haka " yace " Shi mugu Dama Yana da kama ne? Ki daiyi hattara Allah Kuma ya tsare mana Hajara ". Shigowar Haj lami Kenan ta tsinkayi maganar ta karaso tana cewa" Kada Ku damu Hajara tana kyakkyawan hannu babu abinda zai sameta in sha Allah babu matsala " zainabu ta dube Shi tace " kaji ko Mallam babu komai fa" ya jinjina Kai yace "Allah yasa ".

   Duk da haka Mallam sule Bai Sami kwanciyar hankali ba sai bayan kwana biyu da direba ya kawo Hajara asibitin ta duba Shi. Nan da nan har tayi Kyan gani tana sanye da sabuwar doguwar Riga harda abinci ta kawo musu Kula biyu zainabu ta kasa dauke kallonta ga Hajara Sbd yadda tayi kyau acikin kwanaki biyu kawai harta canja.alokacin ko da Hajara ta Fara Basu labarin kirkin hajiyar zainabu Baki ta saki tace" Dama akwai irin wadannan mutanen a birni ," Haj lami tayi dariya tace " kwarai kuwa harma Wanda ya fita , ai na gaya muku Baku da matsala da Haj " Hajara tace " gaskiya ne Inna gaskiya najidadin kwanakin da mukayi tare lafiya lau ga yarta harta Saba Dani ko yanzu da kika Muka rabu."

 Hankalin malllam sule da na zainabu yayi matukar kwanciya .sai yamma sannan direba ya tafi da Hajara bayan taba zainabu kudin kwanaki biyu na aikinta da aka bata. Hakan ko bakaramin farinciki ya dasa a zuciyarsu ba.

   Akalla Hajara ta Sami sati biyu agidan aikinta, Kuma ta Zama Yar gida kullum tana tare da yar Haj me kusan shekaru hudu bacci ne kadai ke rabasu gashi Haj na matukar jindadin aikinta hakan yasa ta sakar Mata komai har yarta domin Hajara ke yi Mata komai. Daga bisani ma hatta bacci tare da Hajara suke yi. A gefe daya Kuma duk bayan kwana biyu Hajara tana zuwa asibiti ta Basu kudin aikinta.an sallami su Haj lami tuni Amma itama jefi jefi tana zuwa ta dubasu.

Hajara tana jindadin zaman gidan aikinta idan Ka dauke wasu acikin halayyar maigidàn da bata so ,Yana yawan sa Mata Ido kallo duk Wanda inda tabi da son ya sata aiki Yana Kure Mata da ido.domin dai Hajara shekara ra goma Sha shida ta shiga ko watanni bakwai baya akwai baikon  sani akanta sa rana kawai  ayanzu ya rage, Hajara tana da kyau da cikar diri kwarai da gaske ina ganin shine yake Jan hankalin Alh Amma Hajara bata taba zaton akwai wata boyayyiyar manufa azuciyarsa ba akanta.

  Wata daya da sati uku da Fara aikin Hajara aka sallami Mallam sule da sharadin dawowa asibit bayan wata uku sun Kuma bashi magungunan na watanni ukun Wanda idan ya dawo zasu sa ranar da zasuyi masa aiki .bayan sallamar tashi da kanta hajiya ta bada mota aka dakko su zuwa gidanta . Su zainabu sun gigita da ganin irin arzikin gidan Haj gida kamar a aljanna cewar zainabu Shi ko Mallam sule tasbihi kawai yake yi ga ubangijin talikai .dakin baki aka saukesu akalla saida sukayi kwanaki uku sannan Haj ta cika musu mota da Kayan abinci da sutura Wanda kafin nan da kamshi Mallam sule yace abar Mata Hajara idan akasa bikinta ya kusa ta dawo gida. Sai dai harda Hajara ayima iyayenta rakiya zuwa Durmi da yar Haj daga bisani ita kanta hajiyar taga kysutuwar ta bisu domin taga inda suke zaune tunda ko cikin labari bata taba Jin wani kauye ba wai Shi durmi.don haka tare suka tafi tunda Ko suka kama hanya zainabu da Mallam sule suke shima hajiya Albarka tare da addua Kala Kala.

Suna ajesu suka juyo duk da Hajara taso ganin sani Amma mallam sule yace zaiyi masa bayanin komai .sosai mutanen kauye suka shiga surutun ina Mallam sule ya Samo wannan hamshakiyar matar meye Kuma dalilin barin Mata tilon yarsa da yayi. Amininsa Mallam kalla kadai ya tsaya yayima bayani ta Kuma fahimce Shi ya gamsu da baida wata mafita bayan wannan ba Kuma wani abu bane tunda baida Mai taimaka masa sai Allah .

Shiko sani ko da yazo gaida Mallam sule dafatan yaga Hajara bai ganta ba sai bayanin da Mallam sule yayi masa, Wanda Sam bai gamsu ba ya dai bishi da to kawai Amma ransa ya baci Bai Kuma jidadin yadda zaayi masa haka ba, kujifa bayan ko kafin  Mallam sule  ya tafi asibiti sau biyu ya taba gaida Shi agida ba tare da ko Sisi ba  Bai Kuma taba zuwa jigawa ya gaida Shi ba . Don haka banga dalilin daukar zafi ba din yarsa ta tsaya don ta rufa masa asiri.

  Hajara dai tana can jigawa tana bautar Haj da yarta da mijinta ba tare da gazawa ba .ta sadaukar da dukkan karfinta wajen yi musu hidima.wanda awannan lokacin idanun Alh Suna dore akanta kullum zuciyarsa na kitsa masa abubuwa da dama , domin ya yarda da cewa tunda yake aduniya babu wata mace da ya ji ta azuciyarsa bayan Hajara Sonta yake kwarai da gaske burinsa ace ya aureta Amma yasan masifar kishin Matarsa.

Wanda yasani tunda mijinta ya rasu suke tare Amma bata yarda zata aureshi ba har saida Allah yayima Matarsa rasuwa da kanta tace zata aureshi, yafi kowa sanin kishinta to Amma ina zai Kai tsananin so da shaawar da yakema Hajara da ko muryarta ya ji sai ya rasa aduniyar da yake saboda so.

TO BE CONTINUE
