HABIBA ABUBAKAR IMAM
  ( YAR GIDAN IMAM )
BABI NA TALATIN DA TAKWAS
            **********
   Sosai Alh yake son Hajara ya rasa sukuni ,kullum zuciyarsa tana Kara kwadaita masa Hajara.

Ayayinda ita bata gane komai ba aikin gabanta take yi kawai.kullum tana tare da yar Haj shakuwa mai tsanani ta Kara shiga tsakaninsu .

Hajara tayi matukar Samun nutsuwa bata da damuwar komai domin tana cikin wadatuwa da komai, kamar yadda tasan can ma iyayenta wahala ta Ragu musu tunda suna Samu daga aikin da take yi naira bata dauka su take turoma.idan ma akwai abinda ke damunta to ba zai wuce maganar sani ba da babu wata hanya ta haduwarsu, tana son sani bata son duk wata baraka da zata iya shiga tsakaninsu. Bata gane sani na cikin damuwa akan zamanta a jigawa ba sai wani zuwa da tayi gida kusan fada sukayi sosai domin yace lalle sai dai ta aje aikin ta dawo gida Wanda ta kasa yi masa alkawarin zata iya yin hakan domin alokacin ta Kara tabbatar da cewa itace gatan iyayenta kusan ci da shansu da kudin maganin mahaifinta duk ta sanadiyyar aikin nan take samunsu domin ita kanta tasan cewa ta hadu da mutanen kirki babu abinda zata ce ma Haj sai dai godiya mara musaltuwa.

   Don haka haka ta dawo jigawa babu wata cikakkiyar fahimta tsakaninta da sani , abin Kuma Yana damunta Yana kuma yi Mata ciwo, sai dai bata da yadda zatayi iyayenta sune gaba akan komai.

Lokacin dawowar Mallam sule asibiti yayi sun dawo Kuma likita ya duba ya Kara dibar masa watanni shida akan ya dawo asa ranar aiki , sun Kuma Kara masa mgunguna, wannan daga ranar aikin da sukeyi ya Fara sa ma su zainabu shakkun ayya kuwa ba suna Jan maganar bane saboda suna ganin ko ciwon nasa Bana tashi bane, domin dai har alokacin jikinsa cikin tsanani yake sai dai sauki na Allah.kwanakinsu biyu agidan Haj ta Kara bada mota da Kayan abinci aka maidasu gida.

  Alokacin ne bayan dawowarsu gida aka Sanya ranar Auren Hajara da sani watanni Tara masu zuwa.duk da daga sanin har mahaifansa babu wani Wanda ya nuna jindadi ko sha'awar hakan, kawai dai sun Zama kamar Yan abi yarima asha Kida.

 Aranar da Hajara ta sanar da Haj ansa ranar aurenta , ta tayata murna ta Kuma Fara tunanin ranar da zasu rabu domin tasan zata shiga damuwar rashinta kasancewar ta saki jiki ta shaku da Hajara yarta ma ta shaku da ita tabbas tasan ranar rabuwa akwai matsala da damuwa.

Amma ta bangaran Alh kuwa ranar kasa bacci yayi , ta yaya Hajara zatayi aure bayan itace mace ta farko da yake Jin tamkar numfashinta zai fita idan ya tuna bazai iya samunta a matsayin Mata ba.ya rasa inda zaisa kanta Bai tabbatar ya fada masifar kaunarta ba sai awannan daren da Sam ya kasa runtsawa washegari ma haka ya tashi kamar Wanda yayi jinya duk Wanda ke gidannan ya fahimci akwai canji da matsala atare da Alh ,ya kasa ci ko sha, sai wani azababben ciwon Kai da ya addabe shi.bai Samun sauki sai ya dubi Hajara .lamarin ya zamto tamkar wata masifa agareshi.

Washegari bayan fitar Haj .Alh ya rutsa Hajara a dakinta, tayi gaggawar tashi daga Kan gadon tana kallonsa cike da tsarguwa da daburcewa tace muryarta na rawa " kana son wani abu ne?  Ya girgiza Kai gami da zaunawa a gefen madubin kusa da gadon yayi Mata kallon tsaf sannan yace " koma ki zauna magana nake son yi da ke" asanyaye ta koma ta zauna tana kallonsa hakanan taji gabanta na matukar faduwa.

  " Ansa Miki ranar aure Ko?" Cike da mamakin tambayar tace " eh " .ya ja numfashi ya aje sannan yace " hakanne yasa naji zuciyata ba zata iya dauka ba" tayi saurin cewa " me yasa?" Ya dubeta sosai kamar daga sama Hajara taji yace " saboda ina matukar sonki fiye da komai a rayuwata Hajara" tayi zumbur ta Mike tana yi masa kallo Mai cike da mamaki tace " subhanallah me zanji haka, don Allah kayi hakuri Ka rufa min asiri Ka daina wannan maganar Kada Ka Kara yinta don Allah" ya Matso kusa da ita yace " Bana tsoron kowa ya ji Hajara ina sonki " ta juya da sauri zata fita daga dakin nan da nan yasa hannu ya fisgota ya dawo da ita cikin dakin 

  Hajara ta gigice idanunta suka Fara fitar da hawaye tace " don Allah Kada Ka bata min rayuwa kayi hakuri" yace cikin jajayen idanu wadanda da alamun sun manta komai basa ganin komai sai Hajara da kwadayin kasancewa da ita.

" Babu kowa agidan nan Hajara bazan iya jurewa ba , ina cikin matsanancin hali na shaawarki Bana iya bacci Baba iya yin komai sai Tunaninki ki taimakeni yau na sauke shaawata babu abinda bazan iya yi Miki ba arayuwa Zan jiyar dake dadi Zan maida ke Yar gata Zan Kula da mahaifanki Zan dauki nauyin jinyar mahaifin.....?

 A gaggauce cikin tashin hankali ta dakatar dashi tace cikin hawaye " kaji tsoron Allah a matsayin ya nake awajenka domin nasan Ka haifeni ,Ka dubeni a matsayin ya Ka taimakeni Kada Ka watsa rayuwata". Ya nufota cikin rufewar idanu ta juya da gudu zata fita cikin ihu .

Nan ma karfi yasa ya janyota wannan Karon kafa yasa ya tura kofar ita Kuma yayi wurgi da ita Kan gado tana ihun neman dauki tana turje turje komai na duniya ya tsaya Mata cak tana ganin abin tamkar mafarki.irin ihun da take yi yasa ya fisgo dankwalin kanta ya tura Mata acikin bakinta gami da bankare hannayenta . Duk yadda taso ta ture Shi abin ya gagara domin dai yafi karfinta hawaye ne kadai ke fita Mata masu matukar zafi da radadi.

Shi Kam Bai San me yake yi ba Mayen shaawar Hajara ya mantar dashi komai .ba tunani domin kwakwalwarsa ta toshe bata ko yin aiki alokacin. Ya Zama tamkar dabba domin haka ya fadama Hajara ya keta Mata rigar mutuncinta ya bata tarbiyyar da iyayenta sukayi Mata shekara da shekaru .

  Ya Gama duk abinda ransa ya ke so.dai dai lokacin haj ta bugo kofa ta shigo dakin da alamar ba ma yanzu ta dawo gidan ba ta Dade da dawowa.bakinta babu abinda yake fada sai innalillahi wainna'ilaihirrajiun, cikin matukar gigita da dimauta Alh ya tashi Yana kokarin maida kayansa Yana tsuma zuwa lokacin kuwa Hajara bata San a duniyar da take ba gaba daya numfashinta ya dauke ta Suma. Kan Alh taso tabi Amma ganin Hajara kwance babu alamar numfashi atare da ita yasa ta tsallake Shi ta nufi wajenta tana Kiran sunanta tana girgizata.

Ganin hakan yasa Alh ya tsallake da gudu ya fice daga dakin.iya tsorata da gigita Haj tayi ta rasa inda zatasa kanta ta Dade tana girgiza Hajara har yar karamar yarta ta shigo itama ganin Hajara kwance haka duk da batasan menene ba kuka ta shiga yi .Haj ta yaye zanin gado ta rufama hajara.sannam ta tashi cikin rawar jiki ta dakko ruwa ta shiga shafa Mata a fuska, hakanne yayi sanadiyyar dawowar Hajara hayyacinta.tana hada Ido da Haj ta Kara fashewa da kuka harda gunji Haj tasa hannu ta rungumeta lokaci daya Kuma na rasa tunanin da tayi Saita cire Haj daga jikinta ta Mike tana kallonta sosai tace " Hajara menene yafaru? Cikin rawar murya gami da galabaita ta gaya Mata komai.

Haj ta koma jikin bango ta jingina gami da lumshe idonta sai alokacin hawaye ya fita daga cikin idanunta.cikin kuka Hajara tace " ina zansa Kaina shikenan rayuwata ta kare Haj".

Haj ta Matso da sauri kusa da ita fuskarta ta canja sosai maimakon tausayin Hajara dake cikin kwayar idonta yanzu babu komai sai kyama da tsana tace " kimin shuru, Hajara na rantse Miki idan kika fitar da wannan maganar wani waje, Saina sabauta rayuwarki domin Bana wasa da mutuncina Zan iya yin komai domin tabbatar da kima da daraja ta bata salwanta ba". Hajara ta tsaida idonta akanta a gigice tace " Haj ashe bazaki taimakeni ayi adalci atsakaninmu ba kema fa kin Haifa Kuma kema ya macece ina Zan Kai wannan kuncin rayuwar" tayi Mata wani irin mugun kallo tace " na gaya Miki kibar wannan maganar anan ki rufama kanki asiri, yanzu ki tashi kije ki yi wanka ki gyara kanki Zan Kira likita ya dubaki Bana son Kuma kiyi wata magana " tana Gama fadin haka ta juya cikin matukar tashin hankali ta fice daga dakin.wanda zuwa lokacin ma tuni Alh ya fice da kayan sawansa  yabar gidan.

Hajara ta Kara gigicewa ganin irin jinin da ya bata Kan gadon. A haka ta tashi cikin matukar galabaita da tashin hankali da bakinciki adaddafe ta shiga bandaki harta Gama wanka da gyara kanta kuka take tuni idanunta suka kumbura sukayi suntum.

Tana daki ta kasa fitowa zazzabi me zafi ne yayi awon gaba da ita .likita ya zo Haj ta kawo Shi har dakinta ya dubata ya Kuma rubuta Mata  magunguna haka suka fita da likitan ba tare da Haj tayima Hajara magana ba sai dai kallo daya zakayi mata Ka tabbatar da cewa tana cikin matsanancin tashin hankali mara musaltuwa.

Aranar aka sayo Mata magani.amma Alh Bai dawo gidan ba har washegari Bai dawo ba. Tun Kuma daga Randa abin ya faru Haj ta daina yima Hajara magana. Itama tana dai kwance adaki ta ji sauki Amma tana cikin tashin hankali da damuwa.

Aranar Haj ta shigo ta dubeta tace ta shirya zaa kaita gida, gabanta yayi matukar faduwa ta so tayi Mata magana sosai Amma babu fuska don haka ta hadiye maganganunta .ta tashi ta shirya kayanta.haj ta Kara shigowa ta Mika Mata kudi masu yawa tace ta rike ta fito Kuma direba Yana jiranta.

Adaki Haj ta aje yarta ta kulle saboda tasan idan taga fitar Hajara to ranar bazaayi sukuni agidan ba ta dinga kuka da damuwa Kenan. Haj ta dubi Hajara tace " Zan zo garin, babu damuwa" Hajara Kai kawai ta gyada 

Har suka kama hanyar Durmi Hajara tana cikin matukar tashin hankali tare da mamakin canjawar Haj .zuciyarta Kuma na bijiro Mata da tambayar ina Alh ? Sannan ya zatayi tayima iyayenta bayanin abinda ya faru? Ta ina zata Fara?.har suka Kai Durmi bata cikin sukuni.su kansu su Mallam sule sunyi mamakin ganinta domin dai sun San bata dade ba rabonta da garin.

Amma da sukayi Mata tambayar dalilin zuwanta ce musu tayi su Haj zasuyi tafiyane shine tace tazo gida idan suka dawo Saita dawo.hakanan zainabu taji bata gamsu da abinda Hajara ta fada ba, musamman data Lura da ràshin nutsuwar da take ciki da ràshin walwala gashi fuskarta ta nuna bata da lafiya, shin meke faruwa? Tambayar farko kenan da ta Fara yima kanta.

Haka Hajara ta cigaba da Zama a Durmi cikin ràshin kwanciyar hankali da nutsuwa duk Dan Kyan da tayi yanzu ya disashe kallo daya zakayi mata kasan tana cikin damuwa.sai awani dare Wanda ya Kai sati hudu da dawowarta gida, harta kwanta zainabu ta tasheta tace " Hajara nasan ba bacci kikeyi ba domin na San tunda kika dawo daga birni bakya bacci kawai dai kina kwance ne," Jin hakan yasa Hajara ta tashi ta zauna tana kallon zainabun fuska cike da damuwa da bakinciki da tun dawowarta basa rabo da fuskarta. Zainabu tace tana kallonta " ni na haifeki Hajara Kuma nafi kowa sanin ki don haka Bana son ki boye min komai domin ko kin boye fuskarki ta bayyana damuwar da kike ciki tun daga ranar da kika dawo gidan nan, shin meke faruwa? Wani laifin kikama Haj data sa aka dawo da ke gida kince Kuma tace zata zo idan ta dawo Amma yau kusan sati hudu Kenan da dadaworki amma bata zo ba,don haka ina son ki gaya min gaskiyar komai".

Nan da nan idanun Hajara suka Kara cika da hawaye ta fado jikin zainabu ta rungumeta tsan tsan kamar zata fasa jikinta ta shiga lokaci daya Kuma kuka Mai karfi ya Kara kwace Mata.

Hakan yayi matukar gigita zainabu ta dago fuskarta tana tambayarta " ki natsu Hajara kiyi min bayani menene ?". Hajara ta cigaba da kokarin tsaida kukanta bakinta na fadin " Inna rayuwata ta Riga ta lalace, bansan ya zanyi da wannan mummunar kaddarar data Fado min ba, bansan ya zanyi ba". Furucin Hajara ya Kara gigita zainabu ta riketa sosai ta daka Mata tsawa tace " ke ki natsu ki gaya min meya faru........
   TO BE CONTINUE

DAGA TASKAR YAR GIDAN IMAM

NIMA MATARSA CE
            NA
HABIBA ABUBAKAR IMAM
  ( YAR GIDAN IMAM )
BABI NA TALATIN DA TARA
           ***********
  Hajara ta hadiye wani zazzafan yawu tace cikin kukan da ta kasa tsaida Shi.

" Inna ina cikin tsaka mai wuya,kullum zuciyata cikin masifar Kuna da damuwa take domin.na rufe bakina naki gayama kowa damuwata balle naji saukin masifar damuwar da nake ciki"

Zainabu adaburce tace " Hajara gaya min Kada kisa zuciyata ta buga , gaya min". Sannu ahankali Hajara ta Gama sanar da mahaifiyarta komai, Wanda zuwa wannan lokacin tuni zainabu ta fita hayyacinta nannauyan numfashi kawai take saki zuciyarta kuwa kamar ana rura Mata wuta ta Kai minti daya ta kasa magana sai Ido data zubama Hajara, saida Hajara tasa hannu tayi ta girgizata sannan zainabu ta dawo hayyacinta hade da fitar da hawaye daga kwarmin idanunta, 

Cikin rawar murya tace " innalillahi wainna'ilaihirrajiun, yanzu Hajara abinda ya faru da ke Kenan Amma kika ki gaya mana bayan mune yakamata mu Fara sanin wannan bakar kaddarar " kuka take sosai tana cewa " Amma Haj ta ci amanar mu , tana nufin tace duk irin wannan ta'addancin da mijinta yayi Miki Shi take goyama baya, ta turo ki tace zata zo taki zuwa duk girman wannan maganar " Hajara ta goge hawayenta tace " tun kafin tahowata gaba daya ta canja min na fuskanci son zuciya da son Kai irin na mutanen birni Yana nan acikin zuciyarta " zainabu tace " me suka dauki mutanen kauye?, Marasa galihu ko marasa daraja wadanda Basu San yancin kansu ba?. Hajara har yanzu cikin kuka take tace " Inna ya zanyi da wannan bakincikin" zainabu ta kama Kai ta rike Kam Jin wani abu take yi Yana yi Mata yawo duhu kawai take gani agabanta ta rasa abinda ke yi Mata dadi.

Zainabu ta dago ta dubeta tace " Hajara ina son wannan maganar mu barta Bana son wani yaji wannan maganar, domin kinfi kowa sanin mutanen garin nan ba zasu taba yi mana Uzuri ko adalci ba matukar wannan mummunar maganar ta fita,mu aje ta anan mu cigaba da zaman jiran zuwan Hajiyar, duk da banji a raina zata iya taimaka mana da komai ba", ta Kara kallon Hajara tace " cuta dai an Riga an cucemu duk abinda zasu yi mana bazai wanke damuwa da bakincikin da suka jefamu ciki ba, shiko Alh bazan taba yafe masa ba har abada".

Hakika tunda zainabu take bata taba shiga bakinciki da tashin hankali irin na wannan dare ba, domin shine dalilin da itama bata Kara Samun bacci ba da daddare, duk dare hakanan suke kwanciya da ita da Hajaran duk babu mai iya runtsawa.nan da nan damuwa ta Fara nuna su duk da Mallam sule kwance yake Yana ji da Kansa to fa ya fahimci akwai damuwa haka yayi ta tambayar meke samunsu amma zainabu tana basarwa tana nuna masa damuwar ciwon sa ne kadai ke damunsa.

Amma duk da haka kullum cikin tambayar yaushe Hajara zata koma jigawa yake daga karshe dai zainabu ta gaya masa zasu Dade Basu dawo daga tafiyar da sukayi ba , shiyasa Hajara ba zata koma ba har sai sun dawo.

   Ahankali kwanaki suka shude satittika suka shude ayau watannin Hajara biyu da dawowa Durmi , Wanda har yanzu wannan lokacin Bai dauke damuwa da fargabarsu Hajara da mahaifiyarta ba, musamman da har zuwa wannan lokacin babu amo babu labarin Haj.

Ciwon mallam sule Kuma kullum gaba yake yi Dan saukin ma da ake Samu yanzu babu sai dai na musulunci.

  A wañnan lokacin ne Hajara ta Fara ciwon da aka Rasa Gane Kansa, laulayi sosai take yi duk abinda ta ci sai ya dawo.

 Hankalin zainabu yayi mummunan tashi ta fada wani mugun damuwar domin idan har abinda zuciyarta take raya Mata gaskiya ne tabbas sun shiga cikin babbar masifa wacce bazata misaltu ba.

  Awani dare ne zainabu ta tada Hajara ta Kura Mata Ido cike da fargaba tayi Mata tambayar " Hajara yaushe rabonki da ganin al'adarki?". Duk da Hajara bata San ma'anar tambayar ba sai da gabanta ya Fadi sosai , ta gyada Kai tace " rabona da ganinsa tun ina jigawa Inna". Wata irin kibiya ta tsire zainabu a kahon zuciyarta kanta ya Sara wasu zafafan hawaye suka dinga fita Mata, ta Dora kanta a jikin cinyoyinta tana Jin kamar ta hadiye zuciya ta mutu.

   Hajara ta rude da ganin mugun halin da mahaifiyarta ta fada , ta matsa kusa da ita ta ce " Inna lafiya menene? Ta dago tana dubanta duba Mai cike da tausayi tace " Hajara ke Kuma ahaka kaddarar ki ta zo , Hajara ciki gareki".

   A dimauce ta dafe kirjinta hannu bibbiyu ta fiddo idanu waje tace " ciki Inna" zainabu tayi saurin rufe bakinta tace " kiyi ahankali Kada babanki ya jiyo ki, Wanda nayi Imani hakan na iya yin ajalinsa". Hajara ta rufe bakinta tana fitar da sautin kukanta a hankali.

   Ta  rasa a duniyar da take , bata jindadin komai , ta rasa ma wane irin tunani kwakwalwarta zatayi.haka suka hadu su kayita kuka gwajin ban tausayi.

  Washegari kuwa da wuri zainabu ta dauki Hajara zuwa asibiti domin ta tabbatar da zarginta.cikin kankanin lokaci aka Gama duk wani gwaje gwaje .sakamako Kuma ya fito da tabbacin Hajara tana dauke da cikin watanni biyu.

  Tashin hankali Wanda baa sa masa rana!. Tsayawa faden tashin hankalin da suka fada ciki  baima taso ba illa dai Hajara da zainabu sun fada tashin hankali mara musaltuwa.

   Tunda suka dawo gida zainabu take neman hanyar da zasu bi amma gaba daya kanta ya kulle, tana son yarta bata son ace zaa zubar da cikin nan ta zo ta rasa ranta adalilin hakan .

   To Amma lokaci yayi da dole Mallam sule zaisan abinda ake ciki.

   Amma kafin hakan ta yanke shawarar daukar Hajara zuwa jigawa gidan Alh domin dole su San abinda ake ciki lokacin Kuma karbar ma Hajara hakki da yanci yazo domin ayau komai talauci da kasawarsu sai ta yima Alh haukar da zaiyi nadamar taba Mata ya.

 Basu bar garin ba saida zainabu ta Sami almajirin da zai zauna Mata da Mallam sule har su dawo.

  Shi Kuma Mallam sule ta gaya masa cewa zata je gidan Haj ne saboda su gani don shurun yayi yawa.wanda ya a mince da hakan batare da tunanin komai ba , musamman da yasan komai ya tsaya tunda Dan taimakon da suke musu ya daina zuwa.gashi ba waya balle Akira asan halin da ake ciki.

  Tun azuba suka bar kauyen Durmi kaf dinsu cikin mugun yanayi da tashin hankali.

Ba wasu kudi gareta ba gun matar Mallam kallah sabira ta aro kudin motar.

  Sun isa jigawa da wuri Hajaran majaran, Hajara tana da hankali da nutsuwa hakan yasa da kanta tayi musu jagora har zuwa gidan Àlh a mashin.

   Amma wani abin Mai Kara dasa tashin hankali shine ganin gidan a rufe, bugun duniyar nan shuru alamar babu kowa a gidan.
  
  Duk da haka kofar gidan suka zauna suna zaman jiran tsammani ga yunwar da ko abincin suka Samu ba zasu iya ci ba saboda tsabar ràshin kwanciyar hankali.

Basu San iya tsawon lokacin da suka dauka a kofar gidan ba, kafin wani Dan dattijo ya iso kofar gidan ya Fara kokarin Bude get din gidan.

 Hakan ne yasa suka tashi da sauri zainabu ta Karasa kusa dashi ta yi masa sallama sannan ta Dora da tambayarsa " don Allah masu gidan fa" ya dubeta sosai yace " ai babu kowa agidan , ba"a Riga an tare ba tukuna" tayi saurin kallon Hajara sannan ta maida kallonta ga mutumin tace " aah ina fa nufin mutanen da suke Zama agidan " ya girgiza Kai yace " oho, na fahimta na fahimta, ai sun tashi tun wajen watanni biyu da suka wuce asalima sun saida gidanne Wanda ya saya ne ya daukeni aikin gadi har yanzu Kuma Bai tare ba a gidan . Zainabu ta lalubi Katanga ta jingina domin ji tayi tana neman faduwa idanunta gaba daya suka rine suka koma jajir, kamar yadda itama Hajara ta rikice ta Dora hannu aka kukanta ya dawo sabo. Cikin rawar murya zainabu tace " don Allah ko kasan inda suka koma" ya girgiza Kai yace " Kai Banda masaniya amma dai ance basama kasar Dukansu Alh da hajiyan da yarsu ance kamar sun tafi Ko London ko'ina amma dai tabbas basa kasar" zainabu ta juya tana neman faduwa Hajara ta kamata ta rike ayayinda mutumin ya karasa Bude get din ya shige cikin gidan.

Tun anan kofar gidan zainabu ta hadu da mummunan ciwon da baa San wane iri bane illa dai sun San damuwa itace sila.

A daddafe da taimakon Hajara suka iya dawowa gida , zainabu tunda ta kwanta ko motsi bata iyawa, ita kanta bata San meke Mata ciwo ba, tasan dai babu abinda zata iya aikatawa domin babu karfi ko kadan atare da ita.damuwa da tashin hankalin Hajara ya dawo sabo duk mahaifanta suna kwance babu Mai iya tada wani tayi hidimar wannan tayi hidimar wancan ga bakin tashin hankali da damuwa da ràshin nutsuwar da yake damunta ga fitinar laulayi itama yau lafiya gobe ciwo.ta rasa abinda ke Mata dadi domin bata San ya zata bulloma al'amarin ba shin shikenan haka zata zauna ta haife abinda ke cikinta?
 
  Dole tasa zainabu sanar da Mallam sule komai ba don komai ba sai don ya cancanci ya Sani bata Kuma San gawar fari ba tsakaninsu Kada ta rigashi mutuwa , yasan maganar ya dauka ta yaudara Shi ne ta Kuma raina lamarinsa yasa bata gaya masa babban lamarin da ya shafi yarsa ba ,domin ayadda take Jin jikinta da yanayin damuwar da take ciki bata tsammanin tana da sauran wani lokaci nan gaba.

  Sai dai kusan zainabu ta so tayi nadamar Gaya masa da tayi domin cikin kankanin lokaci jikinsa ya Kara rikicewa jikinsa ba zai iya daukar damuwar ba.domin a wañnan ranar jinyar Mallam sule ta kare ya koma ga mahaliccinsa cikin tsananin bakincikin kassara rayuwar yarsa da akayi ba tare da hskkinta ba.

Duniya ta tsayama Hajara da mahaifiyarta cak rasuwar Mallam sule ta Kara jefasu acikin matsanancin masifa Mara musaltuwa, Hajara ta dorama kanta nauyin rasuwar mahaifinta domin tana ganin kamar itace silar rasuwarsa, bata taba jin tsanar Alh ba irin na wannan lokacin hakika ya Gama da ita da rayuwarta.

Bayan rasuwar Mallam sule, rayuwa ta Kara zafafa agaresu, Ga uwa  uba kus kus din da jamaar  garin suka Fara na maganar cikin Hajara an rasa ta inda maganar ta Fara fitowa don tuni ta mamaye gari, har zuwa ake ana ganin Hajara da an ganta zasu ce ah gashi nan fa ga alama nan zaman birni ya biya bukata.

 A'irin mutanen marasa kunya da suke zuwa ganinta yau sun fita Kenan , Hajara ta fashe da kuka me karfin gaske ta dubi zainabu wacce jinyarta ta Fara tsanani cikin kuka tace " yanzu Inna haka Zan zauna na haifi abinda ke cikina cikin wannan al'ummmar da basama Dan Adam Uzuri" zainabu ta ce cikin galabaitar ciwo " abinda ya faru da ke Baki da laifi ko daya, amma idan har kikayi yunkurin ganin bayan abinda ke cikin ki ina tabbatar Miki sai kin Zama Mai laifi fiye da Alh , don haka na umarceki da  ki toshe kunnanki ki ji Kiki ji ki gani Ki ki gani Ki taimaki abinda ke cikin ki har ya shigo cikin duniyar da shima zai zo ya fafata da kaddararsa acikinta". Hajara ta zuba tagumi tana rera kuka sosai zainabu tace " ki Zama jaruma domin Bani da wani lokaci daya rage min a duniya Zan tafi na barki ke kadai ba ki da wani mataimaki ba kyama da aibatawa suke zasu Zama abokan rayuwarki".

Hajara ta Kara gigicewa ko kadan bata son taji mahaifiyarta na dorama kanta mutuwa domin dai ita kadai ta rage Mata bata da kowa a duniya .
     Cikin Hajara ya shiga wata na biyar zuwa lokacin kowa ya gamsu da cewa tana da ciki, bata fita ko'ina kyama da tsana ba wacce baa nuna Mata ba duk wadanda suke alakar arziki dasu sun datse zumuncin babu Mai zuwa gidansu duk da matsananciyar jinyar da zainabu ke ciki. A wañnan lokacin sani ya tabbatar ma da Hajara ya fasa aurenta babu Shi babu ita ,ya faffada Mata maganganu masu zafi wadanda harta mutu ba zata taba mantawa dasu ba........
  TO BE CONTINUE

DAGA TASKAR YAR GIDAN IMAM


NIMA MATARSA CE
           NA
HABIBA ABUBAKAR IMAM
  ( YAR GIDAN IMAM )
BABI NA ARBA'IN
           **********

     Maganganun da ya gaya Mata sun fi rabuwarsu ciwo agareta. Tun daga wannan ranar Kuma gaba daya jikin zainabu ya Kara rikicewa, idan so Samu ne ace ta kaita asibiti to amma babu yadda zatayi domin abinda zasu ci ma Basu da Shi balle kudin magani.

Duk inda Zata je ta nemi taimako Hajara ta mutstsuke kunyarta taje amma babu Wanda ya saurareta babu Wanda yake nuna tausayi agareta.
   Mallam kallah ne kawai wani lokacin yakansa matarsa sabira ta zuba abinci ta aika musu dashi,

Tunda Hajara take a rayuwa bata taba shiga kunci da bakinciki irin na wannan lokacin ba , gaba daya ta Zama sai kace wata mara hankali domin kanta bazai iya daukar masifar da take ciki ba .
    Ko ta kofar gidansu ba'a bi saboda tsabar kiyayya.watannin cikin Hajara takwas Allah ya dauki rayuwar zainabu bayan fama da matsananciyar jinya, ta tafi tabar Hajara cikin matsatsin rayuwa da kunci mara musaltuwa, rasuwar mahaifiyarta ya taba ta ya Kara jefata cikin matsanancin hali, haka ta cigaba da Zama agidan ita kadai tunda dai ba yan'uwa gareta ba Dama daga mahaifinta sai mahaifiyarta duk dangi sun kare sai tsirarun da bazasu iya taimakonta da komai ba daya wuce kyama da kiyayya,Hajara takan uni bata ci ba ga damuwa da itace ta Zama cinta da Shanta.

Kwana takwas da rasuwar zainabu Mai gidan hayansu ya bukaci Hajara ta tattara tabar masa gidansa tunda Bana gado bane , ya Kuma San bazata iya biyan bashin kudin hayar da yake bin mahaifinta ba balle harta cigaba da biya, don haka ta fice masa agida. Duk irin magiya da rokon da Hajara tayi baisa yabarta ba, saida ta hada kayan ta agaban idonsa ta fito daga gidan ta rokeshi Akan zata kwashe sauran kayan daga baya domin yanzu ko ta kwashe bata San inda zata kaisu ba.

   Hajara ta rasa inda zata shiga, har zuwa magriba tana kofar gidan azaune kafin ta yanke shawarar zuwa gidan Mallam kallah. Shima din ba wata tarba ta arziki ta samu ba daga sabira shine ma Mallam kallah ya tsaya ya saurari abinda ya faru daganan ya dubeta fuska tamke yace" yanzu Hajara kina ganin rayuwarki zatayi kyau, ace duk bakincikin ki ne ya yi sanadiyyar iyayenki,  yanzu Baki da kowa da zaki kalla amatsayin naki, meye ranar kwadayi da son duniya da babu inda zai kaiki sai halaka , yanzu wa Gari ya waya, ina zaki Kai wannan dimbin hakkin da ke kanki"
   Cikin kuka sosai Hajara tace " nayi maka rantsuwa Bani da laifi Akan duk abinda ke faruwa a rayuwata Allah da iyayena sune shaidata"

Sabira ta zuba Mata harara tace " ke da Allah can, kyace sune shaidar ki mana tunda kinga basa Raye, Shi Kuma Allah sai alahira, kin dai yi asara bakinciki Kuma yanzu kika Fara ganinsa, Kuma yanzu da kika taho nan me kike so muyi Miki, ko don kinga muna tausaya Muku muna Baku abinci to ki sani saboda tausayin zainabu yasa Muke Baku abinci ba' don ke ba".

Cikin kuka Hajara tace ," don Allah Inna ki tausaya min babu inda zanje Bani da kowa ina cikin tashin hankali ga halin  da nake ciki babu inda zanje sai nan".

Sabira ta saki tsaki tace " kanki ake ji Hajara ai bakinciki Baki Fara ganin komai ba, Baki da wajen zuwa to ina Wanda yayi Miki cikin ki neme Shi mana ai sai ya baki wajen Zama".

Irin kukan da takeyi shine ya taba zuciyar Mallam kallah, ya tuna amintar da ke tsakaninsa da Mallam sule.ya dubi sabira yace " rabu da ita, ki bata wancan dakin ta zauna aciki".
  Bai saurari surutan da sabira take yi ba ya tashi ya fita.daker ta nuna Mata dakin ta tashi ta shiga dakin anan Mallam kalla ke aje hatsi. ta Sami gefe ta share ta gyara ta maida Shi wajen kwanciyar ta.

  Zamanta agidan bata Sami sassauci ba, Saima Karin damuwa domin duk da tsohon cikinta, itace take yin aikin komai agidan , babu abinda sabira take yi, sai dai ta wanke Goma ta tsoma biyar tayi hidimarta tayi na yayanta duk da tsohon cikinta har wankin sabira da na yaranta take yi.kuma babu godi bare na gode sai hantara da wulakanci.Amma babu yadda zatayi dole ta yi hakuri tunda bata da yadda ta iya.

A haka cikin wannan matsatsin da kuncin, Allah ya sauki Hajara lafiya a gida bayan ta ci bakar wahala.
   Ta haifi yarta mace, sai dai ko rigar da za'asa Mata babu sai cikin zani aka nannadeta.sai daga bisani ne tausayi yasa Mallam kallah ya fita ya sayo riguna guda biyu.

Zuwa wannan lokacin kuwa mutanen kauyen sai sintirin zuwa gidan Mallam kallah suke yi ganin yar shege kamar yadda suke fade.Tashin hankalin da Hajara ta shiga awannan lokacin bazai misaltu ba.ita kanta sabira haka ta Kara Dora Mata tsana taki taimakonta da komai ko yarta taki tabawa saboda kyama. Hajara da kanta tasama yarta suna Zainab take Mata inkiya da muhseena Wanda shine ya danne asalin sunanta muhseena ya bita.
  Fita ya Kara gagararta, gashi tun bayan haihuwata Mallam kallah ya tabbatar Mata da cewa ba zai iya cigaba da ciyar da ita ba don baisan ranar da Allah zai rabasu ba don haka bazai kashe Kansa ba wajen ciyar da ita dakin Zama dai gashinan tayita Zama . Hakan yasa Hajara ta Kara fadawa mawuyacin hali domin dai ko aikatau ta fita nema babu Wanda yake bata babu Mai son yayi Hulda da ita.ga fitinar aikin sabira da yake neman ya kasheta kullum bata da Hutu.

Haka zalika kullum zuciyarta na raya Mata ta koma jigawa ta cigaba da neman Alh ko Allah zaisa ta dace, amma babu Hali tunda bata da kudin mota ko wani Wanda zai taimaka Mata ya shige Mata gaba, tunda kowa a kauyen Yana ganin ayawon banza ta Samo cikin babu wani bayani da zata yi musu Wanda zasu iya gamsuwa da cewa bata da laifi.hakika Hajara taga rayuwa.

 Tana cikin wannan mawuyacin halin na ràshin madafar dafawa daga ita har jatiriyar yarta wacce Hajara take tausayi fiye da kanta domin tana jiye Mata tare da jinjina yadda zata rayu acikin wannan al' ummar sau tari ita kanta tana rayawa aranta ko dai ta gudu ne tabar garin domin tasan idan ta cigaba da Zama kila itama bakincikin ne zai kasheta.

Watannin yarta biyar muhseena ta kamu da ciwon kyanda, Hajara tayi ta sintirin kaita asibiti amma babu me bi ta kansu tunda bata da kudi babu Kuma me son ya taimaketa hatta Mallam kalla yaki taimaka Mata da komai balle sabira wacce har yanzu bata taba daukar muhseena ba saboda tsana.

Hajara na zaune a wajen asibitin ta rungume muhseena wacce numfashinta ke fita daker, kuka Hajara take kamar wacce ranta zai fita , tana zaunene zaman jiran fitar ran muhseena domin dai tuni ta cire rai da ita.
   Kamar daga sama taji muryar wani bawan Allah Yana tambayarta abinda ke damunta take kuka, Wanda rabon da taji wani tausashenshan lafazi daga wani Dan Adam harta manta arayuwarta, hakanne yasa ta dago da sauri cikin tashin hankali tace " yata ce bata da lafiya amma babu Mai son ya taimakeni Bani da kudin da zanyi hidimar maganinta babu Kuma Mai taimakona".

Tausayi ya bayyana a fuskar mutumin yasa hannu ba tare da yace komai ba ya dauki muhseena a cinyarta yayi cikin asibitin da ita, ba tare daya saurari Hajara ba, ta tashi tabi bayansa da sauri.
  Kai tsaye wajen likitan ya nufa ya Mika masa muhseena yace ya dubata, Hajara na gefe tana rusar kuka har aka Gama duba muhseena aka bata gado, mutumin Shi ya biya komai, Ido kawai Hajara take binsa dashi tana jinjina tausayi da karsmci irin nasa. Tunda wannan ibtila' in ya sameta babu Wanda ya taba nuna tausayi agareta sai wannan bawan Allah.

Da Kansa ya tafi ya samoma Hajara abinda zata ci.sai alokacin cikin zubar hawaye tace " nagode ina rokon Allah yaji kanka fiye da yadda kaji Kaina ya saka maka da mafificin alherinsa". Ya jidadin adduar domin yadda ya zubama Hajara Ido yace " Kada ki damu, ni sunana mallam Umar" ta jinjina Kai tace " ni sunana......?
        Malam  Umar ya amshe ya karasa abinda zata ce " sunanki Hajara yar gidan Mallam sule marigayi ". Cikin dimbin mamaki Hajara ta dubeshi tace " kasan ko ni wacece amma Ka taimakeni" ya zuba Mata Ido sosai yace " nasani, Kuma batun yau nake son na taimake ki ba" tayi masa kuri da Ido tace " saboda mene?" Yace a hankali fuskarsa na bayyanar da abinda bakinsa. Ke son Fadi " saboda ina tausayinki, na dade ina Jin damuwarki a raina, duk da Baki sanni ba ni na sanki, na Kuma San wasu daga cikin matsalolinki ". Hawaye ya Kara zubo Mata gami da Dora kanta ajikin gadon da muhseena ke Kai.

  Mallam Umar yace " ki sassauta ma kanki damuwa Hajara , ki dubi yadda damuwa ta maida ke duk kin tsofe  duk da karancin shekarunki, ki sani kowane rayuwar bawa tana tafiya ne akan kaddarar sa saboda haka kiyi hakuri ko kya samu ki cinye wannan jarabawar" Mallam Umar ya yi ta Mata nasiha Wanda tunda ta rasa iyayenta babu Wanda ya taba yi Mata irinsu.daga bisani ya barsu a asibitin ya tafi gida.

   Tsawon wannan lokacin sai yau Hajara ta ji sassauci Akan damuwarta domin ayau ta Sami Wanda Bai kyamaceta ba , ta samu Wanda ya taimaka Mata a garin da babu me kallonta amatsayin mutum.
   Tun daga wannan ranar Mallam Umar shine ya dauke dawainiyar komai na asibitin muhseena, gami da ci da Shanta, haka zalika kullum Yana tare dasu a asibitin daya dawo aikinsa nan yake yada zango.duk Kuma zuwansa Yana kokarin kwantar da hankalin Hajara tare da bata misalai da dama na rayuwa. Agaskiya haduwa da wannan bawan Allah alheri ne ga Hajara domin har ta Fara kokarin Samun nutsuwa Zama da take kamar mahaukaciya tayi ta tunani kwana biyu ta Sami sauki .

 A kalla saida suka kwashe sati biyu a asibitin kafin a sallamesu, Wanda zuwa lokacin Hajara ta Saba da Mallam Umar Shi kadai ne take kallo amatsayin wani garkuwa agareta duk da bata hada komai da Shi ba, abinda yayi Mata Har abada bazata taba mantawa ba. Har aka sallameta Kuma ta koma gida sabira bata taba zuwa ta dubasu ba, Mallam kallah ma sau daya ya je asibitin.

  Ko bayan komawarta gida, haka Mallam Umar ya cigaba da zuwa Yana duba muhseena Yana kuma Kai musu kayan bukata,Hajara tayima Allah godiya daya kawo Mallam Umar cikin rayuwarta alokacin da kowa ya juya bayansa a gareta.Wani zuwa da yayi ne a kofar gida ya dubi Hajara cikin nutsuwa yace " Hajara ina son naji gaskiyar yadda akayi kika Sami muhseena Bana son kiyi min karya , domin duk abinda mutanen gari suke fada akanki ban taba kamawa ba ba Kuma Zan yarda ba sai abinda ya fito daga bakinki na gaskiya". Hajara ta numfasa idanunta suka cika da hawaye domin bata taba Samun Wanda yayi Mata irin wannan tambaya ta tunani ba, kowa kawai ya yankema Kansa hukunci ne. Ya dubeta ya girgiza Kai yace " Bana son kuka, ina son ki natsu muyi magana ta gaskiya". Hajara tace " A'irin taimakon da Ka min arayuwata Bana Jin Zan iya yi maka karya ko da Don jindadi na ne saboda haka Zan gaya Maka gaskiyar komai babu abinda Zan boye maka". Sannu a hankali Hajara ta labartama malllam Umar komai.ta karasa tana goge hawayenta 

Mallam Umar ya cire tagumin da ya zuba, ahankali yayi dabarar share siraran hawayen dake fita a idanunsa,. Yayi shuru na wasu dakika sannan ya numfasa yace " na yarda da duk abinda kika fada domin Naga gaskiyar a cikin kwayar idanunki" ya Kara numfasawa yace " Allah yayi Miki sakayya Hajara , Allah Kuma ya raya muhseena yayi Mata albarka'.
 Hajara tace " Amin , nagode nagode da irin taimakonka agareni" ya girgiza Kai yace Kada ki damu, zaki gane gidan Alh yanzu idan Muka je jigawa" tayi saurin kallonsa tace " Zan gane" yace " to ina son ki shirya gobe zamu tafi jigawa, ki sanar da mutan gidan ". Hajara ta zuba masa Ido tana cike da mamaki tace " jigawa?" Yace " eh, zamu je mu neme Shi wata kila ko sun dawo daga inda suka tafi". Hajara ta rasa bakin godiya àgare Shi.domin harya tafi godiya take masa da fatan alheri ta Kara ganin girma da kimarsa fiye da kullum, ta ji aranta Allah ne ya kawo Mata Mallam Umar domin ya Zama sanyi acikin rayuwarta.
   TO BE CONTINUE

DAGA TASKAR YAR GIDAN IMAM


NIMA MATARSA CE
                NA
 HABIBA ABUBAKAR IMAM
  ( YAR GIDAN IMAM )
BABI NA ARBA'IN DA DAYA
          *********

  Tun daren ranar ta sanar da Mallam kallah da sabira batun zuwanta jigawa ita da Mallam Umar.

Mallam kallah yayi Mata kallon tsaf yace " Kai Shi Ko Mallam kallah da shegiyar tattaga ya ke , da tsabar iyayi, meye nasa na daukarki kuje jigawa, Kenan Shi yama yarda da karyar taki" 
  Sabira ta tabe baki tace " iyayi ko kinibibi, su suka sani, yin hakan dai bashi zai kankare Miki zargi da bakin fentin da ake ganinki da shi ba".

Kai kawai Hajara ta jinjina ba tare da tace komai ba, domin dai ta gaji da yi musu bayanin gsskiyarta tunda ba zasu taba yarda da ita ba, to tana ganin yi musu bayanin kullum baida wani amfani.

Maganganun da suka cigaba da yi ne marasa dadi yasa Hajara ta tashi ta shige daki.haka ta kwana cikin godiya ga Allah daya hadata da Mallam Umar domin ayanzu dai damuwa ta Fara yi Mata sauki.

Washegari da wuri Mallam Umar ya zo , sai da ya Samu kebewa da Mallam kallah yayi masa bayanin dalilin zuwansu jigawa.mallam kallah ya tamke fuska yace " zadai Ka wahalar da kanka kawai , domin duk abinda Hajara ta gaya maka karya ne , bansan dai dalilin da yasa kake son sa kanka a wahala ba" ya girgiza Kai yace " Banga karya acikin idon Hajara ba, Kuma taimako bsi taba Zama wahala ba, ni na ji ina son na taimaketa don haka babu wata damuwa in sha Allah".

Fahimtar da Mallam kallah yayi na cewar Mallam Umar bazai taba sautarensa ba yasa yayi shuru , daga bisani yayi musu fatan dawowa lafiya.
   Har Hajara da malllam Umar suka Kai jigawa muhseena tana hannunsa.Babu abinda ya bace ma Hajara domin har kofar gidan Alh ta Kai Mallam Umar .

Sai dai wannan Karon wani maigadin suka tadda ba Wanda suka taba gani ba.mallam Umar ya tsaya yayi masa bayani sosai na mutumin da suke nema.
   Maigadin yayi Jim alamar tunani daga bisani yace " gaskiya bansan wani mutum haka ba, domin bashi bane agidan, ni Kaina ban wuce watanni uku da Fara aiki ba, Kuma gadin kawai nake yi na gida domin har yanzu babu Wanda ya tare agidan". Idanun Hajara suka cika da kwalla ta Kara sa hannu ta rungume yarta.
    Mallam Umar ya numfasa cike da damuwa, baisan dalilin da yasa tun daga ranar da Hajara ta bashi Labarinta yaji Yana so ya taimaketa ba , tausayin ta Kuma da tausayin yarta suka tsaya masa arai , yadda ko me yake yi baya rabo da tunaninsu.

Ya dubi Hajara yace " Bana son ki cigaba da sa ma kanki damuwa, domin babu maganin da hakan zai yi Miki".
 Ya Kara numfasawa yace " bazai yuwu mu ce zamu cigaba da nemansa a babban gari irin wannan ba, domin abinda kamar wuya,Amma ina son ki sani  Allah baya zaluunci ya Kuma Sanya Shi abin haramtawa a gare mu, don haka ki sama ranki Allah zaiyi Miki sakayya ko a dade ko ajima ".

Cikin hawaye tace " yanzu shikenan haka yata zata rayu bata San ubanta ba ?" Ya dubeta sosai yace " ko kin ganshi yanzu , kina tsammanin cikin ruwan sanyi zai karbi muhseena amatsayin yarsa ( ya girgiza Kai) bazai taba amsarta cikin dadin rai ba, Kuma a matsayin sa na me kudi Bana Jin. Zamu iya yaki dashi tunda ayanzu an maida kudi sune komai,". Ya gyara tsayuwarsa Yana cigaba da kallonta yace " KO da nace mu zo, mun zo ne kawai saboda ko da mun ganshi ya San Yana da ya da ke ko ya amince ko Bai amince ba ya dai sani ". Gaba daya Hajara ta rushe da kuka me karfi,  tana fadin " ya zanyi da rayuwata, Bana Jin Zan cigaba da rayuwa a Durmi , kawai Ka tafi Ka barni anan domin idan na cigaba da Zama a can bazan taba rabuwa da tashin hankali da damuwa ba, mutanen garin Kuma ba zasu taba yi min Uzuri ba haka Zan zauna yata ta taso su cigaba da jifanta da mummunar Kalmar shegiya, karshe su jefa Mata tsanata kamar yadda suka jefama kansu".

Tausayin ta ya Kara kama Shi ya yi kasa da Kansa ya na nazarin maganar da yake son ya fada Mata.

Zuwa Dan wani lokaci ya dago ya zuba Mata Ido cike da wani yanayi da yake Jin bazai iya Hana Kansa faden abinda ke cikin ransa ba yau.
      "Hajara" ya Kira sunanta a tausashe, ta amsa ahankali ba tare data dube Shi ba.yace " ina son ki kwantar da hankalinki, ni Umar Zan amshi yarki muhseena amatsayin yata Zan riketa Zan bata sunana a matsayin uba Zan kare Mata martaba da mutunci amatsayin ta na ya ta, daga karshe Zan aureki domin Zama Uwa da uba ga Hajara ba tare da mun rarraba kanmu ba".

Tikisa! Hajara jin maganar tayi kamar mafarki, tayi gaggawar kallonsa shima ita yake kallo, suka hada Ido gaba dayansu wani abu ya tsurga musu . Tace tana yi masa kallon mamaki " kasan abinda kake fada kuwa, Mallam yanzu zaka aure ni, Kuma Kaba yata sunanka a matsayin uba?" Ya gyada Kai yace " in sha Allah, domin ina ganin wannan jihadin da zanyi shine dalilin da Allah ya hanani haihuwa" tace da sauri " ya hanaka haihuwa?" Ya gyada Kai yace " eh, Hajara aurena uku duk babu wacce ta iya hakurin Zama da ni , saboda lalurata ta tashin haihuwa domin likitoci sun tabbatar min da cewa bazan taba haihuwa ba, dalilin da yasa kenan Matan da na aura basa Zama, hakanne yasa na yanke shawarar na hakura da aure bazan Kara yi ba tunda babu wata mace da zata iya Zama da ni".

Ya aje numfashi ahankali ya cigaba da cewa " ke kanki bazan iya aurenki ba sai idan har kin amince, idan Baki amince ba babu komai duk da haka ina nan a matsayin mahaifin muhseena ". Zuciyar Hajara ta yi rauni , tausayin Mallam Umar ya kamata nan da nan taji ta aminta dashi za Kuma ta iya hakuri da ràshin haihuwar ko don ta saka Masa da irin halacci da karancin da yayi Mata arayuwa, babu abinda ta hada dashi Amma ya taimaketa ya maida damuwarta damuwarsa karshe ma ya dauki ragamar rayuwar yarta ya danganta ta da Kansa zai Zama ubanta.ita ko ta yaya zata manta dukkan wannan halaccin tace Ba zata aure shi ba saboda kawai baya haihuwa.

Mallam Umar yace " Kada ki takura kanki, idan ba zaki aure ni ba , babu komai bazan dauki hakan a matsayin wani abu ba" . Ta girgiza Kai tace " Ashirye nake na aure Ka, na rantse maka kana da babban matsayi a gareni , ba Kuma Zan taba manta halaccinka  àgare NI ba, duk kyama da mugayen maganganun da ake jifana dasu Bai sa maka shakku akaina ba, Kaine mutum na farko da Ka yarda da ni bayan mahaifana, saboda haka na yarda Zan aure Ka".

Mallam Umar yayi Dan murmushi yace " To Allah ya tabbatar mana da alheri, maganar Kuma muhseena bata da uba wannan ya Zama tsohon zance ni Umar nine mahaifinta".
   Karo na farko kenan da Hajara tayi murmushi sosai, tace " Nagode".
Daga bisani suka tattara suka dawo Durmi, da Kaya niki Niki Wanda Mallam Umar ya tsaya ya sayoma muhseena Duka kayan Sawa ne, Wanda tunda aka haifeta babu Wanda ya taba bata Kaya irin haka harda takalmi. Hajara ta rasa bakin godiya sai murmushi take Wanda Shi Kansa Mallam Umar tun haduwarsu Bai taba ganin murmushinta ba. Bayan komawarsu Yana Kai Hajara gida ya wuce gidansa saboda ya gaji.

Sosai sabira ta nuna hassadarta da ganin sayayya da Mallam Umar yayi musu. Don ko dubawa batayi ba.

Mallam Umar ya toshe kunnansa daga surutan mutanen kauyen, Wanda tunda maganar zai auri Hajara ta fito ake tayi, kullum baya Rasa mutanen da zasuyi sallama dashi suna bashi shawarar Kada ya sake ya auri Hajara me zaiyi da ita.Amma ya toshe kunnansa ya ki sauraran kowa.

Hatta Yan'uwansa sai da suka zo suka same Shi Akan Basu yarda da aurensa da Hajara ba. Nan ma ya nuna musu ya Riga ya zartar da hukunci Kuma bazai tashi daga Kan alkawarinsa ba . Hakan shine dalilin da suka rabu baram baram.
   A Daren ranar ne Kuma mahaifiyarsa Ande Dije ta Kira Shi suka zauna ta dube Shi sosai tace " ni dai dalilin da Ka gaya min na auren yarinyar nan Bai kwanta min ba. Allah ya sani Bana son yarinyar nan Bana son hada komai da ita, ". Malllam Umar yace " Ande , kinfi kowa sanin matsalolin da nake samu da duk matan da nake aure, asalima kinfi kowa sanin babu wata mace a kauyen nan da take son ta aure ni saboda lalurata, shine dalilin da yasa na ce bazan Kara yin aure ba,"

Ya Kara kallonta yace " ita kuwa duk da tasan matsalata ta amince ta aure ni" Ande Dije tayi tsaki tace " to ba dole ta yarda ba tunda tasan babu Wanda zai iya aurenta a kauyen nan" yace " duk da haka dai kamar yadda kike ganin nayi Mata halacci haka itama tayi min halacci ta hanyar yarda ta aure ni".

Ande Dije tace " ni dai Bana Sonta Bana son auren nan Ka hakura kawai". Cikin damuwa Mallam Umar yace " shikenan naji Ande ,Zan fasa aurenta amma Kuma ni bazan Kara yin aure ba har kasa ta nade idan kin amince da hakan shikenan ".
   Ande Dije tayi masa kuri da Ido , sannan tace "Ta Yaya zaka ce ba zaka Kara yin aure ba , bayan Auren shine cikar kamalarka, a irin shekarunka ta yaya zaka ce Kai da aure har abada", shima ya dubeta yace " saboda babu wacce zata yarda ta zauna da Wanda ba zata iya haihuwa dashi ba, ni Kuma babu wacce Zan Kara rufema sirrina na aureta bata San gaskiyar komai ba, na Kuma San babu wacce zata yarda ta aure ni matukar tasan Bana haihuwa ". Ande Dije tayi shuru alamun nazari, hakan yasa Mallam Umar ya cigaba da cewa " kinga sai yadda ta kwashe da aurena na karshe irin bakar tijarar da Ramatu tayi mana , da irin fallashe sirrina da tayi cikin mutane,Amma ita Hajara ta Sani duk da haka Kuma ta amince zata aure ni, to me zai hana mu karbi Hajara ga ta marainiya bata da kowa , ".

Ya Kara dubanta yace " yarinyar nan abar tausayi ce, kuma yadda mutanen gari suka dauki lamarinta ba gaskiya ba ne ....?.

Ta daga masa " Kada kayi wani kokarin kareta, dominba Zan taba gamsuwa ba , kamar yadda bazan taba Sonta ba, amma duk da haka ba ni da wani zabi da ya wuce na yarda Ka aureta, sai dai nasan surutan mutane ma kadai ya ishe mu ,". Cikin matukar Jin dadin amincewarta yace " babu ruwan mu da surutan mutane , tunda ba mutane bane zasu tsara mana yadda zamu tafiyar da rayuwar mu, duk Kuma Wanda ya daka ta mutane to babu abinda zai yi".

Ta tabe Baki tace " Kai Kuma taka kaddarar ta aure a haka ta zo maka,  Allah ya kyauta ya Baka ikon cinye jarabawar".

Tunda Mallam Umar ya Samu ya shawo Kan mahaifiyarsa, shikenan ya Kara fita batun duk surutan da mutane suke yi. Ko da zai tura neman aurenta duk yan' uwansa kin zuwa sukayi, sai liman ne ya je ya Nemo masa auren Hajara a wajen Mallam kallah.

Shi Kansa Mallam kallah ya rasa wace hau ce ta hau Mallam Umar da zai auri Hajara. Da shekarunsa da komai ya manyanta balle ace yarinta ce ke damunsa. To koma dai menene ya ji dadin hakan tunda lokacin da Hajara zata bar masa gidansa ya zo. Itama sabira ta ji dadin hakan domin dai gara Hajara ta bar Mata gida Dama batun yau ba ta gaji da Zama da ita......
  TO BE CONTINUE

DAGA TASKAR YAR GIDAN IMAM


NI MA MATARSA CE
               NA
HABIBA ABUBAKAR IMAM
  ( YAR GIDAN IMAM )
BABI NA ARBA'IN DA BIYU
                *********
   Wata daya kawai aka sa bikin Hajara . Haka zalika Mallam Umar Shi yayi Mata komai na daki dai dai karfina, hakan kuwa ba karamin karama Hajara kaunarsa yayi ba, ta yarda duk duniya bata da gata sama da Mallam Umar.

Mallam Umar a wañnan lokacin Yana da rufin asiri tunda Yana Dan bubbuga kasuwancinsa. Abubuwa da yawa basa masa wahalar yi.

Hakan yasa bai sami wata damuwa ba wajen tafiyar da komai na bikinsa da Hajara ba. Hatta daurin auren babu Wanda ya halarta sai tsirarun mutane da mafi yawanci ma Basu da alaka da Mallam Umar.  Liman ne ya daura auren .

Daker sabira ta bi umarnin Mallam kallah ta raka Hajara gidan aurenta, tana kaita Kuma ta bar gidan. Awannan ranar Hajara tayi kuka kamar idonta zai fita taga ràshin gata yadda akayi bikinta ko bikin yar tsana ce sai haka, rasuwar mahaifanta ta dawo Mata sabuwa, tayi rashinsu matuka da gaske. 

Hajara ta dauki dammarar yi ma Mallam Umar dukkan biyayya tare da mutunta Shi, domin Shi kadai gareta yanzu a duniya. Hakanne yasa duk musgunawa da Ande Dije ke yi Mata ita da yarta bai taba damunta ba. Domin dai tun zuwanta gidan Ande Dije ta maida ta tamkar baiwa komai itace take yi agidan babu Kuma yabawa ko darajawa. Ande Dije mulki take Kuma dole Hajara ta dinga bin duk abinda take so. Idan ma akwai abinda ke damun Hajara to bai wuce kiyayyar da Ande Dije ta dorama muhseena ba domin ko kusa da ita bata son ta je, bata daukarta bata kulata tsakaninsu sai kyara da mugayen maganganun da Hajara bata kaunar ta dinga yinsu Akan yarta da bata da laifin komai.

Duk da haka Mallam Umar Yana cike da damuwar abinda Ande ke yi ma su Hajara a kullum Yana kokarin fahimtar da ita ta dauki Hajara a matsayin ya muhseena Kuma a matsayin jika Amma taki. Bashi da yadda zaiyi tunda Uwa ce dole tasa sai dai ya dawo wajen Hajara yayi ta bata hakuri tare da nuna Mata ta dinga Kau da kanta akan komai wata Rana sai labari.
    Hajara ta kanyi murmushi tace " Ka daina damun kanka domin ba ni da damuwa, na dauki Ande a matsayin Uwa ta don haka babu abinda zata min na ji haushinta, ai Nama gode data yarda Ka aure ni. Irin wadannan kalaman nata Suna karama Mallam Umar kaunarta da ganin mutuncinta. Ya dauki dukkan hidimar muhseena komai Yana yi Mata ga wani irin so da yake Mata har cikin jininsa Yana Jin tamkar Shi ya haifi muhseena bai wasa da duk abinda ya shafeta, duk da kullum yayi hidimar sai Ande ta ci masa mutunci amma baisa ya daina yin abinda yake yi ba, domin ya riga ya daukar ma Kansa alkawarin Kula da muhseena a matsayin ya.

Cikin ikon Allah a kwana a tashi shekarar muhseena hudu, a lokacin Mallam Umar ya hadu da ibtila'in karayar arziki duk kasuwancinsa yayi kasa a hankali a hankali kamar wasa komai ya kakkabe ya zamana bai da komai. Alokacin komai ya Fara musu wahalar Samu tun daga Kan abinci har sauran abubuwan more rayuwa komai ya tsaya cak!.
   To fa nan Ande ta Kara daukar kiyayya ta dorama Hajara domin acewarta Hajara ce mai farar kafa don Mallam Umar ya aureta ne ya tsiyace, ai daman tasan ba zata taba Zama alheri ga danta ba, ko tsakar gida daker Hajara ke fitowa saboda tsabar masifar Ande Dije, duk yadda Mallam Umar ya so ya fahimtar da ita komai da ga Allah ya ke babu hannun Hajara acikin karayar arzikinsa to fa Ande taki fahimta ko Kuma ta fahimta amma kiyayyar Hajara ta hanata ganin hakan.

Tun daga wannan lokacin har zuwa yanzu Mallam Umar cikin fafutukar rufama Hajara da muhseena asiri yake. Satittika sun shige cikin watanni , watanni sun shige cikin shekaru har zuwa girman muhseena tamkar zaa manta da labarin Hajara amma ina tunda muhseena ta Girma itama ake jifanta da Kalmar shegiya. Da Kan Shi Mallam Umar ya sa Hajara tayi masa alkawarin ba zata taba gaya ma muhseena cewa ba Shi ba ne mahaifinta ba.

Haka zalika iya wadannan shekarun har yanzu Hajara bata Kara Jin duriyar Alh ba balle Haj sun bace bat, babu su babu labarinsu.

          **********
   Hajara ta numfasa gami da goge hawayen da ya dade Yana zuba Mata , ta dago ta dubi Kabir da muhseena gaba dayansu kuka suke sosai.
    Ta jinjina Kai tace " wannan Hajarar ba wata ba ce illa ni, muhseena Kuma ba wata ba ce illa ke" ta nuna muhseena da hannunta.

Muhseena ta Kara fashewa da kuka Mai karfi , wani abu ne ya ke yi Mata yawo a cikin zuciya , kamar yadda ta ke Jin tamkar jinin jikinta Yana tafasa,kanta ya cigaba da Sarawa sosai.
Hajara ta dubi Kabir da yake kokarin tattara natsuwarsa waje daya tace " Kabir Ka ji gaskiyar magana akan muhseena, haka zalika ina Mai Baka shawarar Ka hakura da muhseena domin bana fatan muhseena ta shiga rayuwar kunci da takura a gidan aurenta, don ina son muhseena tayi aure a gidan da zaa karbeta da daraja ba tare da kallon ita wacece ba".

Ta Dan gyara zamanta tace " Na so ace mahaifanka sun yarda da muhseena, amma tunda Basu aminta da ita ba dole za kayi hakuri da ita duk da kafi kowa cancantar Ka aureta".

   Kabir ya numfasa yace " Inna na gamsu da labarinki na Kuma yarda da duk abinda kika fada, na Kara Jin son muhseena da tausayinta a raina fiye da kullum, amma ba ni da yadda zanyi Bani da karfin hali kamar Mallam Umar ta yadda Zan bata da kowa a Kan muhseena kamar yadda ya bata da kowa a kanki, amma Allah ya sani  son muhseena ina kaunarta rashinta Kuma agare ni ba karamar masifa ba ce, amma babu yadda na iya sai dai Zan cigaba da roko da bin mahaifana har su amince".
Hajara ta girgiza Kai tace ," ba zasu yarda ba Kada Ka bata ma kanka lokaci, kayi kokarin cire ta kawai a ranka, Kuma ba Zan taba mantawa da dawainiyar da kayi da mu ba Ina fatan Allah ya baka wacce ta fi muhseena ".

Tana Gama fadin haka tayi saurin tashi ta fita daga dakin.
  Kabir ya zubama muhseena Ido wacce bakinta ya rufe ta kasa cewa komai ita kadai tasan halin da take ciki.

Ya Kira sunanta a hankali ta amsa ba tare data kalle Shi ba.
Yace " kiyi hakuri kowa da irin kaddararsa a rayuwa don haka kema ki dauka wannan itace kaddararki, Kuma ki sani dukkan tsanani Yana tare da sauki babu Kuma wani abu da yake dauwama, hakuri Kuma shine matakin nasarar ko wane bawa".
  Haka Kabir ya cigaba da yi Mata nasihohi ,Amma har yayi ya gama muhseena ta kasa cewa uffan tana zaune ne dai kamar gunki.

Fahimtar da yayi muhseena ba ta tare da Shi , yasa yaga kysutuwar ya bata fili ko ta Samu ta fito daga tashin hankalin data fada. Asanyaye cikin yanayi na damuwa ya fito daga dakin , zaune Kan tabarma ya tadda Hajara ya fito da kudi daga aljihunsa ya tsuguna a gabanta ya aje kusa da ita ya Kara da cewa " gobe Zan koma kano, sai ki cigaba da yi mana adduar Allah ya daidaita yasa mahaifana su amince da muhseena" ta Dan yi wani bushasshen murmushi Wanda kasan sa damuwa da tashin hankali ne kwance. Tace " Nagode Kabir, Allah ya kaika lafiya, ya Kuma zaba maka abinda ya fi alheri". Ya amsa da " Amin" sannan ya Kara Mata sallama ya tafi.

Wani abin mamaki Yana fita ya tadda Mallam Umar zaune Kan dankalin kofar gidan yanayin zamansa da alama ya dade zaune a wajen.
   Mallam Umar ya waiwayo ya dube Shi yace " Kabiru kun gama?", Kabir yayi saurin risinawa ya gaishe shi. Mallam Umar ya cigaba da dubansa fuskarsa cike da damuwa yace " Tun alokacin da Hajara take baku labari na shigo , Bana son na ji komai ne yasa na fito na cigaba da Zama a waje" ya Kara kallonsa yace " duk arayuwata babu abinda na tsana sama da wani ya ce ba ni ne mahaifin muhseena ba, saboda ita din ya ta ce babu Kuma abinda zai kankare hakan saboda haka kaima ina son Ka cigaba da dauka a haka, lokuta da yawa ina Jin ràshin jindadi Akan ràshin goyon bayan da mahaifiyata bata ba ni ba na barin garin nan bayan aure na da Hajara saboda ta haka ne kadai ba zaa bijiro da irin wannan maganar agaban muhseena ba, mu je can mu rayu inda ba'a San mu ba, amma mahaifiyata ta ki yarda da hakan".

Kabir ya numfasa yace " A yau na Kara ganin kima da darajarka fiye da ko yaushe, Allah yayi maka sakayya da alheri da irin hidimar da kayi" ya gyara tsugunonsa yace " Na so a ce ina da irin jarumtarka da babu Wanda zai hana ni auren muhseena" malllam Umar ya ce " ko yanzu ina son kasama ranka duk abinda Allah yayi dai dai ne, Kuma babu jayayya tsakaninka da mahaifanka, wuce Ka tafi dare yayi".
   Kabir ya Mike Yana gaya masa gobe zai koma Kano, nan ma yayi masa fatan alheri su kayi sallama ya tafi zuciyarsa cike da tausayin muhseena da Hajara mara misaltuwa.

Har ya koma gida bai Sami wata nutsuwa ba, ya so Kara yin maganar da mahaifiyarsa amma ganin yadda bata bashi fuska ba yasa ya hakura. Washegari kuwa tun safe ya shirya zai tafi.

Mallam Hamza wanzami ya zuba masa Ido Yana hango tsantsar damuwa da ràshin nutsuwar atare da Shi, ya numfasa yace " Kabiru Kada Ka taba dauka damuwarka ba damuwar mu ba ce, Allah ya sani da Zan iya hakura na taushi zuciyata Ka auri yarinyar nan da nayi amma maganar gaskiya bazan iya bata zuriata ba da abin da nasan bazai taba gogewa ba, don haka kayi hakuri ga Mata nan da yawa sai wacce Ka zaba kaje Allah ya zaba maka mafi alheri agareka". Kabir ya bude bski zai yi magana yayi saurin ce masa " Bana son wata magana data shafi wannan yarinyar Ka rufeta Ka manta da ita Ka je kayi harkar gsbanka domin Bana Jin a kwai wata magana da zaka Gaya min ko kasa a gaya min wacce zata sani na amince da bukatarka".
   Ya Kara yi masa kallon tsaf yace " matukar ni na haifeka na datse dukkan wata alaka da ke tsakaninka da wannan yarinyar Bana sin Ka Kara min maganarta kamar yadda Bana son Ka Kara zuwa yanzu in da ta ke". Kabir bai fita da ga cikin tashin hankalin da ya Kara fadawa ba , daga can bakin Kofa muryar Hafsatu ta Kara dakan dodon kunnansa tace "Nima a matsayina na mahaifiyarka na haramtaka cigaba da Hulda da muhseena da gidansu domin babu abinda tarihi ya manta don haka Bana son Ka shiga mummunan tarihin wannan bakar zuri'ar, Ka je Ka Nemo yarinya yar asali yar mutunci". Cikin nauyin zuciya da fitar kwalla kamar ba namiji ba Kabir yace " Na so ace kun fahimce ni amma babu yadda zanyi don bazan iya tsallake duk sharadin da kuka gindaya min ba, sai dai Ku sani ba ni da halin cire muhseena a cikin zuciya ta domin wannan ba yi na ba ne yin Allah ne, amma Zan nisanceta kamar yadda kuka min umarni ". Komai kekashewar zuciyarka sai Ka ji tausayin Kabir yadda yayi wujuga wujuga da yadda yanayinsa ya kasance. Yana Gama yi musu wannan bayanin ya Ciro kudi masu yawa ya aje musu ya juya yayi musu sallama ya tafi, cikin matukar tashin hankali, ya so ya Kara ganin muhseena kafin ya bar garin to amma babu halin hakan domin Shi Kansa yasan cigaba da kusantar muhseena zalincine a gareta domin idan ya cigaba da kusantarta yasan babu yadda zaayi ta fita daga cikin kuncin rashinsa ko mantawa da Shi , don haka ya Zama tilas ya nisanceta . Fitar sa Hafsatu ta numfasa ta dubi Mallam Hamza tace "Mallam , yaron nan yayi nisa Ina Jin tausayinsa matuka araina, don haka ina ganin dole a hada da Nemo masa rubutun dangana ko Allah yasa ya yi dangana ya manta da wannan bakar dagar yarinyar da Bana ko son tunawa da ita". Mallam Hamza yace " Ni Kaina nayi tunanin hakan , bazan yarda yarinyar nan ta sabauta min rayuwar da ba babu gaira babu dalili, Dan da nake mora fiye da kowa, don haka ina fita Zan Sami Mallam zangija nayi masa bayani idan rubutun danganar zai masa duk yayi kawai," haka suka zauna suna cigaba da tattaunawa a tsakar gidan.

Kishiyar Hafsatu sahura ta fito daga dakinta da alama duk abinda suke tattaunawa tana jinsu, ta dube su tace " Ni ko Mallam banga illar goyama Kabiru baya ya auri yarinyar nan ba, tunda Yana sonta ina ganin tamkar an shiga rayuwarsa idan aka raba Shi da ita", Hafsatu tayi Mata wani irin kallo cikin fusata tace " Allah sarki, ai da yake ba ke kika haife Shi ba , da ace Hassan ne ai da Baki Fadi haka ba, sai kace ba ki San yarinyar da ake magana akai ba", sahura ta jinjina Kai tace " Allah ya Baki hakuri bana nufin abinda kika fada, da Kabiru Kuma da Hassan Duka yayana ne babu wani bare acikinsu, kawai dai ina gaya muku gaskiya ne" Mallam Umar yayi Mata wani irin kallo yace " To mun ji gaskiyar mun gode Kabiru Kuma ba zai aure ta ba komai runtsi komai wuya". Fahimtar da tayi maganar tana so ta dawo wata iri yasa ta wuce kiciñ wajen girkinta ba tare da ta Kara yin magana ba.

    Tabangaren muhseena kuwa, sosai ta Kara sa ma kanta hakuri, domin labarin mahaifiyarta yayi sanadiyyar data ji karfin guiwa kwarai da gaske, 
  Domin tasan cewa ko rabin kwatan halin kunci da tashin hankalin da mahaifiyarta ta shiga ita bata shige shi ba, ta Kara tausayama mahaifiyarta kwarai babu Kuma abinda wani zai Kara fada Akan mahaifiyarta da zai dameta. Kamar yadda son ta da son kyautata Mata ya Kara cika zuciyarta, Karo da dama Kenan da ta ji tana son Zama wata a rayuwa saboda ta jiyar da mahaifiyarta dadin da ko wace uwa take mafarki daga danta.A wannan lokacin ta Kara Shan alwashin sai ta Zama abar kwatance na alheri a kauyen Durmi Saita binne tarihin mahaifiyarta da kyakkyawan misali.

Kamar yadda ta gefe daya kima da kaunar Mallam Umar ya Kara kama ta , shine mahaifinta bata da wani uba sama da Shi , babu Wanda zai amsa sunan mahaifinta bayan Shi. Za Kuma tayi komai domin ganin ta Zama ya ta gari a gare Shi zata Zama sanyin idaniyarsa.
  TO BE CONTINUE

DAGA TASKAR YAR GIDAN IMAM


NI MA MATARSA CE
           NA 
HABIBA ABUBAKAR IMAM
   ( YAR GIDAN IMAM )
BABI NA ARBA'IN DA UKU
              **********
 kwarai matuka idan muhseena tace ràshin Kabir bai taba rayuwarta ba, hakika tayi karya sosai take Jin rashinsa , sai dai dole tasan zata sa ma kanta hakuri da dangana  ta yarda da kaddararta.
  Haka zalika ta jidadin hukuncin da Kabir ya dauka na nisantarta, kamar yadda ya gayá Mata a waya, bayan komawarsa, sai dai duk da haka akai akai abokinsa ya Kan kawo Mata sakon kudi ko kayan bukata, bata son Kabir ya cigaba da hidima da ita amatsayin tausayi don haka ta sanar da Shi bata bukatar komai ya dakatar da hidimar da yake yi, Wanda su kansu su Mallam Umar din sunga dacewar hakan.
  Ko ya aiko da kudi ta daina Karba, gaba daya so ta ke ta manta da Shi duk da kullum zuciyarta cikin damuwa da ràshin nutsuwa ta ke, sai dai tana kokarin boyewa saboda bata bukatar aikata duk wani abu da zai zamo karin damuwa ga mahaifanta.
   
               ***********
   Tabangaren mujaheeda da Taufiq kuwa Zama dai ya yi dadi, abubuwa da dama sun canja tunda itama mujaheedan ta canja sosai son Taufiq ya rinjayi izza da son ranta. Tana kokarin bin duk abinda yake so.

Sai dai har yanzu ragamar kulawa da Shi ta na nan ahannun masu aiki, har yanzu bata San ta girka abinci don mijinta ya ci ba, ràshin yi Mata magana ko Kuma nuna baya jindadin hakan yasa har yanzu mujaheeda bata fahimci tana aikata kuskure ba. Ita kuwa Haj murjanatu shurun da ta ji na zaman lafiyar da Taufiq da mujaheeda su ke yi tayi zaton aikin Mallam ne ya ci kamar yadda ta ke zaton aikin Mallam dinne ya mallaka Mata nata Mijin.

Haka zalika ta wajen aikin mujaheeda duk wani tuggu da kulle kulle Alh jibrin yayi domin ya ga aikin ya fita ran mujaheeda, amma babu wani cigaba da yake samu hatta murtala da ya ke Goya masa baya ya fahimci babu wata nasara da zasu samu, hakanne yasa ya zage ya koma wajen mujaheeda Yana cigaba da biyayya da neman fada. Shima Àlh jibrin din hakanan ya hakura Yana cigaba da adduar Allah ya kawo sanadiyyar da mujaheeda zata bar aikin da kanta. 

      A cikin haka ne mujaheeda ta Fara laulayi , gwajin farko likita ya tabbatar Mata da tana dauke da ciki harna wata biyu.suna hanyar dawowa daga adibitinne murna da farinciki gun Taufiq ba'a magana, sabanin mujaheeda da gaba daya fuskarta da yanayin ta ya canja tunda likita yace tana da ciki babu wani alamun farinciki a fuskarta, har suka Kai gida duk irin maganganun farinciki da Taufiq ya ke yi baisa tace uffan ba. Isar su gida ne hakurin Taufiq ya gaza ya dubeta sosai yace " Ni banga alamun farinciki ba atare da ke, duk da irin wannan gagarumar kyautar da Allah ya bamu". Mujaheeda ta dan muskuta ta gyara kwanciyar ta akan gado fuska ba yabo ba fallasa tace " hmm ni fa yaya Taufiq ban shirya haihuwa yanzu ba, asalima ni ban taba kawowa araina wai Zan haihu nan ta kusa ba" ya kafe ta da kallon mamaki sannan yace " Dama haihuwar shirya Mata ake ? Ban fahimce ki ba fa" tayi masa kuri da Ido tace " Shi yasa nake son Ka fahimce ni yanzu" ta yunkura cikin ràshin karfin jiki ta zauna tana kallonsa tace " ina son kayi hakuri a cire cikin nan, domin bazan iya ba , Ka duba Kaga ni yadda na fita hayyacina Bana jindadin komai ga ciwon da ban ma gane Kansa ba, wannan cikin zai tsayar min da abubuwa da dama a rayuwata, don haka don Allah Ka barni na cire Shi ".

Tsananin mamaki da bakincikin maganganunta suka baibaye Taufiq, ya dubeta sosai Ido cikin Ido yace " mujaheeda anya kuwa kina so na?", Ta dube Shi da sauri tace " Ni ko na ke son Ka , wannan wace irin magana ce yaya Taufiq?". Ya girgiza Kai yace " idan har da irin wannan tunanin a ranki to ki Sani ni bakya so na, duk abinda kika fada na game da son da kike min karya ne, don idan kina Sona da Baki kyamaci hada jini da ni ba", tayi saurin cewa " Ya zaayi na kyamaci hada jini da Kai bayan na riga na hada rayuwata da taka, kawai dai ina son Ka fahimce ni , kayi min Kuma Uzuri, ina son na haihu da Kai amma ba yanzu ba........?

Bata karasa abinda take fade ba, Taufiq ya juya a fusace ya fice da ga dakin  cikin matukar bacin rai.Bai taba zaton Jin abinda ya ji daga mujaheeda ba, haushinta da haushin maganganun ta yake sosai.
     Tun daga wannan ranar wata sabuwar fitinar ta kunno Kai tsakanin Taufiq da mujaheeda. Domin dai dagaske take bata son cikin bata son haihuwa yanzu Shi Kuma Yana son cikin Yana kuma son haihuwa yanzu. Haj murjanatu kuwa tsabar son rai da son zuciya da Kuma soyayyar mujaheeda data makantar da ita bayan mujaheeda ta bi duk da itama can karkashin zuciyarta tana da bukatar Samun jika daga mujaheeda to amma tunda mujaheedar bata so itama bata so.
  Ranar farko da Haj murjanatu ta shiga cikin maganar, ta nuna ma Taufiq menene don an cire wannan cikin bayan gaba Allah zai bata wani. Cikin fusata Taufiq yace "Wane garanti gareta na Allah zai bata cikin alokacin da take so bayan Shi ya bata alokacin da yake so tace bata so, Bana son mu Sami gagarumar matsala akan wannan maganar domin babu Wanda ya isa ya cire min ciki, me kuka dauke ni ne sakarai Wanda baisan ciwon Kansa ba ko me?" Ya tashi cike da fushi yace " Ina son Ku sani ni ba bawanku bane , na Kuma San darajata da yancina don haka duk Wanda ya taba min ciki ko waye Shi sai munyi Shari'a da shi " 
  Yana Gama fadin haka Bai saurari komai ba ya fita daga falon,
  Yana dukan iska, Bai taba Jin zafi da damuwa akan duk abubuwan da suke biyo bayan aurensa ba irin wannan.

   Haj murjanatu ta jinjina Kai ta kurama mujaheeda Ido tace " mujaheeda Taufiq ba bazai taba yarda da abinda kike so ba, " mujaheeda ta numfasa cike da damuwa tace " Ni Kuma mummy Allah ya sani Bana son haihuwa yanzu, gabana har faduwa yake yi idan na tuna wai ina da ciki, amma kwata kwata yaya Taufiq ya ki fahimtata".
   Haj murjanatu tace " bazai fahimce ki ba,". Sosai Haj murjanatu ta Fara tunanin ko dai asirin da malllam yayi ya Karye ne domin ba tayi tsammanin irin wannan kafiyar daga Taufiq ba.

Wannan Karon Kuma mujaheeda ba zata iya zuwa wajen surikinta da wannan maganar ba Wanda tasan shine zaisa Taufiq yayi Mata abinda take so.
   Ràshin jituwa ta Sami matsuguni tsakanin Taufiq da mujaheeda.Babu wani kwanciyar hankali, ko gidan Taufiq baya son ya dawo.

    Aikin hanyar da kamfanin su Taufiq ya ke yi, yayi nisa, anyi canjin ma'aikata Taufiq Yana daya daga cikin wadanda aka tura cigaba da yin titin kauyen Durmi.

Cikin lokaci kankani Taufiq ya gama shiri. Saura kwana biyu ya tafi ranar a gidansu ya uni har dare tare da iyayensa domin dai yasan idan irin wannan aikin ya taso yakan Dade Bai dawo gida ba, tun Kuma bayan aurensa bai je irin wannan aikin ba. Sai dare da zai dawo yayi musu sallama, kamar kullum sunyi masa fatan alheri da fatan dawowa lafiya.
    A  Daren ranar har sun kwanta, ya kasa jurewa ya tashi ya zauna gami da kunna wutar dakin, Yana kallon gefen da mujaheeda take kwance ya Kira sunanta a hankali, ta dan motsa a hankali sannan ta amsa.
  Yace " ki tashi ina son yin magana da ke".
Ta dan yi Jim kafin ta motsa sosai ta tashi ta zauna tana kallonsa Wanda rabon da ta kalli fuskarsa haka ta kwana biyu.

Tace " ina jinka", ya jinjina Kai Yana kokarin gyara zamansa.

Sannan yace " Ina son mu kawo karshen wannan matsalar da take neman ta rushe duk wata soyayya da jituwa da ke tsakaninmu".
   Tace " Ni ban dauki wannan abin amatsayin abinda zamu zauna mu rasa kwanciyar hankali saboda Shi ba, Kai ne Ka dauka da zafi".

Ya jinjina Kai yace " Bana son wata magana kawai ina son ki sauka daga wannan akidar ta ki, kiyi hakuri mu rungumi kyautar da Allah yayi mana muyi masa godiya"
 Mujaheeda tace " Ka kasa fahimtata yaya Taufiq,...?.

" In dai akan wannan ne bazan taba fahimtar ki ba,kawai ina son ki cire tunanin cire cikin jikinki domin wannan cikin shine farincikina" shuru kawai tayi bata Kara magana ba, har ya cigaba da maganganunsa masu kama da nasiha.
  Daga bisani yace " jibi Idan Allah ya kaimu Zan tafi aiki daya daga cikin hanyoyin da kamfaninmu yake aiki, Kuma Zan dauki lokaci ban dawo ba,"

Ya dubeta sosai yace " saboda haka ina son ki Kula da kanki da abinda ke cikin ki don Allah ".
  Tace " Allah ya kaimu" tana fadin haka ya juya ta kwanta abinta ba tare da nuna wani alhinin rabuwa da Shi ba.ya Dade zaune kafin shima ya kwanta amma ya kasa runtsawa matsalolin da ke cikin aurensa sun ishe shi.

Ko ranar da zai tafi ba wani sakin fuska ko wata kulawa da ya Samu daga mujaheeda.Hakanan ya tafi, zuciyarsa a jagule tunaninsa na ga cikin jikinta Yana adduar Allah ya tsare masa cikinsa.
   TO BE CONTINUE

DAGA TASKAR YAR GIDAN IMAM

NI MA MATARSA CE
              NA
HABIBA ABUBAKAR IMAM
  ( YAR GIDAN IMAM )
BABI NA ARBA'IN DA HUDU
              ***********
     Bayan isar su wajen aikin hanyar, dole kauyen Durmi suka karasa shi da sauran kananan ma'aikata Yan'uwansa domin neman dakin da zasu kama haya, domin bazai iya Zama a filin da aka Dan gine ma ma'aikatan ba a bakin hanya.

  Taufiq tare da abokan aikinsa guda uku da Suma suke son Samun daki a garin .

 Suka shiga cikin kauyen a motar Taufiq. Fitowar Mallam Umar Kenan da ga masallaci sallar la'sar duk da an Jima da kammala sallar yayi zamansa a masallacin bai fito ba sai yanzu biyar saura na yamma.

Taufiq ya tsaida motar a gaban Mallam Umar gami da Bude kofar motar ya fito cikin cikar Kamala yayi masa sallama.
    Mallam Umar ya dakata Yana amsa sallamar gami da Mika masa hannu sukayi musabaha.

Sannan Taufiq yace " Mu baki ne, muna daya daga cikin masu aikin hanyar da ake , mun zo ne da bukatar Samun gida ko dakunan da zamu zauna na haya, don Allah idan kasan yadda zaayi mu Samu to muna neman taimakon Ka".
   Fara'ar fuskar Mallam Umar ta Kara fadada yace " Aah masha Allah , sannunku da zuwa,ai mun jidadin yin hanyar nan saboda matsalar mu Kenan tunda ake dimukuradiyya bamu taba Samun tagomashinta ba sai yanzu" Taufiq yace " Allah sarki, Dama ai hakane".

Mallam Umar yace "Akwai wani gidan haya, amma tunda Ku baki ne ba zaku iya ganewa ba , saboda haka mu je na Kai Ku".

Cike da jindadin yadda Mallam Umar ya tarbesu Taufiq yace " To mun gode Allah ya saka da alheri".

Daganan Mallam Umar ya shige mota, Yana yi musu kwatance har gidan Alh sabitu, me kudin garin Durmi Kenan shi kadaine yake da gidan haya agarin, sai yadda ya so yake yi .

Mallam Umar bai tafi ba sai da ya hada su Taufiq da Alh sabitu, sannan yayi musu sallama ya tafi bayan Taufiq yayi masa godiya mai yawa.

Sun Sami dakuna agidan Alh sabitu, ganinsu Kuma daga birni ga Kuma aikin da suka zo yi yasa ya tsauwala kudin duk da haka sun Karba.

A ranar Taufiq har katifa ya saya sauran kayan bukatun da zai nema ya bari sai ahankali.

    Muhseena dai tuni ta tursasama kanta hakuri da dangana akan ràshin Kabir, ta yarda komai na Allah ne sai dai bata Jin zata Kara Samun wani da zai dauki kaddarar aurenta idan Kuma haka Allah ya tsara Mata babu yadda zatayi.

Haka zalika kullum tana adduar Allah yasa yadda ta sa hakuri da dangana akan lamarin , Allah yasa shima Kabir ya sama Kansa hakan , duk da ta Dade bata ji daga gare shi ba, tun sakon kudin daya turo Mata na karshe  ta Kira Shi akan Kada ya Kara aiko Mata da ko wane sako saboda bata San wane irin alaka bane atsakaninsu yanzu,
   Bayan nan aranar ta rufe nambar sa daga kiranta.saboda tana son ta mallaki natsuwarsa gaba daya tana Kuma son ta manta da Shi ta daina ma tunaninsa domin ta hakane kadai zata iya mantawa da Shi.

Ita kanta Hajara kullum addu'ar ta bai wuce Allah ya kawo ma muhseena Miji Nagari ba Wanda zai iya aurenta a yadda take ya Kuma Kula da ita cikin tausayi da Jin Kai.

      Mujaheeda ta cigaba da Zama da cikin da Sam Sam bata son haihuwarsa. Zuciyarta na yawan saka Mata ta zubar da cikin amma Kuma tana tunanin irin matakin da Taufiq zai dauka akanta idan hakan ya kasance .
   Domin dai iya fushin da zatayi da Taufiq tasan tana sonsa so na hakika , ba Kuma zata iya rabuwa da Shi ba akan ko wane dalili.
   Hakan ne yasa ta kasa yarda ta bi abinda zuciyarta ta ke gaya Mata. Ita kanta Haj murjanatu ta fahimci ta na dai Goya Mata baya ne kawai saboda taga abinda take so kenan ba wai don ranta na so na hakika ba.

Tun tafiyar Taufiq babu wata wayar kirki da suke yi, domin ko ya Kira sai ta ga damar dauka ita Kuma bata kiransa . Fushi take sosai da Shi. Duk da Shi burinsa kullum shine ya Kira ya ji lafiyar cikin jikinta.

   Tabangaren Taufiq dai sosai yake Jindadin zaman kauyen Durmi. Duk da babu wani abu na more rayuwa to Yana matukar jindadin halayen mutanan garin na son bako tare da nuna tarbiyyar yaransu , Yana sha'awar yadda yake ganin yaran kauyen suna zuwa makarantar islamiya domin duk yamma idan ya dawo daga wajen aiki nan yake lalacewa a kofar gidan Yana kallon yadda yaran kauyen suke dawowa daga islamiyya.

Yauma kamar kullum Taufiq Yana zaune a kofar gidan, aka taso Yara Yan islamiyya kowacce na kokarin wucewa idanunsa na kansu.

Dammm gabansa ya Fadi sosai asanadiyysr hada Ido da muhseena da yayi, Wanda ya rasa dalili illa dai ya ji wani abu ya tsurga masa tun daga Kansa har zuwa Yan yatsun kafarsa.
     Hakanan ya ji ya kasa dauke idanunsa daga muhseena. Harta fahimci hakan ta dan dube Shi sosai irin kallon da ya tsareta da Shi ne yasa bata San lokacin da ta gaishe shi ba, da sauri ya amsa yana cigaba da kallonta .
  Hakanne yasa muhseena tayi saurin barin wajen .
Amma Taufiq ya kasa dauke kallonsa daga gareta harta bacema ganinsa, amma wani abin mamaki Shi Kansa idan an tambaye Shi dalilin kallon da yake Mata da Kuma dalilin Jin sauyin da ya ji atare da Shi to fa shi Kansa bai sani ba.

Hakanan tun daga wannan ranar Taufiq ya dorama Kansa bai tashi a inda yake har sai yaga wucewar muhseena.

Fahimtar hakan da tayi ne yasa gaba daya ta canja hanya ta daina biyowa ta hanyar da zata ga Taufiq, 
    Domin dai bata San dalilin wannan sa idon da yake Mata ba.

   Yau kusan kwana biyar Kenan Taufiq bai Kara sa muhseena a idanunsa ba. Bai Kuma San dalilin da yasa ya ji ya damu da hakan ba, akai akai Kuma tunanin ta yake fado masa .
    Kabir dai ya takurama Kansa ta hanyar yin hakuri da muhseena domin bin umarnin iyayensa duk da ji yake kamar zai mutu.
   Baya farinciki baya shiga jama'a Yana neman ya sama Kansa ciwon damuwa.
  Kullum Alh Musa Yana nuna masa babu abinda ya gagari ubangiji, don haka yayi ta addua , tunda da dai ya fi kowa sanin mahaifansa ba zasu taba yarda da bukatarsa ba.

 Hakanne yasa Kabir ya dage da addua ya maida  komai ga Allah, ya Kuma yarda yayi ràshin masoyiya, wacce har abada bazai taba mantawa da ita ba .
     Makonnin Taufiq hudu a Durmi. Ranar wata laraba wacce muhseena ba zata taba mantawa da ita ba acikin tarihin rayuwarta.

Tun safe ta ke Jin irin ciwon kan da take yi Wanda yake matukar gigitata.
   Duk da haka tayi kokarin tafiya islamiya saboda bata son ta zauna a gida, ko da ta je islamiyyar shima bata wani samu sukuni ba kallo daya zaka mata Ka gane cewa bata da lafiya, duk idanunta sunyi jajir haka zalika dishi dishi take gani.
   Daya daga cikin malamin islamiyyar su ne ya Lura da halin da take ciki.
 Hakanne yasa ya bata izinin tafiya gida.
Ba tayi masa musu ba , saboda itama tasan jarumtarta ta kusa gazawa sosai take Jin kamar zaa cire Mata rai saboda azabar ciwon Kai .
  Tana tafiya rike da littafan makarantarta,cikin yanayi na jigata bibbiyu take gani. Bata da zabi da ya wuce ta bi ta hanyar da ta daina bi adalilin tafi sauri akan waccan hanyar da take bi, domin ta kosa kwarai ta Karasa gida saboda ta kusa sarewa.

 Tafiya kawai take bata San inda take jefa kafarta ba. Dai dai lokacin gaba daya muhseena ta ji ganinta ya yaye duniyar na juya Mata.
  Lokaci daya ta sulale kasa, bata Kara sanin inda kanta ya ke ba.
Tana nan gefen hanya har Yara sun Fara taruwa.
   Dai dai lokacin motar Taufiq ta taho da nufin zuwa gida ganin Yara zagaye da wata mace yasa yayi gaggawar karasawa kofar gidan yayi parking din motarsa ya fito da sauri ya nufi wajen.

 Gabansa yayi mummunan faduwa a lokacin da yayi Ido biyu da yarinyar da kusan kullum sai yayi tunanin inda zai Kara ganinta.

  Ya duba duk babu wata mace ko wani babban namiji awajen da zai Taya Shi  taimakama muhseena.

Hakan tasa Bai saurari komai ba ya sa hannu ya dauki muhseena cancak cikin tsuma da damuwa gami da tashin hankali.
  Kai tsaye cikin gidan ya wuce da ita , ya Bude dakinsa ya shiga da ita ciki ya kwantar da ita akan katifar dakin.
  Ya rasa wane irin taimako zai bata, don haka ya shiga dibar ruwa Yana zuba Mata a fuska.

A hankali ta dan Bude idonta amma har alokacin bata San inda take ba.
  A lokacin da Taufiq ya shigo da ita gidan akan idon abokin Mallam kallah ne, Mallam Hakilu ya shawo kwana Kenan abinda idonsa ya gani kawai Taufiq ne dauke da muhseena ya shige da ita cikin gida.
  Cikin tsoro da fargaba ya wuce da sauri kamar zai fadi saboda sauri. Kai tsaye gidan Mallam kallah ya wuce.
  Ya dinga kwala sallama. Sabira ta dubi Mallam kallah tace "'kai jama'a wanene wannan yake kwala irin wannan sallama kamar yaki".

Mallam kallah ya amsa sallamar shigowarsa kenan gidan. 
   Yadan saki tsaki yace " ina Zan San waye, nagaji Hutu kawai nake bukata" tace " Ai sai Ka leka Ka gani".

Ya wuce ya fita, ya Sami Mallam Hakilu cikin kaguwa " yauwa Mallam kallah" cewar Mallam Hakilu, Mallam kallah ya dube Shi yace " Dama Kaine , lafiya ko irin wannan sallama haka".
   Yace " ina fa lafiya, ina tabbatar maka tarihi zai maimaita Kansa a karkarar nan". Mallam kallah yace " Meya faru? Don Allah Ka natsu kamin bayani ".
   Mallam Hakilu yace " wadannan bakin masu gyaran hanya da suke haya a gidan Alh sabitu ne na Ganshi ya dauki muhseena yar wajen Hajara ya shige da ita cikin gidan, Allah kadai yasan abinda ke faruwa yanzu atsakaninsu".
   Mallam kallah ya bugi kirji ya zaro idanu waje yace " Ka tabbatar da abinda kake fade" yace "kwarai da gaske , mu je Ka gani da idonka" Mallam kallah yace " mu je mu Kara daukar wasu shaidun, abinda suke yi a birni ne zasu zo suyi mana anan to daga yau sun gama aikata hakan ".

Cikin fusata suka wuce suka tafi suna maganganu.sai da Mallam kallah ya kwashi mutane guda uku ya Kuma je ya taho da Mallam Umar Wanda tunda suka taho yake tambayar dalilin kiransa Amma Mallam kallah yace idan sun je ya gani.
   Cikin daurewar Kai da fargaba Mallam Umar ya cigaba da binsu har gidan Alh sabitu, Kai tsaye suka fada cikin gidan.
   Mallam Hakilu ya nuna wani daki yace " ina zaton  nan suka shiga don kofar dakin ne kawai akwai takalimi da alamar akwai mutane aciki".

Cikin zafin Nama gaba daya suka rufta dakin.

Dai dai lokacin muhseena ta Fara dawowa hayyacinta tana kokarin tashi daga kwanciyar da take idanunta cike da neman Karin bayanin yadda akayi ta zo dakin lokaci daya Kuma tsoro ya mamaye ta don tsintar kanta a inda bata taba tsammani ba.
Karar shigowar su Mallam kallah ya Kara firgita muhseena.

 Shima Taufiq din a tsorace ya Mike Yana kallon bataliyar mutanen da suka shigo dakin suke yi masa kallon tuhuma gami da tsana da kyamata.............!
