DAGA TASKAR YAR GIDAN IMAM


NI MA MATARSA CE
            NA
HABIBA ABUBAKAR IMAM
   ( YAR GIDAN IMAM )
BABI NA ARBA'IN DA BIYAR
             **********
    Mallam kallah ya saki salati Yana janyo Mallam Umar Yana fadin " shigo sosai Ka ga abinda yar taka take aikatawa, ai dama barewa bata gudu danta yayi rarrafe Ka dai gani ko".

Idanun Mallam Umar suka tsayu Kan muhseena wacce take ta kokarin tashi amma ta kasa.
    Bakinta ahankali yake furta " Baba abinda kuke zato bashi bane, bansan Shi ba babu abinda ya hadani da Shi, Nima farkawa kawai nayi na ganni a dakin nan na rantse bansan inda nake ba".
 
Mallam kallah da sauran mutanen su kayi Kan Taufiq cikin fusata suna tambayar me kayi Mata ?. Ayayin da Mallam Umar jiri ya shiga dibarsa yayi saurin jingina da jikin bango.

Cikin tashin hankali Taufiq ya Fara kokarin kare Kansa Amma mutanen gurin sun hana Shi damar yin hakan .
    .maimakon hakan ma sai ingiza keyarsa sukayi cikin fada da karaji.mallam kallah ya dubi Mallam Umar yace " mun wuce wajen megari kana iya taho da muhseena itama, domin ba zamu taba barin wanna shegantakar ba , abinda su keyi a birni ne zasu zo suna mana a kauye, idan bamu dauki mataki ba duk a haka zasu Gama bata mana yaran kauyen".
   Yana Gama fadin haka ya bi bayan su Mallam Hakilu Yana cigaba da fada da sababi.
 
Sai bayan tafiyar su ne Mallam Umar ya zubama muhseena Ido ,Yana kokarin lafarma da zuciyarsa radadin da take.

A sanyaye ya Matso kusa da yarsa idanunsa jajir saboda damuwa, yace " muhseena ki ji tsoron Allah ki gaya min gaskiyar abinda ya faru makaranta kika tafi ta yaya kika kare anan?, Ina da kyakkyawan zato akanki Bana Jin zaki iya aikata abinda bai kai wannan ba balle wannan al'amarin da kika fi kowa sanin illarsa ta yaya kika shigo dakin nan".

Ta kasa tsaida hawayen da tuni ya ke zuba Mata ta dube Shi sosai cikin zubar hawaye tace"Dana aikata abinda ake zargi na rantse Baba gara na mutu, domin idan akwai abinda na tsana arayuwata to zina ce, don Allah Ka yarda da ni bansan lokacin da na zo dakin nan ba bansan komai ba,".

Ta cigaba da kuka tana fadin "Ràshin lafiyar da na tashi da ita tun safe ce ta matsa min a makaranta na kasa sukuni shine Mallam ya Bani izinin komawa gida, to ina cikin tafiya bansan inda Kaina yake ba na dai ji na daina gani daganan ban Kara Jin komai ba ban Kara sanin komai ba sai farkawa nayi na ganni a wañnan dakin wannan mutumin ya na shafa min ruwa a fuska ".

Mallam Umar ya dade Yana kallon gaskiyar zancen ta a cikin kwayar idonta, yasan yarsa yasan halinta hakika yasan ba zata taba aikata abinda ake zarginsu da aikatawa.kamar yadda yake ji aransa Shi Kansa Taufiq din ba zai aikata wannan ta'addancin ba ga muhseena.
    Domin tun haduwarsu ta farko Wanda daga Shi ba su Kara haduwa ba ya gamsu da natsuwa da kamalar yaron.
   Ya dubi muhseena jigatar ciwo ta bayyana sosai a fuskarta . Yace " ki yi shuru ki daina kuka na yarda da duk abinda kika gaya min domin nasan ba zaki taba yi min karya ba".

Ta Kara fashewa da kuka tace " na ji saukin damuwata Baba tunda Ka yarda da ni duk abinda wani zai dauka bazan taba damuwa ba,"

  Mallam Umar ya umarceta da ta kokarta ta tashi su tafi. Daker ta iya tashi suka tafi alokacin sauran masu hayar gidan suka Fara dawowa duk Wanda ya wuce sai ya juyo ya Kara kallon su muhseena.

Mallam Umar ya fahimci  muhseena tana cikin jigatar ciwo da yayi niyyar su wuce gida amma ya ji tsoron sharrin su Mallam kallah hakan yasa su Ka wuce wajen megari.
   Suna zuwa daker muhseena ta samu waje ta zauna jiri na kwasar ta. Sun tadda takaddama da hayaniya tayi yawa daker megari ya samu su kayi shuru.
  Zuwa lokacin duk inda ran Taufiq ya Kai to fa ya baci sosai ya Fara Jin danasanin taimaka ma muhseena da yayi haushi da takaicin yadda mutanen garin ke kokarin Dora masa mummunan zargin da Bai aikata ba Bai da wata niyya da ta wuce na taimakon muhseena.
   Amma ana neman a dora masa mummunan kazafin da zai iya bata dukkan wani kyakkyawan halinsa. Haka zalika mutanen garin kokarin dole sai ya yarda ya Kuma karbi lefin da bai aikata ba.

Taufiq yayi gaggawar kallon muhseena yace cikin daburcewa da damuwa " ke bauwar Allah ki Fadi tsakani da Allah nayi miki wani abu" da sauri ta girgiza Kai tace " Bansan komai ba saboda bana cikin hayyacina amma nasan koma meye ni babu abinda ya shiga tsakanina da Kai ,".

Mallam Hakilu ya daka Mata harara yace " ke da Allah can , ai dama dole ki Fadi haka saboda ki Kare barnar da kuka aikata, to Baki isa ba babu Wanda ya isa ya zo garin mu ya cimu da yaki".
  
Mallam Umar ya daga masa hannu yace " Don Allah Ku bar wannan maganar Ina son na Kai yata asibiti saboda bata da lafiya, duk Kuma wannan maganganun da kuke yi babu gaskiya a cikinta abinda muhseena da Taufiq suka fada gaskiya ne na Kuma yarda na gasgata su".

Taufiq ya lumshe idanunsa saboda dadin maganar Mallam Umar, domin duk wajen Shi kadai ne ya Fadi abinda yake son ji.

Mallam kallah ya sakar ma Mallam Umar wata irin harara, sannan yace "Don Allah Mallam Ka dawo hayyacinka lokaci yayi da zaka daina mara ma mara gaskiya baya harka bashi mafaka, kana kokarin boye gaskiya Ka tusama mutane karya a zukatansu kamar yadda kayi alokacin matsala irin wannan ta mahaifiyarta, Ka dage dole saika wanketa Akan laifin da ta aikata, to ayau ba zamu yarda ba".
  Ya juya ya dubi megari yace " megari kaji koken mu Ka taimaki mutanen garinka , Ka taimaki tarbiyyar yayan mu, kayi hukuncin da muka roka kawai wannan yaron ko yaki ko ya so sai ya auri yarinyar nan a yanzu ba sai anjima ba domin ta hakane kadai zamu kawo karshen wannan keta haddin da mutanen birni sukema yayanmu domin idan aka barshi na rantse sun dinga cigaba da aikata hakan Kenan yanzu kuwa idan akayi hakan zai Zama ishara ga masu son aikata hakan nan gaba".

Nan da nan muhseena taji ta wartsake daga ciwon da take yi, domin Jin wannan maganar me kama da mafarki a daura musu aure? Ta tambayi kanta.
   Mallam Umar ya dubesu yace " wace irin magana ce wannan". Taufiq shima ya Kara gyara tsugunonsa cike da tashin hankali yace " Ta yaya Zan auri yarinyar da ban sani ba babu wata alaka a tsakaninmu".
   Megari yayi masa wani kyakkyawan kallo yace " Rufe min baki mugu, babu wata alaka atsakaninku amma aka tsintota a dakinka, maganar banza kawai wa zaka maida kananan Yara, ". Ya juyo itama muhseena ya watsa Mata harara yace "Wa zai karbi shaidarki bayan bakinku daya meya kaiki dakinsa idan ba dama kun Saba aikata Masha'a ba, idan an tura ki makaranta sai ki wuce dakinsa ko, ai Baki yar akasa ba".

Mallam Umar ya ce" megari kayi ma maganar nan kyakkyawan bincike...? Kafin ya karasa magana megari ya katse shi " kaima ban son Jin komai daga gareka saboda yarka zaka kare, ni nine Zan yanke hukuncin da ya dace Kuma dole abi abinda na zartar".

Zuwa lokacin kuka muhseena take me karfin gaske tana rike da kanta tana fadin " innalillahi wainna'ilaihirrajiun". Shi Kuma Taufiq gaba daya gumi ya Gama jike masa Riga ya Fara tunanin wace mahallaka ya kawo Kansa wace irin kaddara ce yau zata fada masa.
  Ya daga idonsa gefensa mutanen gari ne daya gefen ma haka dukkan su a fusace suke sosai yake ji aransa babu abinda ba zasu iya yi masa ba.

Daker ya Bude Baki yace"Don Allah Ku yi hakuri na rantse babu abinda ya shiga tsakanina da ita taimakonta kawai nayi"
Itama cikin kuka tace "Nima na rantse babu abinda ya shiga tsakanin mu don Allah Kada Ku yi mana mummunan hukunci". Mallam kallah ya daka Mata harara yace " ki rufe min Baki ko na zuba Miki Mari".

Megari yace " fito da kudin aljihunka" cikin tsuma ya sa hannu a aljihu ya fito da duk kudin jikinsa kusan nera dubu talatin, megari yayi ma wani dogari umarnin ya amshi kudin ya Mikama Mallam Umar. 
    Cikin sanyin jiki ya amshi kudin Yana juya su alamun neman Karin bayani.

Megari ya dubi  Taufiq yace " matanka nawa?", Cikin rawar murya yace"Matata daya". Megari ya dawo da kallonsa ga Mallam Umar yace " wannan kudin sadakin yarka ne,". Taufiq da Mallam Umar da muhseena suka zaro Ido cike da gigita kafin daya daga cikinsu ya yi magana, megari yace "Jama'a ni megari nine Zan Zama  waliyyin wannan bakon Mallam Umar Kuma zai Zama waliyyin yarsa, ga shaidu ga kowa yanzun nan zamu daura auren su".

Gaban muhseena yayi dam ya Fadi gaba daya ta rikice ta rasa a wace duniya take cikin kuka take fadin " Baba Ka taimakeni Kada Ka bari su kashe min rayuwa, ta yaya Zan auri mutumin da ban San wanene shi ba"
   Shima Taufiq din gaba daya rikicewa yayi yace " ta yaya zaku hukuntani da wannan mugun al_amarin bayan ba ni da laifi  taimako kawai nayi".

Mallam Umar ya wuce a sukwane gaban megari Yana cewa " Kada Ka min haka megari yata tana da gaskiya kamar yadda bakon nan ya ke da gaskiya ba ko wane Dan birni bane yake da mugun Hali Kada Ku sabauta rayuwar yayan nan".

Mallam kallah yace " haba Mallam umaru gata fa zaayi ma yarka, yanzu kana tunanin ko babu abinda ya shiga tsakanin muhseena da Taufiq, akwai wani da zai iya auren muhseena bayan bakin fentin da ke shafe a jikinta sannan ta Kara da wannan Kuma to waye zai aureta, Kuma bayan haka lalle Bama son yayan mu su cigaba da ganin mummunar tarbiyyar da wata kila su yi tunanin Fara bin irin tarbiyyar".

Gaba daya mutanen wajen suka dauka " Kwarai da gaske mun gaji da irin wannan rayuwar muna neman canji ,dole ya aureta Kuma ya dauke mana ita agarin ".
 Daker megari ya sassauta hayaniyar da ake yi a wajen.

Wanda zuwa lokacin Taufiq ya kusa shidewa a zaune saboda tsabar tashin hankali. Haka itama muhseena ta fada mugun tashin hankali mara misaltuwa.

Wanda bai sa megari ya fasa abinda yayi niyya ba domin Mallam Umar Yana ji Yana gani bashi da yadda zaayi haka aka daura auren Taufiq da muhseena.

Tashin hankali Wanda baa sa masa rana. Gaba daya Taufiq ji yayi kamar an dora masa dala da goron dutse turus yayi akasa kamar bashi da laka.

Megari ya  dubi Taufiq yace " ci da sha da sutura da wajen Zama da kula da lafiya da iliminta duk ya rataya a wuyan Ka , don haka idan ka tashi tafiya zamu tabbatar da Ka tafi da matarka".
   Taufiq ya kasa magana kawai ji yayi hawaye Yana fita a idonsa. 
  Ayayinda mutanen gurin kowa sai murna da son barka yake yi tare da jinjina ma hukuncin megari . Sosai suke Jin da wuya akara Samun wani me tsaurin idon da zai Kara aikata abinda Taufiq da muhseena suka aikata. Ahankali kowa ya Fara watsewa.

Amma Mallam Umar ya kasa motsa ko da kafarsa , sai alokacin ya Lura da muhseena ta sulale kasa sumammiya a gigice ya yo kanta Yana Kiran sunanta a firgice.
  TO BE CONTINUE

DAGA TASKAR YAR GIDAN IMAM


NI MA MATARSA CE
             NA
HABIBA ABUBAKAR IMAM
  ( YAR GIDAN IMAM )
BABI NA ARBA'IN DA SHIDA
           ********
   Amma ina muhseena ta riga ta fita hayyacinta ta Suma bata San Wanda ke kanta ba.
    Mallam Umar ya cigaba da girgiza muhseena, shima megari ya taso ya taho , Amma wani abun mamaki Taufiq mikewa yayi ya Mata kallon tsaf ya juya zai tafi ba tare da nuna damuwarsa ga halin da take ciki ba.

Da sauri megari yayi masa tsawa yace " dawo ina zaka Maza Ka zo ba matarka bace ai ance kana da mota Ka dakko motar Ka kaita asibiti tun kafin kaga fushi na".
 Cikin tsabar takaici da tsanar muhseena da lokaci daya ya ji ya na neman ya baibaye masa zuciya.

Ya juya ya ya tafi dakko motar ba tare da ya ce komai ba.

Duk suna Kan muhseena har Taufiq ya dawo da motar ya dauke su zuwa asibiti daya kwal na garin . Harda megari aka tafi asibitin sai da aka bata gado aka tabbatar ta dawo hayyacinta saboda taimakon gaggawar da aka bata sannan megari ya dubi Taufiq da har zuwa lokacin bakinsa Bai furta komai ba.

Yace "Komai na hidimar asibitin Yana hannun Taufiq amatsayinka na mijinta ya Kuma Zama dole Ka Kula da lafiyarta har Allah ya bata lafiya, Ka kuka da kanka idan Ka Saba wannan tsarin,"
   Ya Kara kallon yadda Taufiq duk yayi wujuga wujuga ya fita hayyacinsa yace " Garama Ka karbi wannan auren hannu bibbiyu domin ba auren wasa bane zahiri ne wannan,  muhseena matarka ce, Kuma ba zamu lamunci duk wani abu da zakayi Wanda ya sabama aure ba, Ido Kuma Yana kanka Baka Isa kabar garin nan ba tare da matarka ba kafin Kuma Ku tafi Kaine zaka cigaba da daukar dawainiyar Komai nata".

Ya Kara kallonsa yace " Mu Yan kauye ne Amma munsan girma da martabarmu duk Kuma Wanda yace zai sabama dokar mu to hukuncinsa bazai yi kyau ba".
   Shi dai babu abinda ya ce Yana tsaye tamkar gunki, zuciyarsa na tukuki sosai yake son ya fitar da hawaye saboda ya Sami saukin tashin hankalin da yake ciki.

Wanda yafi daukar lamarin tamkar mafarki. Daga bisani megari yayi sallama da Mallam Umar ya fice.

Mallam Umar ya dago Yana duban Taufiq da yanayin tashin hankalin da yake ciki, Karo da dama tausayinsu gaba daya ya Kara kama Shi, tun ganinsa na farko da Taufiq ya ji ya kwanta masa arai saboda ya yarda da Kamala da mutuncin da yake kwance akan fuskarsa. Ya so kwarai alokacin yayi masa magana amma ya kasa ya bari tukuna a samu natsuwa,
       Kamar yadda shima Taufiq din ya kasa yin magana.
     Ko da zai bar asibitin baiyi ma kowa magana ba ya tafi.
     Bai Jima da barin asibitin ba Hajara ta shigo asibitin cikin matukar gigita.

    Duk iya yadda Mallam Umar ya so ya shawo kanta ta daina kukan da take to fa abu ya fassakara.
    Kam ta kama muhseena ta rungume tun dawowar muhseena hayyacinta sai yanzu ne itama ta fashe da kuka Mai matukar karfi harda gunji,cikin jigata take fadin " Inna rayuwata ta kare tawa kaddarar ba irin taki bace,Inna an aura min mutumin da bansani ba bansan ko Shi waye ba bansani ba ko zai iya rayuwa da ni, Inna babu komai acikin kwayar idanunsa sai tsanata".

Hajara ta Kara kwakume yarta kamar zata tsaga jikinta ta maida ta ciki.

Tace " kiyi hakuri Allah zai dube ki muhseena zai kawo Miki sauki domin dukkan tsanani Yana tare da sauki".
    A hankali Mallam Umar ya dubi Hajara da take gaba daya a birkice sannan yace " Ya akayi kika ji? Waye ya gayá Miki?".
    Ta juyo cikin hawaye tana kallonsa tace " Baba kallah ne ya zo har gida ya sanar da ni , to na tafi gidan megari anan ake Gaya min ta Suma kun taho da ita asibiti, Mallam wannan wace irin masifa ce?".
   Mallam Umar cikin tsananin bakinciki yace "Ki natsu don Allah Hajara komai Yana da iyaka, kibar wannan maganar har sai mun koma gida".
    Hajara tayi shuru ba ta Kara maganar ba , amma duk da haka hawayenta ya kasa tsayuwa ita da muhseena kuka suke sosai Wanda yin hakanne kadai yake Dan lafar musu da damuwar.

Tun komawar Taufiq  gidan yake zaune adakin cikin matsanancin bakincikin da bai misaltuwa , tunda yake ko a tarihi bai taba ganin mahaukacin kauye irin wannan ba.
   Yanzu shikenan matansa biyu, dam! Kirjinsa ya buga ya Kara firgicewa saboda tuno mujaheeda da mahaifiyarta da yayi hakika babban tashin hankali ne a gabansa tashin hankalin da yafi Wanda yake ciki a yanzu.

Ta yaya zaayi masa haka, zaa sa Shi abinda baiyi niyya ba. A hankali zuciyarsa ta  cigaba da kokarin Dora masa tsanar muhseena. 
     Ko a mafarki bai taba tsammanin wannan mummunan abin zai faru da Shi ba, hakika zuwansa Durmi masifa ne yayi danasanin zuwa. Shin yaya zaiyi ta ina zai bulloma wannan gagarumin al'amarin.

Gaba daya ji yake kamar Yana zazzabi jikinsa ya dauki zafi sosai.Abokin aikinsa salisu ya fado dakin a gigice Yana kallonsa yanayin da ya ganshi ciki ne ya tabbatar masa da gaskiyar abinda ya ji, ya zube gabansa Yana kallonsa fuska a dame yace "Da gaske ne Kenan abinda naji " ya dago fuska sharkaf da zufa yace " har kaji Kenan?" Ya jinjina Kai yace "Na ji, wai menene ya faru haka"?

   Cikin tsabar tashin hankali ya Gama gaya masa komai.
     Ya Kara da cewa 
"Itace yarinyar da nake 
     Gaya maka ina yawan ganin wucewarta islamiyya daga baya Kuma na daina ganinta 
    Ranar da Kuma 
Na tashi ganinta yau Kenan shine wannan masifar ta afka mun".
   Salisu ya cije lebe yace "Amma mutanen nan Basu da mutunci sun Raina maka hankali wannan abu sai kace a littafi, yanzu to meye abin yi".

Taufiq yace " Na rantse maka ina cikin tsaka me wuya Nima bansan ya zanyi ba na rasa wane irin tunani zanyi".

Salisu yace " Tikisa, babbar magana lalle Kam kana cikin tsaka me wuya auren da babu shiri babu so ko Kauna, wannan shine asalin auren dolen"
   Taufiq ya numfasa gami da rike Kansa yace " jama'ar kauyen nan na tabbatar Basu da hankali babu wani hukunci da na isa na yanke idan ba na yarda da wannan auren ba domin sabanin hakan babu abinda ba zasu iya yi min ba na rantse maka tsoronsu ma nake ji".

Salisu cikin damuwa yace "Gaskiya kana cikin jarabawa Allah ya kawo maka mafita.

Ya kwantar da Kansa Akan cinyoyinsa Abu goma da ashirin ga matsalar mujaheeda da har yanzu baida tabbacin zata yi hakuri ta bar cikin jikinta ga wannan auren da baisan yadda zaiyi da Shi ba,
  Domin dai yasan ko ya sha giyar wake ne babu yadda zaayi ya gayá ma mujaheeda ya Kara aure da ga kawai ya zo yin aiki Durmi.
    Yayi Imani duk bayanin da zai Mata ba zata taba gamsuwa ba shin yaya zaiyi?, Ko guduwa zaiyi? To idan na ya gudu wadannan fitinannun mutanen kauyen ai sai sun Nemo Shi.
   Ya rasa dalilin da yasa Kuma can kasan zuciyarsa yake Jin damuwar ciwon da muhseena ta ke yi. Sai dai duk da haka baya son ganinta.

   Bai koma asibitin ba har washegari ana Shirin sallamarta ya fado asibitin ,lokacin Mallam Umar Yana ta kokarin bada kudin asibitin acikin irin kudin sadakin da Taufiq ya bayar.
   Amma sai Taufiq ya dakatar da Shi yace zai je ya biya, Ido kawai ya bishi da Shi kamar yadda Hajara da muhseena suka bishi da Ido har ya bace ma ganinsu.
   Hajara ta dawo da kallonta ga Mallam Umar tace " shine Mijin nata",
  Ya gyada Kai ahankali alamar eh, ta aje numfashi, aranta tasan baida wani makusa Amma waye Shi? Sannan wace irin rayuwa zata yi tare da shi?, Shin yarta zata samu farinciki atare da Shi?.
Tunanin hakan yasa Hajara faduwar gaba me tsanani.ita Kam muhseena tana runtse da Ido ita fa hatta tunani ma ta rasa ta kasa gane ta ina zata Fara duk karfin halinta ji tayi tana neman ta sare.
   Bayan ya dawo daga biyan kudin asibitin, hakanan ya ji bazai iya tafiya ba har sai ya maida Su gida. Cikin natsuwa ya gaida Hajara Mallam Umar ya dube Shi yace "Itace mahaifiyar muhseena".
  Dama yaga alama domin tashin hankalin da ke Kan fuskarta sai Uwa.
  Har ya dauke su zuwa gida bai ko kalli inda muhseena take ba ballefa ya tambayeta jikinta.

Yayi nufin Yana ajesu ya tafi Amma sai Mallam Umar ya nemi alfarmar ya shigo Yana son yin magana da Shi.
 Kwarjinin da Mallam Umar yayi masa ne yasa ya kasa yi masa musu ya bishi zuwa cikin gidan.

Suna shiga Ande Dije ta dubi Taufiq Yana kokarin gaisheta ita Kuma tana sakar masa tambayar "Kaine Mijin muhseenar?",
   Ya kasa bata amsa domin baisan abinda zaice mata ba.

Mallam Umar ne ya amsa da "shine".
  Kafin ta Kara wata magana yayi masa umarnin shiga dakin. Adole ya shiga Hajara ta shimfida masa tabarma ta koma Kan katifar da muhseena take kwance ya zauna cike da damuwa da fargaba.

Mallam Umar ya zauna a gefen tabarmar da Taufiq ke Kai.sun Dan Jima babu Wanda ya iya yin magana kafin daga bisani Mallam Umar ya Dan gyara zamansa yace "Taufiq da farko Zan Fara da Baka hakuri Akan duk abinda ya faru, domin idan kowa bai yarda da Ku ba to ni na yarda da Ku, duk da Kai ban San Ka ba haduwa ce  kawai mu kayi na Dan taki to na fahimci abubuwa da dama a game da Kai ciki kuwa harda cewa ba zaka iya aikata abinda ake zarginka da aikatawa ba".
   Sosai zuciyar Taufiq take yin fari idan ya ji irin wadannan kalaman daga bakin Mallam Umar.

Malllam Umar ya cigaba da cewa "ita ko yata nasan wacece ita nasan irin tarbiyyar da nayi Mata don haka ko ita da kanta ta Bude Baki ta gaya min ta aikata wannan aikin Zan karyatata, kawai dai komai da sila don haka Allah ya kawo wannan amatsayin sila, saboda Dama can Allah ya Riga ya rubuta muhseena acikin Jerin Matan da zaka aura a duniya, duk da nasan ba ayi maka adalci ba".
  Ya dubi Taufiq sosai yace " Amma don Allah ina neman taimako da alfarma awajenka".
  Taufiq akaro na farko ya dago ya dube Shi yace "Wace irin alfarma ".
    Mallam Umar yace "don Allah Kada Ka wulakanta mun rayuwar ya ta hanyar sakinta ko guduwa Ka barta, idan kayi min haka Ka kashe ni Ka sani ita kadai ce sanyin idaniyata Bana fatan wani mummunan lamari ya faru da ita, akwai dalilai da yawa da yasa muhseena take bukatar kulawa, kamar yadda akwai abubuwa da yawa da ya kamata Ka sani a game da ita Wanda sai lokaci ya yi sannan zaka sani, yanzu dai wannan alfarmar  nake so kayi min Ka taimaka min".
   Ya karasa magana cikin kaskantar da Kai.

Maganganun Mallam Umar sun taba zuciyar Taufiq sune Kuma dalilin da yasa ya ji bazai iya juyama rokon Mallam Umar baya ba.

Duk da Yana cikin damuwa to fa ya hango tsantsar tashin hankali da fargaba a cikin kwayar idon mallam Umar.
   Ya numfasa sannan yace "in sha Allah duk abinda Ka fada bazan aikata daya daga cikinsu ba, bazan saketa ba Kuma bazan gudu na barta ba".
  Hajara bata San lokacin da ta ce "Allah yayi maka albarka ".

Ita kanta muhseenan wannan Karon tsayawa tayi tayi masa kallon tsaf. Sannan ta maida kanta ta kwantar.
Mallam Umar yace " Nagode Taufiq Allah yayi maka albarka".
  Yace "Zan tafi" Mallam Umar yace " yi hakuri, ina son nasan wanene Kai ko ya ya ne ina son nasan wani abu daga tarihin rayuwarka".
   Ahankali Taufiq ya gayama Mallam Umar abinda zai iya gaya masa daga cikin rayuwarsa.
  Abu daya dai Mallam Umar ya yarda da Shi Taufiq ya fito daga kyakkyawar zuri'a shin. Yaya makomar muhseena zai kasance alokacin da yasan ainihin wacece muhseena.
 Sosai ya ke Jin Bai kamata ya gayá mishi komai ba har sai an fita daga wannan sarkakiyar.

Daga bisani dai Taufiq yayi musu sallama ya fice ba tare da yabi ta Kan muhseena ba.

Yana fita Ande Dije ta shigo dakin tana kallonsu tace "To wannan yaro dai da ganinsa na Dan gidan kananan mutane bane don haka wannan karfa karfa da akayi masa nasan iyayensa ba zasu taba yarda ba balle Kuma su San muhseena bata da uba to fa nayi Imani ko sun karbeta sai sun rabata da dansu, sannan yaya zatayi Zama da kishiyarta tunda ance Yana da Mata, na rantse muhseena ta debo ruwan dafa kanta".
  Mallam Umar yace " Don Allah Ande kiyi hakuri ki barmu haka, mu ji da abinda ke damunmu na roke ki Kada ki Kara mana damuwa akan wacce Muke da ita".

  Ta tabe Baki tace " umm gaskiya ce dai bakwa so Kuma ita Zan cigaba da gaya muku".

Babu Wanda ya Kara tankawa hakan yasa ta fice tana maganganu.
  Sai alokacin Hajara ta dago ta dubi Mallam Umar tace cikin sarkakiyar murya " Mallam Taufiq dan jigawa ne,Bana son muhseena ta yi rayuwa a wañnan garin shin wace kaddara ce kuma take bin muhseena".
Ya fada hawaye na tsiyaya akuncinta, kamar yadda itama muhseena ta Kara fashewa da kuka ko a mafarki bata son ta ji an ambaci jigawa.

Mallam Umar ya saki ajiyar zuciya ya dubi Hajara yace "ki fauwalama Allah komai domin jikina yayi sanyi Allah kadai yasan dalilin hada auren Taufiq da muhseena ".
 TO BE CONTINUE

DAGA TASKAR YAR GIDAN IMAM

NI MA MATARSA CE
            NA
HABIBA ABUBAKAR IMAM
  ( YAR GIDAN IMAM )
BABI  NA ARBA'IN DA BAKWAI
           *********
   Taufiq ya rasa inda zaisa Kansa ya jidadi, kamar yadda baya iya bacci baya iya sakin fuskarsa.
   Kallo daya zakayi masa Ka tabbatar Yana cikin matsananciyar damuwa tare da tashin hankali. Gaba daya zaman kauyen ya dame Shi.

       Ko waya yake yi da mujaheeda gaba daya sai yaji ya daburce ya rasa abinda zai ce.
 Hakan yasa har fargabar yin waya da ita yake .

   Haka zalika zuwa lokacin babu Wanda baisan abinda ya faru ba a kauyen.

  Surutu Kala Kala mara dadi.

Kowa yake yi har tsohuwar maganar rayuwar Hajara sai da ta dawo sabo a kauyen ,gaba daya sun rasa inda zasu sa kansu . Musamman Mallam Umar da yake fita kullum. Domin dai tuni muhseena ta daina zuwa ko da islamiyya, Ga ciwo da ya sata a gaba duk ta rame ta kanjame saboda damuwa.

Ko Taufiq yayi niyyar ba zai je ya dubata ba, to fa yakan rasa dalilin da yakansa ya ji ya kasa hakuri, dole sai ya je ya duba yanayin jikinta.
   Duk da ko ya je din babu wata magana da take hadasu da ya wuce ya jiki tace da sauki, daga nan basa Kara tankama juna har ya tafi.

Sai dai Yana kokarin biya duk wani kudin maganin da muhseena take sha.

Badon megari yasa asa masa Ido ba, a'a sai don kawai yayi ra'ayi , Kuma hakanan yake matukar Jin tausayin mahaifiyarta saboda wani irin kallo da akullum take masa idan yaje me kunshe da sakonni Kala Kala.

Wannan aure ne me cike da al'ajabi.

Taufiq ya rasa ya zaiyi ranar da duk ya tashi tafiya, shin zai gudu ne ko zai tafi da ita?.
   Wannan tambayar da ita yake kwana yake tashi a ransa, Amma har yanzu ya rasa amsa.
   Ta bangaren muhseena dai ta riga ta sallama rayuwarta ta yarda duk abinda ya faru da ita daga Allah ne.
    Sai dai hakan bai hana
 
ta yima kanta fatan mutuwa ba kullum, domin a yanzu gaba daya bata son rayuwar gara ta mutu ta huta mahaifanta ma su huta da bakinciki ta dalilinta kullum.

Duk da tun faruwar al'amarin kullum Mallam Umar da Hajara suna Kan kwantar Mata da hankali, to fa ta kasa Samun nutsuwa tunda Suma Basu da nutsuwar balle kwanciyar hankali.

Sosai Hajara ta Kara ramewa, saboda damuwa, hatta Ande Dije duk mugun halinta wannan Karon ta yi shuru da bakinta daga furta munanan kalaman da ta Saba, masu Kara Haddasa damuwa a zuciyoyinsu.

    Zukata dai duk sun Baci rayuwa tayi tsauri a wajen muhseena da Taufiq.Tun bayan da muhseena ta warware gaba daya sai Taufiq ya dauke kafafunsa a gidan. 

Sam hakan Bai damu muhseena ba , Amma ya damu iyayenta. Wannan Karon sosai Mallam Umar yasa Ido akan Taufiq domin ji yake tamkar bazai rike alkawarin da yayi masa ba, Yana ganin kamar guduwa zaiyi ya bar muhseena Wanda yin hakan shine zai Kara kassara rayuwar yarsa Kuma bazai taba barin hakan ya kasance ba.

   Watanni uku cif Taufiq yayi a garin Durmi, akayi canjin ma'aikata domin an Gama da hanyar Durmi za"a cigaba da yin  wata hanyar.

Alokacin fargabar Taufiq tafi ta kullum bacci ya gagare shi.

Shin yaya zaiyi da muhseena? Tambayar da yayima salisu Kenan.

Salisu ya numfasa Yana kokarin karasa shirya jakarsa,yace " To yanzu dai yadda mutanen kauyen nan suka sa maka Ido babu yadda zaayi Ka hudu kabar kauyen nan ba tare da muhseena ba, abinda Kamar wuya".

Taufiq ya dafe Kansa da yake Sarawa yace " Amma kasan babu yadda zaayi naje gida da ita amatsayin matata ko, domin yin hakan tamkar lasisin rabuwa da matata ne, Kai ni bansan ma yadda zanyi ba".
   Salisu yace " kasani ne, Ka tafi da ita bayan kunje kasan yadda zaayi Ka Sami wani waje da zaka ajeta, daga bisani kawai Ka saketa kaga batasan gidan Ka ba balle bayan kun rabu ta dawo kauyensu ace zaa biyo Ka inda kake aci mutuncinka, ni shawarata Kenan, domin dai kasan babu ta yadda zaka iya barinta a garin nan".

Sosai shawarar salisu tayima Taufiq.

Ita Kuma ya dauka, zai nemi inda zai aje ta yadda babu Wanda zaisan abinda ke tsakaninsu. Domin kuwa Shi Kansa salisun sai da ya gargade shi akan yabar maganar nan anan Kada su je da ita ko da office, Suma sauran abokan aikin nasa duk sai da ya ja kunnansu.

   Amma saboda tsabar wukakanci Taufiq bai sanar da muhseena da mahaifanta batun tafiyarsu jigawa ba sai Daren da gobe zai tafi, sannan ya je gidan.

Yayi sa'ar Samun Mallam Umar a kofar gida Yana shirin shiga gida.Taufiq ya same Shi da maganar tafiyarsu gobe. 

Ko gidan bai shiga ba bayan daya gaya masa ya juya yayi tafiyarsa.
  A sanyaye Mallam Umar ya shiga gidan tun da ya zauna akan tabarma a waje ya kasa tabuka komai da ya wuce tagumi hatta tuwon darensa da Hajara ta aje masa ya kasa budewa balle ya ci. Damuwa ce fal a zuciyarsa da fuskarsa.
    Hajara ta zauna a gefensa gami da Karo hasken fitilar Kwan da ke aje asaman tabarmar.
    Sannan ta dube Shi tace " Mallam me ke faruwa ne naga duk ka canja ne". Ya dan numfasa sannan yace "Ina muhseena ", tace "Tana daki tana karatu" yace "Kira min ita".

A hankali ta Mike ta shiga daki ta kirata, muhseena ta rufe kur'anin cikin nutsuwa ta tashi taje ta aje a maajiyarsa, sannan ta fito. 
      Itama gefen tabarmar ta zauna, tace "Gani Baba".
    Ya dubeta damuwarsa ta Kara yawa yace "mijinki yazo yanzun nan kafin na shigo gida, ya sanar da ni wa'adin aikinsa ya kare a garin gobe kuma zai koma don haka gobe zaku wuce tare zuwa jigawa".

Dam! Gaban Hajara da muhseena ya Fadi nan da nan suka daburce.
   Hajara ta dube Shi Ido jajir tace "Yanzu Mallam goben nan muhseena zata barmu, babu wani shiri, zata mu barta ta tafi da mutumin da bamu San waye ba baya ga sanar da mu tarihinta bamu San komai ba akan zuriarsu".
   Hawaye ya Fara zuba a kuncin muhseena ta Dora kanta a Saman cinyoyinta tana Jin tamkar zuciyarta zata fashe.
    Mallam Umar yace "To yanzu me ye abin yi?Hajara barin yaron nan ya tafi ba tare da muhseena ba babban gangancine a garemu domin Yana iya idan ya tafi ya ki dawowa duk da ina kyautata masa zato, Kuma Karin wasu surutan zamu samu a kauyen nan da duk yanzu Ido ya Kara dawowa kanmu".
  Hajara tace "To yanzu meye abin yi Mallam".
   Ya nisa yace "abin yin Kenan muyi hakuri mu barshi ya tafi da ita, zamu karbi nambar wayarsa idan komai ya lafa daga bangarensu zamu neme Shi mu je mu ga inda yar mu take, zuwa lokacin in sha Allah ubangiji ya hore min abinda zanyi Mata kayan daki, sai Kuma mu cigaba da rokon Mata Samun nutsuwa da kwanciyar hankali a duk inda take".

Ande Dije ta amshe maganar fitowarta daga daki Kenan.
   Tace "Kai dai wahala ta ganka ummaru' daukar matsalar wani ta Zama taka, tunda Ka auri Hajara Baka da wata matsala arayuwa sai tata Allah dai ya Baka lada".
   Bai tanka ba illa kallonta da yayi yace "Gobe idan Allah ya kaimu muhseena zata wuce gidan mijinta tare da Taufiq ".
   Ande Dije tace "Tikisa to Allah yasa su karbeta ".
    Mallam Umar yace "In sha Allah zasu karbeta".
  Bata Kara magana ba ta dau butarta ta shige daki ta kullo kofa.
    Mallam Umar ya dubi muhseena da take ta aikin sharar hawaye, tausayinta ya Kara kama Shi ya zuba Mata Ido yace "Allah ya sani muhseena da ina da ikon canja kaddararki da tuni na canja saboda duk duniya Bani da wani abun so da kauna sama da ke, duk wadannan abubuwan da suka faru kudura ce da ga cikin kudurorin Allah babu Wanda ya isa ya canja abinda ya riga ya rubuta acikin allon kaddararki".

Yadan gyara zamansa ya cigaba da cewa "Kiyi hakuri ki bi mijinki Ku tafi ki yi ta addua ki maida dukkan lamarinki ga Allah Shi zai isar Miki daga dukkan komai, kamar yadda muma zamu cigaba da yi Miki addua za Kuma mu kasance da ke ko da bakya tare da mu".
   Kuka me karfi ya Kara kwacema  muhseena duk Mai tausayi sai ya ji tausayin yanayin data fada ciki.
   Nan da nan itama Hajara ta shiga kuka Mai tsanani.
   Mallam Umar ya koma dukkan su Yana basu hakuri.

    A wañnan Daren dai yadda muhseena da Hajara suka ga Rana haka suka ga dare, saboda tsabar tashin hankali da damuwa.

Amma tun daren Hajara ta hadama muhseena duk kayan sawanta da Wanda zata bukata sai da ta cika karuwar jaka.

Washegari damuwar muhseena tafi ta kullum , don saboda kuka daker idanunta suke buduwa.
   Da wuri Taufiq ya zo, Abu daya ya Kara Sagar da guiwarsa shine irin maganganun da Mallam Umar yayi masa musamman akan ya rike amanar muhseena bata da kowa a inda zasu sai Shi don haka ya Zama dole ya Kula da rayuwarta .  Karo na farko kenan da kalaman Mallam Umar suka cire masa tunanin sakin muhseena bayan sun je jigawa domin irin alkawarin da Mallam Umar yasa ya daukar masa Abu ne mawuyaci ya iya karya wannan alkawarin.
   Haka zalika Basu tafi ba sai da Mallam Umar ya karbi nambar Taufiq. Iya daurewarsa Kuma sai da ya zubar da hawaye alokacin da muhseena suka tafi.

Itama tayi kuka kamar ranta zai fita.saida dogaran megari suka tabbatar da tafiyar Taufiq da muhseena sannan suka koma fada suka sanar da megari.

Shi Kam Mallam Umar dakalin kofar gidan sa ya cigaba da Zama Yana sharar hawaye tare da yi ma yarsa fatan alheri a duk inda take.
  TO BE CONTINUE

DAGA TASKAR YAR GIDAN IMAM

NI MA MATARSA CE
              48

     Tunda suka Fara tafiya muhseena take kuka a hankali me ratsa zuciya.
    Tun Taufiq Yana jurewa har ya kasa domin hakanan yake Jin kukan nata har cikin kwakon zuciyarsa, duk tsigar jikinsa sai tashi ta ke yi.
   Cikin wata irin bakuwar murya ya ce "Meye amfanin wannan kukan, ko kina ganin akwai maganin da kukan zaiyi Miki ne?".
   Bata tanka ba ya cigaba da cewa "Ko kina ganin Zan cutar da ke ne".
  Tayi kokarin dakatar da kukan da take yi amma hawayenta bai daina zuba ba.ya gyada Kansa yace "Nasan bakya Sona auren dole akayi mana duka" ta dube Shi sosai hakanan ta ji bakinta ya furta "Kaima Baka Sona ko?". Amsar tayi masa tsaurin fada domin Shi Kansa baisan amsar ba. Don haka yace "Na Sani daga ni har ke babu Mai laifi illa kaddara data fada mana Wanda babu yadda zamu yi".

Ya Kara numfasawa damuwa mara misaltuwa ce a fuskarsa.
    Ya cigaba da cewa "ina da Mata tana da ciki ahalin yanzu babu yadda zaayi na iya zuwa Kai tsaye na gaya Mata cewa nayi aure domin bansan hslin da zata sa kanta ba, domin duk duniya matata babu abinda ta tsana sama da kishiya".
  Hawayen muhseena ya tsaya Karo na farko kenan data zubama kyakkyawar fuskar Taufiq Ido tana Jin wani bakon al'amari da yake shiga dukkan sassan jikinta.

Taufiq yace "Dalilin hakanne yasa nayi Mata alkawarin bazan taba yi Mata kishiya ba". Gaban muhseena yayi mummunan faduwa shin wace irin rayuwa zatayi da mutumin da bata Raba daya biyu gidansa ya fi karfinsa.
  A hankali tace "To yanzu ya kake son ayi, me yasa ba zaka sake ni ba Ka maida ni wajen iyaye na".
   Ya girgiza Kai yace "Bazan sake ki ba," "Saboda mene?",
Ta tambaya ya Dan waiwaya ya dubeta sosai yace "Saboda nayi ma mahaifinki alkawarin bazan taba sakinki ba" tace "Idan hakane to yaya makomar rayuwata take a hannunka".
   
Ya yi shuru na wasu dakikai sannan yace "Har yanzu ban tsara komai akan hakan ba, shiyasa nake son ki taimake ni ki Bani hadin Kai, Zan nemi inda Zan ajeki idan mun isa jigawa ahankali idan na gama warware matsalar sai na dawo da ke gidana".
   Tace "Zuwa tsawon wane lokaci Kenan".
  Ya make kafada yace "Ni Kaina ban sani ba,".
Gaba daya su kayi shuru kowa da abinda yake sakawa aransa.
  Ita Kam muhseena ta rasa irin tunanin da zatayi hakika ta riga ta Fara saduda da Samun rayuwar farinciki shin wace irin rayuwa ce wannan tana matsayin MATARSA amma babu damar ya nunata.
   Karo da dama Kenan da tun faruwar al'amarin Kabir ya Fado Mata arai.
Da Shi ta aura da tasan tayi aure a duniya ràshin Kabir ya dawo Mata sabo . Hakika iyayen Kabir sun taimaka wajen kassara rayuwarta domin da sun Bari ta auri dansu duk da ba taga wannan masifar ba.
   Amma ba zatayi fushi da yin Allah ba.
Domin tasan ko a dade ko ajima zai dubeta da idanun rahama.
   Ta hadiye dukkan damuwarta ta zaro Dan karamin kur'anin ta acikin jaka ta shiga karantawa a zuciyarta, hakanne kadai ke lafar da tashin hankalin da take ciki.

 Isar su jigawa Kai tsaye Taufiq wajen cin abinci ya aje muhseena Wanda daker ta Dan ci abincin shima din saboda ya matsa mata ne.
   Bayan fitowarsu daga wajen cin abinci ne ya nuna Mata Kan wani benci yace ta zauna ta Jira Shi anan .
   Yace zai je ya samo inda zai ajeta tukuna sannan ya zo ya dauke ta.
  Saboda yawo da ita acikin mota zai iya Zama hatsari a gare Shi domin wani Wanda ya Sani Yana iya ganinsu.

Har Taufiq ya tafi mamakin yadda wannan rayuwa zata kasance take yi Kai da mijinka amma Yana tsoron wani ya gansu tare.
  To wai ita a wane matsayi ma take agurinsa?. Ta yaya Kuma take son ta tabbatar da wannan auren da baida wani takamaiman suna ko asali.
   Taufiq ya Dan dauki lokaci da tafiya kafin muhseena ta Fara tunanin Anya kuwa Taufiq ba guduwa yayi ya barta ba. Hankalinta yayi mummunan tashi, ta Fara kokarin rasa tunaninta.
   Cikin dimbin tashin hankali da son gasgata tunaninta ta tashi tana kokarin Jan akwatinta .
   Lokaci daya ta Fara tunanin idan ba son ya gudu yake ba don me zai sauke Mata jakar kayanta?. Tabbas Taufiq bai ajeta anan don ya dawo ya dauketa ba. Shin ina zata sa kanta a babban gari irin wannan?.
   Wa zai taimaketa?. 
Hawaye ya Fara wanke Mata fuska tana Jan Jakarta tana sharar hawaye.
   Komai na duniyar ya tsaya Mata cak!. Dai dai lokacin da take kokarin tsallake titin ne. Cikin tsautsayi wata mota ta tunkarota duk iya yadda wacce ke tuka motar ta so ta tsaida motar Kada ta taba muhseena to fa abin ya ci tura domin kuwa sai da ta bugeta.
   Jama'ar wajen suka taso kowa Yana salati da kokarin taimakon muhseena wacce tuni ta sume.

Anyi sa'a wacce take tuka motar babu abinda ya sameta.
  Kamar yadda babu abinda ya Sami  motar. 
Don haka da taimakon mutanen wajen aka Samu akasa muhseena a motar matar da gaba daya ta Gama gigicewa.
   Kai tsaye wani babban asibitin kudi ta wuce da ita hankalinta a matukar tashe yake faranta daya  Allah yasa ba wani mummunan abu bane ya sameta.
   Tana isa asibitin aka amshi muhseena, nan da nan aka bata gado.
   Aka cigaba da bata taimakon gaggawa.
 Gefe daya wacce ta bugeta duk ta Kara rikicewa tana zaune Kan kujerar karfen asibitin tana kokarin Kira a wayarta.

Bugu daya acikin na biyu mahaifiyarta ta dauka.

Ta so suyi magana sosai Amma Jin muryarta cike da tsoro da fargaba ta tambayeta "yaya akayi? Kina Ina ne najiki cikin damuwa ne".
       Da sauri tace " ina nan zeenat hospital wata budurwa na buge a mota ina kokarin komawa gida bayan barina nan".
   Gaban ta ya Fadi tace "Subhanallah Allah yasa babu wani mummunan abu da ya same ta, me likitan yace".
   Ta numfasa tace "Baice komai ba suna can tare da ita, amma dai a sume Muka taho asibitin da ita".
   Mahaifiyarta ta numfasa tace "ina fatan dai ke babu abinda ya sameki ".
   Ta girgiza Kai tace "Babu abinda ya same ni" tace "To gani nan zuwa asibitin".
    Haka muhseena ta yi ta Kai gauro ta Kai Mari a harabar wajen.
   Ta Kara daga waya ta Kira me aikinta ta sanar da ita ,
    Tayi duk abinda ya dace,
Na game da abinda megidan zai ci, domin Yana hanyar dawowa . Cike da girmamawa ta amsa.
    Tana aje wayar likita na fitowa.
Da sauri tayi Kansa tana tambayarsa yanayin jikin muhseena.
   Likitan ya tabbatar Mata babu karaya amma akwai buguwa a kafa da gefen kafadarta sai Yan kananan ciwurwuka, da dinki da sukayi Mata a gefen kafarta ta hagu.

Ta lumshe Ido cike da godiya ga  Allah , sannan tace " sumewar da tayi fa".
   Yadan dubeta yace fargaba ce fa da tsorata da tayi Amma ko yanzu ta dawo hayyacinta kina iya shiga ki ganta".

Tayi masa godiya ya wuce, itama tana Shirin shiga dakin.
 Muryar mahaifiyarta ta tsaidata.
   Ta juyo da sauri ta wuce ta rungumeta cike da sangarta.
 .ita Kuma tana shafa bayanta cikin so da kulawa.
   Duka tare suka shige dakin.
Kai tsaye suna shiga ta wuce Kan gadon da muhseena ke kwance.
   Tana kallonta cike da tausayi tana yi Mata sannu.
  Muhseena cikin jigata ta amsa, lokaci daya ta shiga bata hakuri.
   Da sauri Karo na farko da mahaifiyarta tayi magana tun shigowarsu dakin.
   "Me yasa kike bata hakuri haka, bayan kinyi dukkan kokari wajen dakkota ki kawota asibiti bayan ba kowane mutum bane yake tsayawa idan ya buge wani ".
    Ta dubi mahaifiyarta tace "Don Allah ki daina wannan maganar".
  Muhseena ta daga Ido sosai tana Karema fuskar ta kallo sosai take hango ràshin tausayi da wata bakuwar tsana acikin idanun matar, to dalili da me?.
   Ta tambayi kanta gami da runtse idanunta.
   Ta Kara matsowa kusa da ita.
Tace "kina bukatar wani abu ne", ta girgiza Kai alamar aah .
   Ta Kara kallonta tace "Ko zaki iya bada nambar da zaa Kira wani naki, saboda gidanku su San abinda ya faru da ke".

Gaban muhseena ya Fadi , halin da take ciki ya fado mata.
   A sanyaye tace "bansan kowa ba anan garin Kuma Bani da wani da Zan Baki number sa ki Kira, Bani da kowa".

Ta Karasa magana cikin zubda hawaye.
  Wani irin tausayinta ya ratsa zuciyarta ta Kura Mata Ido.
 Tana hango tsantsar damuwar da ke ciki Wanda hakane ya bata damar gasgata abinda bakin muhseena ya furta.

Mahaifiyarta ta saki tsaki tace "wane irin Baki da kowa?, Kinga ki dawo hayyacin.....?.
   Da sauri ta dakatar da ita tace "don Allah ki kyaleta ta ji da abinda take ji, koma dai menene idan ta Sami lafiya sai muyi maganar".

Ta dubeta tace "Zan tafi gida zuwa anjima Zan dawo in sha Allah kinji ko". Muhseena ta gyada Kai ahankali Kuma tace "Nagode ".
   Daganan suka fice Duka , mahaifiyarta na korafin yadda take son ta zubar da kimarta ga yarinyar da daga ganinta bata da wani matsayi.
  Sam ita Kuma taki yarda da abinda take fada karshema cewa tayi abar maganar.
  Direban ta ya dauketa zuwa gida. Ita Kuma ta shiga motar ta ja zuwa gida, zuciyarta cike da godiya ga Allah da abin ya tsaya anan.
  Amma duk da haka ta rasa dalilin da yasa ta ji zuciyarta ta cika da tausayin budurwar kwarai da gaske, gashi ta yi ragon azanci ko sunanta bata tambaya ba , sai dai idan ta koma.
   Tana ganin mugun damuwa da tashin hankali acikin idonta, tabbas akwai wani boyayyen al'amari atare da ita.

    Tun komawar Taufiq inda ya bar muhseena bai ganta ba.
   Ya shiga mugun tashin hankali mara misaltuwa duk inda zai duba awajen ya duba amma bai ganta ba.
   Gashi wadanda suka San anyi hatsari awajen kusan duk sun bar wajen balle Su gaya masa akwai hatsarin da akayi ko zai bincika kila itace wacce yake nema.
   Haka yayi ta bulayi cike da tashin hankali Yana cigiyar muhseena amma bai dace da wani ya ganeta ba.

Babu ta inda zufa bata karyo masa. Maganganun Mallam Umar sune suke tayi masa yawo acikin kunnansa," muhseena itace sanyin idaniyata Bani da wata damuwa sama da damuwarta babu wata alfarma da zaka min aduniya sama daka Kula min da ita".

   Taufiq baisan akwai wani boyayyen al'amari a zuciyarsa game da muhseena sai yanzu .
   Domin damuwar batanta tayi mugun taba shi.
    Shi Kansa baisan iya lokacin da ya dauka Yana binciken inda muhseena ta shiga ba.
   Kuran mujaheeda kuwa bazai kirgu ba tana son ta ji abinda ya tsaida Shi har yanzu bai karaso gida ba.
   Dole karyar motarsa ta lalace yayi Mata.
 
Har kusan magriba babu labarin muhseena, ga gajiya ga yunwa ga damuwa .
   Duk su suka taru suka maida Taufiq tamkar mahaukaci.
    Dole ya hakura ya kama hanyar gida Wanda kallo daya zaka yi masa kasan Yana cikin mugun yanayi.

Yana shiga gida ana Kiran sallar magriba.
    Ganin mujaheeda da alamun cikin jikinta ya Fara Dan fitowa, shine kawai ya lafar da tashin hankalin da yake ciki.
   Duk inda ta juya ita yake kallo ta Kara kyau da cika fatarta ta Kara gogewa.
  Haka zalika irin tarbar da ya Samu, Yana son ya tabbatar masa da cewa mujaheeda ta saduda ta amince zata haifar masa abinda ke cikin ta .

     A daddafe Taufiq ya samu yayi wanka, Amma baya minti biyu tunanin. Inda muhseena ta shiga bai fado masa arai ba.

Ita kanta mujaheeda ta Lura akwai damuwa atare da Shi.

Ya Fara cin abinci Kenan , mujaheeda ta Kara zuba masa Ido tace "Naga duk Ka rame ne, kasha aiki sosai ko wata uku fa".
   Yadanyi murmushi yace "AI dole na rame, tunda Baki kusa da ni da dai ina tare da ke nasan babu abinda zai ramar da ni".
   Ta jidadin maganarsa tace " hmmm ai bansan na shaku da Kai ba, sai bayan tafiyarka ni kadai nasan yanayin da na fada".

   Taufiq ya zuba Mata Ido yace " kin Kara kyau, kin cika da yawa, wai kinga yadda cikin nan yayi matukar karbarki kuwa".
  Tayi dariya tace "Kai yaya Taufiq, kasan wai harya Fara motsi".

Ya saki murmushi yace "Masha Allah, ina cikin Maza masu saa arayuwa".
  Ta danyi kasa da kanta cikin sanyin murya tace "Shi yasa nake son na Baka hakuri Akan yunkurin da nayi wajen son zubar da cikin , don Allah kayi hakuri Ka manta".

Cike da farinciki da jindadi ya aje cokalin abincin hsnnunsa yace "Dama yarinta ce ke damunki, domin idan ba yarinta ba babu yadda zaayi Allah ya Baki irin wannan kyautar sannan kice bakya so, don haka komai ya wuce Allah ya saukeki lafiya ".

Tace "Amin".
   Sai bayan da ya Gama cin abinci suka koma falo. 
  Mujaheeda ta dube Shi sannan tace "Amma akwai damuwa a tare da Kai ".
  Ya yi saurin girgiza Kai yace "damuwa ko gajiya?"
    Tace "to gashi nan. Dai"
Bai Kara magana ba sai gabansa da ya cigaba da faduwa shin ina yarinyar mutane ta shiga? Tana hannun waye awannan daren?.

Tambayoyin da yake ta jera ma Kansa Kenan.
  Mujaheeda tace "yaya Taufiq dazu ina kokarin dawowa gida daga gidan mu, Allah ya hada ni da tsautsayi na buge wata budurwa".
  Hakanan Taufiq yaji gabansa yayi mummunan faduwa.
  Ya dubeta da sauri yace"innalillahi wainna'ilaihirrajiun, ba dai abinda ya sameta ko, tana ina yanzu?".

Tace "zeenat hospital na Kai ta, Alhamdulillah babu wani abin damuwa sosai,"
   Ta Kara kallonsa tace "yanzu ma nake son na koma na dubata na Kai Mata abinci".

Taufiq ya numfasa yace "Allah ya bata lafiya, ni dai na gaji sosai bazan iya zuwa asibiti yanzu ba ".
    Tace "gaskiya Ka gaji domin fuskarka da jikinka sun nuna hakan , Dama na Kira direban mummy bayan shugarka wanka har ya zo Kuma Yana waje Yana jirana".......

     Taufiq ya gyada Kai yace "To ai gara kiyi sauri ki je ki dawo, saboda dare Yana yi".
  Ya Kara kallonta sosai cikin Dan murmushi yace "Kuma kinfi kowa sanin nayi rashinki wata uku ba kwana uku bane,don haka duk lokacin nan ni yakamata aba".
   Tayi dariya sosai ta tashi tana fadin "yaya Taufiq Allah baka da dama".
    Nan da nan ta dakko gele sannan ta umarci daya daga cikin hadimanta biyu da suka rage, domin dayar ta koma garinsu anyi Mata aure.
    Akan ta zo su tafi tare saboda ta barta acan ta cigaba da Kula da muhseena.

Uwale babu musu ta shiryo gami da daukar kwandon kayan abinci suka tafi.
    Fitar su, mujaheeda tasa direban ya tsaya a super market tayima muhseena sayayyar kayan shayi da sauran Dan tarkacen kayan bukata.

Alhamdulillah sun tadda jikin muhseena ya fi dazu Jin sauki sosai.
   Don ayanzu ma tana jingine a jikin gadon .
   Mujaheeda ta janyo kujera ta zauna tana fadin "aah masha Allah jiki ya Fara sauki, sannu".
  Cikin murmushi ta amsa
Yauwa sannu da zuwa.
   Mujaheeda ta umarci uwale data zuba Mata abinci ta ci.
   Sosai muhseena take Jin yunwa don haka abincin da uwale tasa Mata ta so ta ci sosai .
Amma ràshin dadin zuciya da damuwa ya hanata cin abincin yadda ya kamata.

Mujaheeda ta dubeta cike da kulawa tace "ki ci abincin mana".
  Ta girgiza Kai tace 
"Ya ishe ni nagode da dawainiya".
 
Mujaheeda ta dubeta sosai tace "ya sunanki ne".
   Tace "sunana Zainab".
Ta gyada Kai tace "sannu kinji, ga uwale nan zata zauna da ke har zuwa lokacin da zaa sallameki".
Ta Dan dubi uwale tace "to nagode sosai".
  Zatayi magana kenan wayarta ta dauki Kara tana dubawa taga Haj murjanatu ce.
  Ta dauka "hello mummy".
   Daga daya bangaren Haj murjanatu tace "kin tafi gida ko kina asibitin".
Tace "ina asibiti ".
  Ta saki numfashi tace
"Wai ke me ke damunki ne,don me zaki dinga wahalar da kanki a Kan wannan yarinyar wacce babu wani galihu agareta, da wannan Daren kina yawo a titi saboda ita, ko kin manta cikin da ke gareki ne,".
 Sam mujaheeda bata son muhseena ta gane fada ake Mata akanta don haka tayi saurin cewa " yanzun nan Zan koma gida".
   Bata saurari komai ba 
Ta kashe wayar don bata son maganar ta cigaba da nisa.
  Daganan tayi sallama da muhseena da uwale ta tafi. Wasu lokuta da dama tana ganin suna da bambancin ra'ayi tsakaninta da mahaifiyarta domin yadda take kyamatar talaka Sam ita bata Jin haka aranta.
 Asalima mugun tausayin sa take ji.
Tana Kuma son taimakonsa akowane hsli,
    Kamar yadda take Jin tausayin Zainab tare da son ta taimaka Mata. 
  
Tun fitar mujaheeda Taufiq ya kasa Jura, dole tasa shima ya fice.
  Duk da yasan neman muhseena a wañnan Daren abu ne me matukar wahala.
  Tofa ràshin kwanciyar hankalin da yake ciki yasa ya yanke shawarar fita nemanta.
   Duk yadda zaa kwatanta damuwar da Taufiq ya shiga to fa ba zaku gane ba.
   Shi Kansa matsalolin da ya hango suna da yawa, yanzu idan baiga muhseena ba ta yaya zai sanar da mahaifanta?.
   Yaya Kuma zaiyi da jamaar garin da yasan duk boyewarsa suna iya binciko Shi.
   Bayan ma haka ta yaya zai fitar da Kansa?.
Shin ina muhseena ta shiga?.
    Haka Taufiq ya yi ta yawo acikin wannan Daren.
  Amma Bai dace da ganin muhseena ba.
  A hankali ya tsaida motarsa ya zaro ways cikin dimbin tashin hankali ya danna ya Kira salisu.
   Wanda bai bata lokaci ba wajen daukar wayar.
"Ya akayi mutumina".
   Salisu ya fada daga daya bangaren.
Jin muryar Taufiq cikin tashin hankali yasa ya Sami nutsuwa ya cigaba da tambayar sa abinda ke faruwa.
  Sannu a hankali Taufiq ya gayá masa komai.
   Ya Kara da cewa 
"Yanzu ta ina Zan Fara, ina cikin muguwar damuwa Kuma bana son mujaheeda ta fahimci wani abu, babu inda ban duba ba salisu har na gaji shin ina yarinyar nan ta shiga?. Tana neman ta jefa ni cikin bala'i, sai da na gaya Mata ta tsaya inda na ajeta naje na dawo amma Ka duba fitinar data jefani ciki".

Salisu shima nan da nan ya gigice yace "to ina yarinyar nan ta shiga Allah dai yasa ba wata masifar ta fada ciki ba".
   Taufiq ya numfasa yace 
"Yanzu salisu ya zanyi?"
Salisu yace "hakuri zaka yi Ka koma gida kayi kokarin mallakar hankalinka ko don Kada matarka ta Fara zarginka idan Allah ya kaimu gobe sai muje mu Kai maganar wajen yansanda".
 Shawarar salisu tayi don haka suka aje magana akan gobe zasu je su sanar wajen Yansandan.
Daga bisani sukayi sallama sannan Taufiq ya kama hanyar gida.

Yayi matukar saa sai bayan daya koma gida tukuna sannan mujaheeda ta dawo.

  A daran ranar duk iya yadda ya so ya faranta ran mujaheeda ya nuna Mata lalle yayi kewarta. To fa hakan ya faskara saboda tashin hankalin da yake ciki ya fatattaki duk wani farinciki da karfin da ke jikinsa.

Kusan yadda ya ga dare haka ya ga rana.
Bai runtsa ba saboda alhini.
   Washegari duk sunyi Shirin tafiya wajen aiki.
Mujaheeda ta dauki abincin asibiti.
  Tare suka jero da Taufiq shine ma ya rike Mata kwandon abincin.
   Hakanan ya ji bakinsa ya furta "ya sunan wacce kika buge din".
  Tadan dube Shi , tana kokarin shiga mota.
Sannan tace "sunanta Zainab".
   Ya Dan yi Jim yace "Amma ya jikin nata".
 Tace "akwai sauki sosai gaskiya, don jiyan ma da na koma a jingine na tadda ita, Kuma ta Dan ci abincin da na tafi dashi".

Yace "idan na tashi office da wuri, zanje na dubata".
A haka suka rabu.

Yana fita ya dauki salisu Kai tsaye ofishin yansanda mafi kusa suka wuce.
   Sun shigar da komai an rubuta ,
Insifektan wajen Kuma yayi alkawarin bada dukkan gudunmawar sa.
Yadda Taufiq ya zauna Yana zayyana siffofinta, ba karamin mamaki yaba salisu ba.
  Sai bayan barinsu ofishin Yansandan ne.
Sannan salisu ya dubi Taufiq sosai yace"Amma nayi mamakin yadda kake siffanta muhseena babu ko gargada gaba daya babu abinda Baka fada nata ba".
  Yadan aje numfashi ahankali yace "ni Kaina nayi mamakin hakan".
Salisu ya Dade Yana kallon fuskarsa Yana kokarin gano abinda yake son ya gane.
  Amma har suka rabu Bai samu ya gano hasashen da yake yi ba.
 Taufiq Yana Kan hanyar zuwa office ne wayarsa ta dinga Kara tana neman agajinsa.
   Dole tasa ya janyo wayar sannan ahankali ya sauka daga Kan titi yayi parking din motar, sannan ya dauka cikin sallama.

    Daga can daya bangaren kauyen Durmi, Mallam Umar ya amsa da sauri "Taufiq Mallam ummaru ne mahaifin muhseena".
   Dam gabansa yayi mummunan faduwa nan da nan ya gigice.
   Bayan sun gama gaisawa ne yace"Tun jiya nake ta Kiran wayar taka amma bata shiga, ya ya kuka je gidan".
  Yace "Lafiya lau"
Yace "yaya muhseenan komai dai lafiya ko".
  Ya daure Yana kokarin boye tashin hankalin da yake ciki yace " komai lafiya lau, ina kokarin daidaita komai ne".

Yace To Allah yasa mu ji alheri, Ka bata wayar muyi magana ".

Yayi saurin cewa "Na Riga na fito Bana gida sai dai idan na koma Zan hadaku in sha Allah ".
  Yace "to shikenan nagode ".
 Daganan sukayi sallama.
Ya juya Yana kallon Hajara sannan yace "kina damuwa a banza ne, lafiyarta kalau yace ma ya idan ya koma gida zai hadamu a waya muyi magana ".
  Hajara ta numfasa tace "To ai gara dai da aka ji halin da ake ciki kaga ai hankali ya kwanta".

Gaba daya Kai Taufiq ya Dora ajikin sitiyarin  motar Yana maida wata irin ajiyar zuciya.
  Shi Kansa yasan akwai gagarumar matsala, Shin ina zaiga muhseena?.
  Zahiri Yana cikin tsaka mai wuya.

   Kwanaki uku cur!
Aka kwashe yansanda na neman muhseena amma babu ita babu Labarinta.
  Zuwa wannan lokacin kuwa damuwar Taufiq ta kasa buya domin sosai mujaheeda ta gane akwai abinda ke damunsa, Amma duk iya yadda zata shawo Kansa ya gaya Mata matsalar ya ki saboda baima yarda Yana da matsalar ba, yaki nuna Mata Amma ita ta riga ta Gama gane akwai damuwa, Amma tunda yaki yarda ya gaya Mata dole tasa ta hakura ta sa masa Ido.
  Hatta jamaar gidansu sun gane baya cikin kwanciyar hankali, Haj saratu itama tayi tayi ya gayá Mata amma ya tabbatar Mata babu komai.

    Muhseena ta Sami sauki sosai acikin wannan kwanakin, har tafiya ta nayi sannan da yawa daga cikin ciwurwukan jikinta sun Fara kamewa wajen dinki ne kawai yake Mata ciwo. Shima din makonta daya a asibitin aka kwance dinkin.

Muhseena ta Sami dukkan kulawa a wajen mujaheeda.
  Hakika idan haka ake Samun masu kudi to da an huta domin dai mujaheeda tana da halin karsmci matuka da gaske. Babu abinda ta nema ta rasa.
  Sai dai tsoro da fargaba da kullum yake hanata sakat saboda kullum tunaninta idan an sallameta ina zata?. Don kuwa ko kadan bata jin zata iya komawa Durmi ta Kara dorama iyayenta damuwa da tashin hankali.

Tabangaren Taufiq Kuma duk da kullum sai Mallam Umar ya Kira Shi da son ya hada Shi da mujaheeda, to fa kullum hanya hanya yake masa Yana Samun wata karyar da yake yi masa, Wanda Shi Kansa ya Fara sikewa tare da kokarin dorama Kansa tunanin Anya kuwa babu wani abu da Taufiq yake boye masa, musamman da akullum shima Hajara ke damunsa da son sanin halin da yarta take ciki.

 A yayinda Haj murjanatu ta sa kahon zuka agame da yadda mujaheeda ke kulawa da muhseena, domin acewarta ita Kam bata ga wani dalili da zaisa ta dinga bata lokacinta Akan yarinyar da bata da maraba da kaskantacciya.
   Anan mujaheeda ta ki saurarenta domin kullum tayi Mata irin wannan maganar tana maida Mata da cewa "yanzu mummy ya ya kike son nayi, Kada fa ki manta duk abinda ya faru da Zainab to nice sila kinga kuwa ta yaya Zan kyaleta a garin da take ikirarin bata da kowa Kuma bata San kowa ba".
  Kusan kullum sai sunyi Irin wannan maganar.
Kullum Kuma sau mujaheeda ta je ta dubata ahankali ta ji sabo da son muhseena suna neman su Sami gurbi acikin zuciyarta.

       Mujaheeda na zaune a gefen Taufiq. Tana matukar kokarin jansa da hira domin ràshin sakewarsa.
   Karfe biyar ne na yammacin ranar asabar.
Dai dai lokacin wayar mujaheeda ta dauki ruri, cikin ràshin karsashi ta dauki wayar, "uwale ya akayi".
    Ta tambaya, uwale ta gyara zamanta tace "Haj Dama an sallame mu ne yanzu, Kuma akwai kudi da zaa bada ragowar balance".
    Mujaheeda ta ce cike da jindadi.
 "Alhamdulillah, to Ku Jira gani nan tahowa yanzu". Ta aje wayar tana duban Taufiq.
   Ya rigata yin magana yace "me jinyar taki aka sallama".
  Tace "eh kaga dai har an sallameta baka Sami damar zuwa Ka dubata ba". A ransa yace ina Naga wannan lokacin ina cikin wannan masifa.
   Yace "To yau ai sai muje  na kaiki".
   Tayi Dan murmushi tace "dako Ka kyauta, don da ganin yarinyar tana cikin matsala Shi yasa kaga na dage wajen taimakonta".
 Ya zuba Mata Ido cike da son Karin bayani yace "kamar yaya tana cikin matsala".
  Tace "to ai Baka cikin halin da zamu zauna mu tattauna Shi yasa na bar maganar, kasan ta gaya min bata da kowa a garin nan asalima ko an sallame ta batasan inda zata je ba".

Da sauri ya ce "bata da kowa? To ai yakamata ki tashi muje saboda tunda an sallame ta ya kamata Kiji komai agame da ita"
  Nan da nan mujaheeda ta tashi ta hau shiri, zuwa lokacin kuwa hakurin Taufiq har ya kusa gazawa domin kuwa tunda ta ambaci yarinyar bata San kowa ba, yaji gaba daya tunaninsa ya karkata acan .
  Zuciyarsa na matukar gaya masa abinda tasan da wuya hakan ya kasance amma duk da haka ya kosa yaje ya ga yarinyar.musamman tadda gabansa yake matukar bugawa tunda tayi maganar.

        Garin babu yawan cunkoson ababan hawa, hakan ne yasa nan da nan Taufiq ya isa asibitin tare da mujaheeda.
    Kai tsaye dakin suka wuce, Taufiq ya rasa dalilin da yasa yaji bugun zuciyarsa ya tsananta.
   Cikin sallama suka shiga mujaheeda na gaba Taufiq Yana binta abaya.

Da sauri uwale ta tashi ta na yima uwar dakinta sannu da zuwa, tana gaida Taufiq.
     Mujaheeda ta dubi muhseena da take kwance "Ashe anyi sallama?".
   Cikin fara'a muhseena ta yunkura tana kokarin tashi zuwa lokacin kuwa gaba daya Taufiq idanunsa na dore akanta.
Tana dago kanta caraf suka hada Ido da Shi.
   Dam! Gaban Taufiq yayi mummunan faduwa nan da nan ya rikice.
  Itama muhseena cak! Komai ya tsaya Mata numfashinta ya dinga wuyar fita.

DAGA TASKAR YAR GIDAN IMAM
NI MA MATARSA CE
                  50

     Shin ina hajiya ta Samo Taufiq?, Shin tasan alakar da ke tsakaninsu ne?.
  Irin Jerin tambayoyin da muhseena take ma kanta kenan, ba tare da ta dauke idonta akansa ba. Shima saurin jingina yayi da jikin bangon dakin, saboda yadda ya ji jiri Yana neman kwasar sa, shin mafarki yake ko na Ido biyu, Dama muhseena itace Zainab din da mujaheeda ke fada?, Itace ta buge a mota duk tsawon wannan lokacin da yake cikin masifa da tashin hankali Dama maganin matsalar tana hannun mujaheeda.
   Lokaci daya Kuma gabansa ya cigaba da faduwa da karfi, saboda tsoro da fargabar Kada muhseena ta bude bakinta.

  Muryar mujaheeda ta katse shurun da akayi a dakin.
Tadan waiwayo tana duban Taufiq tace "yaya Taufiq ga Zainab kaga jikin da sauki sosai, tunda gashi har anyi sallama". Kokarin boye firgitar da yake ciki yake yi, ya gyada Kai yace cikin shakewar murya "eh gaskiya jiki yayi sauki duk da Banga yadda take ba alokacin da aka kawota asibitin".
   Kan muhseena Yana kasa magana take da zuciyarta sosai,
Tana kokarin Nemo amsoshin tambayoyinta.
  
   Dai dai lokacin kamar da ga sama muhseena ta ji mujaheeda tace "Zainab wannan shine mijina Taufiq ".

Cikin tsantsar kidima da saura kadan mujaheeda ta gane irin tashin hankalin data fada. Muhseena ta dago kanta nan da nan idanunta suka yi jajir.

     Ta shiga maimaita kalaman mujaheeda aranta " mijina". Ta shiga kokarin Nemo fuskar Taufiq ta kalla sosai ta tsaida idanunta akansa, irin yanayin da ta gan shi ciki ya tabbatar Mata da halin fargaba da firgici da yake ciki.
   Wanda hakanne ya tabbatar Mata da cewa, Taufiq baya da masaniyar itace matarsa ta buge a mota.

"Uwale ki harhada kayan ki Kai mota, zanje na karasa biyan kudin asibitin", cewar mujaheeda. Uwale ta amsa da toh, ita Kuma ta juya tace "yaya Taufiq muje na karasa biyan kudin asibitin". Cikin dakewa da kokarin boye duk wata gaskiya da ke shimfide a fuskarsa yace "aah , jeki kawai ki Gama ki dawo mu tafi".
  Ta jinjina Kai tace "to shikenan".
  Ta juya da sauri ta fice
Yayinda uwale ta ajema Taufiq kujera alamar ya zauna. Daganan ta hada kaya ta dauka ta Fara fita dasu.

Tana fita Taufiq yayi saurin dawo da kallonsa ga muhseena yace "me yasa kika ce sunanki Zainab".
   Ba tare data dube Shi ba tace "saboda shine asalin sunana, ko Ka Bani damar da zaka san wani abu ne agame da ni".
   Ya kauda maganar da cewa "me yasa kika bar inda na ajeki ba bayan na hanaki yin hakan, ko taurin kai Yana daya daga cikin abubuwan da ban sani ba acikin halinki?".
   Kanta na kasa tace 
"Nabar wajen ne saboda banyi zaton zaka dawo ba, na dauka Ka wofintar da nine ba tare da tausayi ba ko sanin makomata ga halin da Zan shiga agarin da bansan kowa ba ".

Ya nisa yace " me yasa Baki kyautata min zato ba, Kin San halinda kika jefani aciki kuwa na ràshin kwanciyar hankali, ko bacci Bana yi saboda túnanin halin da kike ciki da inda kika shiga".
  Sai alokacin ta dago da sauri suka hada idanu tace "yaushe harna Sami wannan matsayin awajenka, na dauka ai ni ba komai bace face lalura agareka".
  Ji yayi kalamanta suna sukar zuciyarsa Sam Sam bai jindadin yadda take maida masa magana.
  "Wannan CE matarka?"
Ya gyada Kai "itace".
Ta lumshe manyan idanunta ta Bude sannan tace "kayi saa tana da kirki da tausayi fiye da Kai".
   Ya zuba Mata Ido Yana Jin wani abu Yana fisgar zuciyarsa yace "hmmm ni Bani da tausayi?" Shuru tayi Amma bata dauke idonta akansa ba.
   Sai Shi ne ya dauke kallonsa akanta, 
Domin bazai juri hada Ido da ita ba.
  Ta Gama nazarin fuskarsa tsaf.
  A hankali tace "Ka kwantar da hankalinki Ka cire wannan tsoro da fargabar da ke tattare a fuskarka, saboda Kada abinda kake tsoro ya afku".
  Nan ne ya sake kallonta yace "me nake gudun ya afku".
   Ta danyi wani bushasshen murmushi sannan tace "fargabar Kada matarka tasan abinda ke tsakaninmu"
 Baiyi magana ba Amma sosai idanunsa suke nuna gaskiyar abinda ta fada.

 Ta Kara numfasawa tace "Kada Ka damu, hakan bazai taba kasancewa ba, Amma sai idan har Ka cire wannan fuskar da take saye da Kai, ko kadan ba Zan zamo silar rushewar farincikin gidanka ba, don babu dalilin da Zan bata dangantakar da akwai Akan wacce babu".
  Hakanan ya ji karshen maganarta Bai yi masa dadi ba, yace "wace dangantakar ce babu?".

   Zatayi magana kenan uwale ta yi sallama ta shigo ,
Hakanne yasa duk sukayi shuru tamakar Basu taba yin magana ba .
  Bata Dade da shigowa ba Mujaheeda ta shigo tana fadin "mu je yaya Taufiq ".
  Da sauri ya Mike ya Riga kowa fita .
Ba tare da taimakon kowa ba muhseena ta sakko daga Kan gadon ta gyara hijabin jikinta uwale ta rike hannunta mujaheeda na binsu abaya har zuwa wajen motar.
   Uwale da muhseena Duka suna baya Taufiq shike Jan motar muhseena na gefensa.
Sun danyi nisa da Fara tafiya, mujaheeda ta dubi Taufiq tace "ina ganin mu biya super market adan sayama Zainab Kaya, saboda lokacin da na bugeta tace akwai jakar kayanta , Amma banganta ba, ina ganin mutanan mu da Basu da imani da anyi hatsari su kwashe kayan mutane bana raba daya biyu irinsu ne suka dauke Mata jakar".
  Taufiq yace "duk zai yuwu tunda akwai irin mutanen da yawa".
Kai tsaye wani babban super market ya shiga. Uwale kadai aka bari acikin motar.
   Muhseena tayi mamakin irin kayan da mujaheeda ta saya Mata dogayen riguna sun Kai guda shida duk daya ta Kai dubu talatin .
  Sai atamfofi da less ko su ta dibar Mata da yawa harda su takalmi.
  Har suka bar super market din muhseena mamakin kirkin mujaheeda take yi, gami da karamcinta.
  Hakanan take ji azuciyarta tamkar bata kyauta Mata ba. Tun a motar take yi Mata godiya, har mujaheeda ta gaji da amsawa .
   Jefi jefi suna hada Ido da Taufiq ta madubi.
Har suka isa gida.

   Isar su gidan, muhseena tayi mamakin irin katon gidan da suka shigo. Tunda take bata taba ganin gida irin wannan ba, tsaruwar gidan ta yi yawa, komai kallon mamaki take masa har zuwa tsararren falon mujaheeda inda aka nuna Mata wajen Zama ta zauna.
   Daganan fa gabanta ya cigaba da matsanancin bugawa tana tunanin daganan Kuma ina zata fada.
   Tana nan zaune mujaheeda ta shigo ta kirata da sauri ta Mike tabi bayanta.
   Har zuwa wani daki me kyau me dauke da setin gado da kafet da kujera ta hutawa guda daya.
   Mujaheeda ta dubeta sosai tace "Zainab ga dakin da zaki zauna, ga bayi nan ki shiga kiyi wanka ki gyara kanki, zaa kawo abinci ki ci sannan Zan nemeki domin muyi magana".
   Tace "to nagode Allah ya Kara sutura".
  Har mujaheeda ta Kai bakin Kofa, muhseena tace "Haj ".
Ta tsaya ta juyo tana kallonta , sannan ta tako ahankali zuwa kusa da ita , tace "ina rokon Allah yayi Miki sakayya ta alheri ya Kula da ke da dukkan farincikin ki , ya karama dukiyarki albarka , dukkan hidimar da kikayi da ni ina rokon Allah ya mayar Miki da dubunsa".
   Sosai mujaheeda ta ji dadin adduar da muhseena take Mata .domin tunda take babu Wanda ya taba yi Mata irin wannan adduar. Hakanan ta ji son muhseena Yana Kara shiga ranta. Sosai take ganin mutunci da gaskiya a cikin idanun muhseena. Ta yarda da cewa duk inda muhseena ta fito to ta Sami ingantacciyar rayuwa ta tarbiyya.

   Ta numfasa tace "Amin Zainab nagode nima".
Daganan ta juya ta fita.
  Har muhseena ta Gama wanka ta shirya cikin wata jar doguwar riga ta ji  duwatsu ajikinta, haka zalika tayi matukar amsar ta, tayi kyau sosai, ramar da tayi ta Kara fito da kyawun fuskarta.
  Har ta Gama Shiri zuciyarta tana bugawa tana tunanin yaya al'amarinta zai kasance?. Kamar yadda Taufiq yake can falo ya kasa zaune ya kasa tsaye cike yake da fargabar Kada muhseena ta saki baki ko ta tashi daga alkawarin da tayi masa.
  Amma duk da haka Yana kokarin dakewa tare da boye duk abinda ke zuciyarsa, saboda gudun Kada mujaheeda ta Sanya zargi acikin zuciyarta.
   Har daki uwale ta biyota da abincinta, ta ci abincin sosai Wanda tunda ta zo garin jigawa ba ta ci irinsa ba.
   Karfe bakwai na dare uwale ta shigo ta Kira muhseena.
   Tare suka jera har zuwa falon mujaheeda. 
Zaune ta tadda mujaheeda kusa da Taufiq sosai ta ci kwalliya da doguwar rigar bacci me taushi sosai ta lafe ajikinta Dan matashin cikin jikinta ya Dan turo rigar sai tashin kamshi take na musamman. Muhseena ta rasa dalilin da yasa taji faduwar gaba da wani irin bakon al'amari ya na ratsa zuciyarta ganin mujaheeda daf da Taufiq ya Haddasa Mata Jin damuwar da ta rasa dalilinta.
   Asanyaye ta nemi kasan kafet Zata zauna. Da sauri mujaheeda tace "aah zauna Kan kujera". Ta girgiza Kai tace "nan ma ya yi", ta zauna idon Taufiq Yana dore akanta, muhseena tayi masa kyau sosai da sauri ya dauke Kansa ya maida Kan wayar hannunsa.

    "Zainab ina son naji gaskiyar labarinki da abinda ya kawo ki jigawa duk da kina ikirarin Baki San kowa agarin ba".
   Gaban muhseena ya Fadi kamar yadda Taufiq yaji kamar an tsira masa allura a kahon zuci da sauri ya dubi muhseena itama Shi take kallo.
     Ya yi kasa da kanta bakinsa dauke da adduar neman rufin asiri.
   A hankali muhseena ta Fara magana , kamar wacce ake karantama.
  "Ni yar kauyen danzaki  ce dake can jahar Kano, marainiya ce ni gaba da baya ba ni da kowa, Allah yayima mijina rasuwa ( da sauri Taufiq ya dubeta cike da kaduwa, don me muhseena zata maida Shi matacce), bayan na fita takaba rayuwa tayi min tsauri hakan yasa na yanke shawarar zuwa nan garin jigawa ko Allah zai taimakeni yasa na cika burina".
    Mujaheeda cike da tausayawa tace "menene burin naki?".
   Ta Dan numfasa sannan tace "Duk arayuwata tunda nasan ciwon Kaina Bani da burin da ya wuce nayi karatun boko me zurfi yadda Zan Zama abar alfahari , Amma Allah Bai Bani wannan damar ba saboda ban taso a yanayin da Zan Sami cikar burina ba".
   Mujaheeda ta numfasa tana cigaba da kallonta, sosai take Jin damuwarta a cikin zuciyarta,
 Ayanzu ne Kuma ta Kara gane dalilin da yasa take ganin tsananin bakinciki da tashin hankali a tattare da ita.
    Wanda ya rasa iyaye ya rasa Miji ba Shi Kuma da wasu yan'uwa da zasu tallafe Shi, ga talauci. Lalle abin a tausaya masa ne, shin idan ta rike muhseena ta taimaketa ta cika burinta ba zata Sami lada ba?.
   Hakika zata Sami dimbin ladan da ita kanta bata San yawamsa ba, domin akwai falala acikin rikon maraya, Kuma bayan hakama sosai take Jin aranta zata iya taimakonta tun ganinta na farko da ita.

   Shi Kansa Taufiq yayi mamakin yadda muhseena ta zauna ta tsara wannan bayanin , Wanda ya San ta rufe komai ne domin ta cika alkawarin da ta dauka na ràshin son ta tarwatsa masa gida, to Amma Anya kuwa ita yayi Mata adalci.
    Mujaheeda ta waiwayo ta dubi Taufiq tace
    "Yaya Taufiq kaji labarin Zainab, zuciyata na kokarin Dora min na taimaketa, Amma menene tunaninka Akan hakan , ".
    Taufiq ya Kara sanya nutsuwa acikin maganarsa yace "hakan Yana da kyau domin lada zaki samu, bazan hanaki aikata abinda nasan alheri ne agareki ba".
 Ta yi Dan murmushi tace 
"Nagode da kwarin guiwar da Ka Bani, Dama kaga na Saba Zama da Yan aiki uku yanzu guda biyu ne, saboda haka sai Zainab ta cike gurbin mutum dayan".
  Gaban Taufiq ya Fadi yayi gaggawar kallon muhseena ta yaya zata Zama Mai aikin kishiyarta?. Anya yayi Mata adalci kuwa. Suna hada Ido tamkar muhseena tasan irin tunanin da yake yi sai tayi wani dan murmushi Mai kunshe da sakon babu komai kaddarata ce tazo da haka.
    Zaiyi magana kenan muhseena ta riga Shi "nagode Haj Allah ya saka Miki da alheri, na yarda Zan Miki aiki duk abinda kike so zanyi Miki matukar zaki taimakeni na cika burina".
   Mujaheeda tayi murmushi sosai tace "Kada ki damu, Zan taimake ki  zakiyi karatu zaki sami cikar burinki, amma ina son ki rike amanata da amanar gidana".
   Tace "wannan babu damuwa in sha Allah zaki sameni me kiyayewa Akan duk abinda ya shafeki, nagode nagode ". Tace "babu damuwa, wannan dakin shine dakin Ki, " tace "to nagode ".
    Taufiq dai Bai Kara furta komai ba, 
  Duk da Yana Jin babu dadi aransa.
  Mujaheeda tace "Aina kika tsaya a karatun boko".
   Muhseena tace "iyakata aji uku na karamar sakandire".
   Tace "babu damuwa, kina iya tafiya".
   Ta Mike tana cigaba da yi Mata godiya ta koma daki. Dai dai lokacin wayar Taufiq ta dauki ruri nambar Mallam Umar ce dannawa yayi da sauri ya maidata silent.
  . muhseena tana shiga daki ta turo kofa ta jingina da jikin kofar gami da runtse idanunta .
  Jin hawaye tayi Yana zuba a fuskarta.
   Rayuwa irin tata tana cike da kalubale, shin wace kaddarace tayi sanadiyyar haduwarta da mujaheeda.?
   Sam bai dace tayi Mata karya ba, a irin mutuncin data nuna Mata Bai kamata ta yaudareta ba.
To Amma bata da wani zabi yin hakan shine kadai rufin asirinku Duka. Da kuma tabar gidan gara ta zauna tayi Mata bauta domin Akan auren da baida wata makoma ba zata zauna tayi asarar cikar burinta ba tunda mujaheeda ta amince zata taimaketa ita Kuma tayi alkawarin aje komai ta rungumi abinda Allah ya kaddara Mata .
   Amma ta rasa dalilinta na yin kuka Mai yawa har idanunta suka kumbura, ta rasa murna zatayi da zata samu cikar burinta ko kuwa bakinciki zatayi da zata jingine aurenta agefe saboda cikar burinta.
