  Alh yusuf lamido ya ce "shine dalilin da yasa na bukaci ki zo, saboda a bayyana duk abinda ke boye, domin wannan maganar da Zainab ta gaya muku ba gaskiya ba ce, ta fada ne saboda ta taimaka ma mijinta rufe sirrin da suke Jin tsoron bayyanawa ".

 Alh Mustapha labaran ya yi kasa da Kansa Yana adduar Allah ya kawo sassauci a lamarin.

Gabanta Yana dakan uku uku ta dubi Àlh yusuf tace "bangane ba, tana da aure ne Zainab din, Kuma waye Mijin nata?".

  Ta Karasa magana bakinta na rawa.
 Sannu a hankali Alh Yusuf lamido ya Gama bayyanama Haj murjanatu asalin gaskiyar al'amarin da matsayin muhseena a wajen Taufiq.
Ya cigaba da cewa "Daga auren har boyewar da sukayi anyi Shi ne bisa kuskure, aure dai ya dauru domin ko da wasa aka daura aure a addinance ya dauru balle auren da aka daura Shi da sadaki da waliyyai da komai, don haka Zainab matar Tau..............?
  
  Cikin mummunar fusata hade da tashin hankali, Haj murjanatu ta Mike tsaye idanunta sun rine sun koma jajir kamar Jan Gauta, nan da nan fuskar ta ta canja ta yi baki ta fita hayyacinta.
  
   Alh Mustapha labaran ya yi saurin Kallonta yace "ki yi hakuri ki mallaki Tunaninki Kada kiyi wani abu da zai janyo danasani". Ta watsa masa harara tace  cikin tsawa "na Dade banyi danasani ba , mayaudara ma ciya amana".
  Ta juya tana kallon Taufiq cike da tsana ta nuna Shi da hannunta tace "kalleni da kyau Kada Ku Raina min wayau Ku maida ni shashasha , wato saboda mujaheeda tana jinya shine zaka Kara aure " ta juya ta dubi Àlh yusuf lamido tace "Kai Kuma Baka ji kunyar tsayawa Ka  goyi bayan danka ba Ka Kuma yi Karya ba wani ya aureta tuntuni nayi Imani kwanciyar mujaheeda a asibiti ne kuka hadu kuka aura masa wannan makirar yarinyar, wacce Dama nasan masifa zata zame mana arayuwa muguwa".

Mallam Umar zaiyi magana kenan Alh Yusuf lamido ya daga masa hannu alamun yayi shuru Kada ya tanka.

Ta nuna Taufiq da hannu tace "Baka Isa kayima Yata kishiya ba, ko Ka manta takardar da kasa hannu Akan ba zaka taba yi Mata kishiya ba,butulu mugu annamimi Wanda Bai da mutunci tir da Hali irin naka".
  Ananne Alh Yusuf lamido ya dubi Taufiq yace "Taufiq wace takarda ce kasa hannu ". Ta saki tsaki tace ,"ni zaka tambaya ,".
  Bai bi ta kanta ba. ,ya Kara yi masa tambayar. 
  A sanyaye Taufiq ya gayama Alh Yusuf lamido komai.
  Alh Yusuf yace "Kai Kuma saboda alokacin Kana cikin Mayen soyayya shine kasa Mata hannu a takardar".

 Haj murjanatu ta ce "magana ta kare dole ya saki Zainab yanzun nan , matukar kuna son Zaman Lafiya da kwanciyar hankali".
 Alh Mustapha ya girgiza Kai yace "Haj ki bar wannan maganar domin babu yadda zaayi Taufiq ya saki Zainab saboda babu wani dalili ". 
  Ta juyo a harzuke tace da karfi "eh, dole Ka Fadi hakama tunda ba Kai Ka haifi mujaheeda ai dole yau Ka nuna bakin halin Ka a kanta, Ka Kuma zabi bare akanta, na rantse duk na ishe Ku". Alh Yusuf lamido ya ce "Haj ki kwantar da hankalinki ki natsu kisan abin nan da Taufiq ya aikata babu inda addini ya haramta masa don haka tunda Allah Bai haramta masa ba babu Wanda ya isa ya haramta masa, kiyi hakuri kawai ayima yarinyar nan adalci itama ta rayu tare da mijinta".
  Haj murjanatu ta zazzaro idanu waje cike da bala'i tace "na rantse kotu Zan Kai Ku, ai takardar yarjejeniyar da mukayi tana nan Zan Kai kotu sai munyi sharaa da Ku".

 Alh Yusuf lamido ya jinjina Kai yace "babu wata kotu da ta isa tasa Taufiq ya saki Zainab don kawai yasa hannu a takarda Akan bazai yi Mata kishiya ba, saboda ita kotun ba jahila bace ba ta Kuma isa ta goge umarnin ubangiji ba, don haka da wannan takardar da takardar tsire duk uwar su daya uban su daya, idan zaki  dawo hayyacinki ki amshi wannan kaddarar gara ki dawo don ni banga amfanin wannan hayagagar ba".
  Shi dai Alh Mustapha  tunda Haj murjanatu ta ambaci ba shi ya haifi mujaheeda ba ya ji komai ya tsaya masa, tashin hankalin ràshin haihuwar sa ya dawo masa sabo, komai ya tsaya masa ya Kuma zafafe Shi gefen kirjinsa sai zafi yake masa,
   Da karfi cikin fusata tace "to shikenan dole a cikin biyu Taufiq ya zabi daya ko ya saki Zainab ya zauna da mujaheeda ko ya zauna da Zainab ya saki mujaheeda don bazan taba daukar wannan bakin kaskancin ba wacece Zainab da zatayi kishi da mujaheeda bata isa ba , ba Saar yata ba ce ".

Dai dai lokacin muhseena ta shigo gidan ,dawowarta kenan daga makaranta.
  Ta ji maganar da Haj murjanatu ke yi , gabanta ya yi mummunan faduwa, musamman da ta cigaba da Jin yadda Haj murjanatu ta dage da masifa da bala'i iya karfinta Wanda ba cikin gidan ba har Makota sai sun jiyo irin tartsatsin da take yi.

Da sauri muhseena ta nufi falon Alh Yusuf lamido, dai dai lokacin Suma Haj saratu da Hajara suka fito a gigice, saboda Jin irin ihun da Haj murjanatu take yi, duk a gigice suka runtuma falon Alh Yusuf lamido.

Gaba daya suka fada,cikin falon, Haj saratu na fadin "Haba Haj murjanatu menene haka, ya kike kokarin maida kanki wata mahaukaciya ne, menene abin tashin a wañnan maganar sai kace Wanda aka aikata haramun haba sai kace ba musulma ba".

    Haj murjanatu ta juyo da karfinta,zata zazzagama Haj saratu nata balain.

Amma matar data gani a gefen Haj murjanatu yasa tayi dif. Ta hadiye abinda tayi nufin fada.

Shin wa take gani kamar Hajara, wannan fuskar ba zata taba boye Mata ba, ko da ace ta canja balle Hajara bata canja da komai ba sai manyanta da fuskarta ta nuna, har yanzu ta na nan siririya ba tayi Wata kiba ba, Haj murjanatu tasa hannu tana murza idanunta don ta Kara tabbatar da abinda take gani.

Yayin da Hajara ta Dora idonta Akan Haj murjanatu. A hankali ta tako zuwa cikin falon suna cigaba da kallon kallo, lokaci daya duk jamaar falon suka maida kallonsu ga abinda yasa Haj murjanatu yin shuru ga barin balain da take yi.

Gabanta Hajara ta tsaya suna kallon kallo.

 Zumbur Alh Mustapha labaran ya Mike Yana kallon Hajara ya tako har zuwa inda take tsaye Yana zaro Ido domin ya tabbatar Hajara ce ko ba ita ba ce.

  Cikin rawar murya Hajara ta nuna Haj murjanatu tace "Haj". Itama Haj murjanatu hannunta na rawa ta nuna Hajara tace "Hajara, ke ce?".
  Cikin matukar gigita gami da dimauta, Hajara ta dubi Àlh Mustapha labaran Wanda gaba daya ya dauke wuta Ido kawai ya Dora Mata ko motsi ya kasa yi. Alh yusuf lamido shima ya Mike Yana cewa " aah Dama kunsan juna ne, Amma Kuma wannan firgici da kuka fada ciki meye dalili".
  Mallam Umar ya tashi ya nufi wajen Hajara da gaba daya ta Gama birkicewa kuka me karfi ya kwace Mata, ya dubeta a dimauce yace "Hajara kwantar da hankalinki menene? Su waye su a gareki".
  Gaba daya guri ya gaure da kukan Hajara,su Kuma SU Haj murjanatu da Àlh Mustapha suna tsaye kamar wadannan aka dasa.
 Muhseena ta rungumo mahaifiyarta itama tana hawayen da ganin mahaifiyarta na kuka ya kawo su fuskarta tace "Inna menene ? Meye ke faruwa?". Haj murjanatu ta Kara rikicewa ta dawo da birkitattun idanunta Kan muhseena cikin nauyin harshe tace "zainab yar ki ce Hajara". Hajara kuka kawai take me karfin gaske Mallam Umar ya cigaba da tambayarta menene.
   Hannunta na rawa ta nuna Alh Mustapha labaran tace "Mallam ga mahaifin muhseena ".
  Tashin hankali Wanda baasa masa rana. Yawun bakin haj murjanatu ya kafe ayayinda Alh Mustapha ya yi taga taga zai Fadi Alh Yusuf lamido ya tallafo Shi ya rike. Cike da mamaki da tashin hankali yace "Alh wace magana ce wannan me kama da tatsuniya Kaine mahaifin Zainab ta ya ya? ta ina?".
Muhseena gaba daya ta Kara dimaucewa ta saki mahaifiyarta ta tako a hankali har gaban  Alh Mustapha labaran tana yi masa wani irin kallo me cike da tsana da kyamata, shima cikin fitar da hawaye ya zuba Mata Ido ya kasa magana saboda wani abu da ya tokare kirjinsa. Muhseena tace "Dama Kaine mahaifina, duk tsawon wannan lokacin ina tare da Wanda ya rusa rayuwata da ta mahaifiyata, ina tare da Wanda Bana kaunar ganinsa saboda bakinka da nake gani, duk duniya banga abinda na tsana sama da Kai ba".
  Nan fa kallo ya koma Sama maganar muhseena ta sakar da guiwar kowa a cikin falon.
Musamman Taufiq da Alh yusuf lamido Wanda tuni ya zare jikinsa daga jikin Àlh Mustapha ya na yi masa wani irin mummunan kallo.
  Yayin da Haj murjanatu ta ce  cikin galabaitacciyar murya wacce take cikin mugun Hali me wuyar fassara.
  "Ta yaya zaki Kira Zainab da yar Alh ".
  Hajara tayi Mata wani bushasshen kallo tace"Dalilin mummunan abinda Alh yayi àgare ni na Sami cikin muhseena, kin ji ta yadda ta Zama yarsa". Taufiq ya dafe kirji gami da zaro Ido ya so fitar da kalaman bakinsa Amma dimauta tasa sun kasa fita illa ya  nuna muhseena da hannu ya nuna Alh Mustapha da hannu ya kasa cewa uffan.
  Haj murjanatu tayi luuuu ta Fadi kasa , tana dukan kasa gaba daya hauka ta bayyana a tare da ita, tana fadin "na shiga uku na lalace ni murjanatu karshena ya zo , da Naga wannan Rana gara na mutu na bar duniya wayyo Allah kaicona kaicon abinda na aikata, da ace na biki na Baki hakki da yancin ki kila Allah da Bai yi min wannan mugun kamun ba, na shiga na Gama wulakanta a duniya, yau bayan shekaru Sama da ashirin abinda na aikata ya dawo gare ni zai kashe ni".
  Mallam Umar a fusace yace "Allah ya nuna Miki ishara ne don kisan Allah baya zaluunci Kuma ya Sanya zaluunci abin haramtawa a tsakanin mu, kun zakinci Hajara amatsayinki na ya mace na yi zaton zaki ji irin zafin da macen da ta tsinci kanta a irin wannan halin take ji, Wanda hakan zaisa ki ji tausayin Hajara ki Kuma tsaya Mata domin Samun yanci da hakkinta, aah maimakon kiyi hakan sai kika goyi bayan mijinki kika Kuma kare Shi da ga fuskantar hukunci kika tauye yanci da hakkinta kika lullube asirin mijinki kika tone na Hajara, ta yaya kuke zaton zaku Sami kwanciyar hankali a duniya".
  Alh Mustapha labaran ya fada Kan kujera Yana kokawa da numfashin sa da ya ke kokarin saukewa, muhseena ko a jikinta ta juya ta koma wajen mahaifiyar ta ta rungumeta suna kuka.
 A yayinda dole su Alh Yusuf lamido suka yi Kan Alh Mustapha suna taimakonsa , Taufiq ya dakko ruwa suna shafa masa a fuska.
Falo dai gaba daya kowa ya fada tashin hankali, don Haj saratu can ta koma ta zuba tagumi hannu biyu, tunda ta fahimci inda zancen ya dosa ta ji komai ya kwance Mata.
 Ita Kam Haj murjanatu Abu goma da ashirin ya hade Mata , duk ràshin imaninka idan kaga yadda ta koma hakika sai Ka tausaya Mata .

  Tsawon minti arba'in aka kwashe a falon cikin tashin hankali, kafin daker Alh Yusuf lamido ya shawo Kan al'amari aka Samu kowa ya dawo hayyacinsa.
  Sannan Àlh yusuf lamido ya dubi Hajara yace "Hajara,ki Kara Samun nutsuwa kiyi hakuri, mu da ba mu San komai ba kiyi mana bayanin komai saboda mu fahimci yadda al'amarin ya ke , kafin mu ce komai".

  Hajara ta girgiza Kai tace "bazan iya faden komai ba da bakina(ta juya ta kalli Mallam Umar) Mallam Ka yi masa bayani ". Muhseena ta goge hawayenta gami da kudundune kanta acikin hijabin jikinta.
  Mallam Umar ya muskuta , sannu a hankali ya warware masu komai, sannan ya Kara da cewa "saboda tsabar zaluunci duk tsawon wannan shekarun , Basu taba waiwayar Hajara ba duk da sun San inda zasu ganta, sannan ita Hajaran da suka San tasan inda zata gansu ko ba su je ba ita zata zo wajen su shine suka tattara suka bar kasar gaba daya, sun manta Allah shine me sakayya Kuma baya yafe laifin da ke tsakanin bawa da bawa, yau gashi duk kun gama guje gujen Allah ya hada Ku ba tare da Shan wahala ba".
 Tsananin kunya tasa Alh Mustapha labaran kasa dago Kansa sai hawaye ke zuba kamar an Bude famfo.
  Haka Haj murjanatu duk jarabar nan ta yi lakwas sai Ido take zarewa tana kallon muhseena tana kakabin kasancewarta yar Alh Mustapha.
  Duk falon nan babu Wanda baiyi tir da abinda Alh Mustapha labaran ya aikata ba, duk wani mutunci da kimar da suke ganinsa da Shi duk ya zube, kallon mugu azzalumi suke masa .
  Alh Mustapha labaran ya dago cikin zubar hawaye yace "Don Allah don son Annabi Hajara ki yafe min nasan nayi kuskure ba tun yau ba , tun faruwar al'amarin na rantse Miki ban Kara Samun kwanciyar hankali ba, kullum ina cikin zullumi da tunani babu abinda ke min dadi a duniya, ki yafe min Hajara ki yafe min , na Dade da yin nadama ".
..Mallam Umar ya watsa masa harara yace "idan har Kayi nadama meya hanaka nemanta Ka nemi yafiyarta, mu kasan sau nawa muna zuwa nemanka ?".
  Ya waiwayo ya dubi Haj murjanatu cike da tsana yace "kaga wacce ta Hana komai nan, masifa da bala'inta yasa ba Zan iya tunkararta da tambayar garin da  Hajara ta ke, domin ita kadai tasan kauyen da Hajara take, domin tun faruwar lamarin ban Kara Samun kwanciyar hankali a cikin zaman aurena da murjanatu ba, saboda ita kanta ta kasa yafe min".
  Malllam Umar ya ce cike da fusata "me yasa kuka bar Nigeria bayan faruwar al'amarin, ko kun tafi ne saboda Ku Kara tabbatar ma duniya da mugun halinku da zalincinku".

Nan ma Haj murjanatu ya duba wacce take cikin masifa me girma ko Kai bata iya dagawa duk wani abu ajikinta bata iya sarrafa Shi.
  Ya jinjina Kai yace 

" Bayan faruwar masifar da babu na biyun ta a rayuwata, a ranar ban dawo gidan ba saboda tashin hankali da damuwa, domin tun a lokacin da me faruwa ta afku na yi nadama na rasa inda zansa Kai na kamar yadda na rasa dalilin da yasa na aikata abinda na aikata.
  Abu dai daya na Sani shaidan yayi amfani da son da nake ma Hajara ya ingiza ni na aikata aikin danasani.
 Nayi ta fargabar Kara komawa gida saboda bansan masifar da Zan tarar ba a wajen murjanatu hakan yasa sai da nayi kwana bakwai a hotel ban dawo gida ba, ina ta istigfari ina neman gafarar ubangiji,sosai na tsani Kaina nayi kuka bansan iya yawansa ba, na zama tamkar mara lafiya, ko abinci Bana iya ci na rame na fita hayyacina gaba daya harkokina sun tsaya cak.
  Daga bisani na yanke shawarar komawa gida domin ina da tabbacin kila zuwa yanzu murjanatu ta Fara hucewa ko hukunci zatayi nasan ba zatayi hukunci cikin fushi ba.
  Nayi saa kuwa duk da mummunar tarbar da tayi min ta saurare ni na bàta hakuri sai dai tayi rantsuwar ba zata taba yafe min ba, Kuma ba zata kashe aurenta ba Amma ba zan taba Samun jindadin aure daga gareta ba, tayi alkawarin Sai dai mu zauna zaman doya da manja.
Haka rayuwar mu ta cigaba har zuwa yau babu wani jindadin aure bata mutunta ni bata daraja ni bata Jin maganata. Saboda nayi watsi da darajata ita Kuma ta hau Kai ta take.
Ita ce Kuma tayi tunanin tabbas idan Hajara ta fadama mahaifanta abinda ya faru zasu zo su neme mu karshe kowa sai ya ji abinda na aikata Wanda zubewar kima da darajar mu ce duka, ba Kuma zata juri wannan tozarcin ba, hakanne yasa tayi mana biza da ni da ita da mujaheeda Muka tattara Muka tafi London .
  Mun zauna tsawon lokaci acan Wanda saida ta tabbatar Kura ta lafa sannan Muka dawo Nigeria.
  Kasancewar tunda zamu bar Nigeria ta saida gidan da Muke ciki, yasa bayan dawowar mu Muka koma wani gidan Wanda babu yadda zaayi ko wata rana Hajara ta gane inda Muke. Domin bata San wannan gidan ba sai wancan.

Sai dai Kuma duk wañnan abin babu natsuwa ko kwanciyar hankali a gare ni, Bana iya bacci kullum ina tunanin Hajara da zalincin da nayi Mata.
Sai dai Banda wani damar da Zan tambayi murjanatu garin da Hajara take domin gudun fitina da tashin hankali.
 Ga uwa uba Allah ya jarabce ni da ràshin haihuwa, domin har yanzu ban taba haihuwa ba ba ni da da Wanda shima ina tunanin Yana daya daga cikin sayayyar da Allah ya yima Hajara".
  Ya juya kunya ta Kara kama Shi da ya ga yadda duk jama'ar falon suka zuba masa Ido.
Ya dauke Kansa ya dubi muhseena da ta kafe Shi da Ido sai dai baya jindadin irin kallon da take yi masa.
 Ya ce"Tun ranar da na Fara ganin Zainab na ji wani irin kaunar da ban taba Jin irinta ba agame da ita, ya zamana ko bacci nake sai nayi mafarkinta, gashi ko kadan Bana son na ganta cikin damuwa sosai nake jinta a raina, kullum nakan tambayi Kaina me yasa nake kaunarta?.
  Sai yau na Sami amsata dalilin da yasa nake kaunarta saboda ita din jinina ce ya ta ce, Allah na gode maka da Ka azurtani da Samun ya kamarta"
 Muhseena ta goge hawayenta ta dube Shi sosai tace tana nuna Mallam Umar, "Ba ni da wani mahaifi da ya wuce Shi (ta nuna Mallam Umar), saboda shine cikakken UBA shine Wanda ba ni da na biyunsa duk duniya kowa ya sanni ne a matsayin yarsa,don haka ba na kallonka a matsayin mahaifina asalima ina Jin kunya ina kyamatar a danganta ni da Kai ".
 Kalamanta sun taba zuciyar sa Kuka me karfi ne ya Kara kwace ma Alh Mustapha,a yanayin da Mallam Umar ya ke gyada Kai saboda tsananin Jindadin kalaman muhseena.
 Alh Mustapha yace  "don Allah Zainab ki yafe min ki yafe min ko na Sami saukin wannan masifar na rantse ko da wannan kunyar da na ji yau aka bar ni duk Allah ya Gama yi muku sakayya , Wanda bayan haka har ciwon hawan jini na Samu saboda tashin hankalin da nake ciki kullum".
  Ya Kara kallon Hajara yace "Hajara ki yafe min ki min afuwa ki yafe min".

  Ita dai Haj murjanatu gaba daya batasan a duniyar da take ba, gaba daya ta fita hayyacinta.    
  Alh Yusuf lamido ya numfasa ya Kara numfasawa sannan ya dago da Kansa yace " Duk inda irin wannan ta kasance Yana Zama abin tir, amma babban abu shine yadda Alh Mustapha labaran yayi nadama ya ke Kuma neman yafiya ga wadannan ya zalunta , hakika fadan baa kyautama Hajara ba ma duk Bai ta so ba domin iya ràshin kyautawa anyi Mata sai dai babu wani abin da zamu yi illa ahadu Duka ayi hakuri akuma yima juna afuwa domin Allah Yana son bayinsa masu yafiya, duk ayi hakuri a taushi zuciya".
  Malllam Umar yace "hakane , babu komai tunda yau dai Alh Mustapha ya karbi muhseena a matsayin yarsa ya Kuma yi nadamar komai to Allah ya yafe mana gaba daya".
  Taufiq dai Kansa na kasa yau ya sha mamaki har ya gaji, wai Dama Alh Mustapha shine mahaifin muhseena ba Mallam Umar ba, lalle Mallam Umar ya cika mutumin kirki, a yau ya Kara ganin girma da mutuncina fiye da kullum.

 Alh Mustapha ya dubi Alh Yusuf yace "don Allah Alh kasa Baki na Sami Hajara ta yafe min". Alh Yusuf ya dubi Hajara wacce tuni fuskarta ta kumbura saboda kuka .
 Yace "Hajara ki sani Allah Yana tare da masu hakuri, Yana kuma son masu yafiya adalilin yafe masa da zakiyi Alka Yana iya Jin kanki kema ya yafe Miki duk kurakuranki ,ansan an zakunce ki kwarai da gaske Amma idan kinyi hakuri kin yafe masa tabbas kinyi babban jihadi, don haka kiyi hakuri ki yafe masa"
Asanyaye cikin taushin murya me cike da sakonni Kala Kala aciki, tace "babu komai na yafe masa domin ràshin yafe masan ba Shi zai goge duk masifar da na fada ciki ba a dalilin faruwar hakan saboda haka na yafe masa".
  Sai a lokacin haj saratu tayi magana tace "lalle Hajara kin cika mutum domin yafe irin wannan laifin ba abu ba ne me sauki, Amma ladanki na gun ubangiji, sosai kimarki ta Karu a wajena na Sara Miki".

Alh Mustapha ya zuba Mata Ido yace " nagode Hajara Allah ya saka da alheri nagode"
Alh yusuf yace"Alhamdulillah Allah ya saka Miki da alheri," ya dubi Taufiq da sauran jamaar falon yace"to kunga dai yadda al'amari ya kasance da mujaheeda da muhseena duk abu daya ne saboda haka komai ya zo karshe".
  A fusace Haj murjanatu ta Mike idanu jajir tace "ba Abu daya ba ne hanyar jirgi da ban ta mota da ban babu abinda ya hada mujaheeda da muhseena sai gaba da masifa da bala',I na rantse bazan sassauta ba zanyi yaki da kowa Akan wannan lamarin domin yanzu nagane dalilin tsanar da nayi ma muhseena Bana Sonta Bana kaunarta Kuma Saina tarwatsa rayuwarta".Gaba daya Baki suka saki suna kakabin mugun halin Haj murjanatu suna mamakin yadda duk wañnan abin baisa ta saduda ba.
  Alh Mustapha ya Mike shima ya nunata da hannu Yana tsuma yace "ni Kuma na rantse Miki bazan Bari ba duk abinda ya Sami muhseena sai na daureki har igiya tayi rara bazan Raga Miki akanta ba domin Zan iya rabuwa da ke Zan Kuma iya sa kafar wando daya da ke akanta".
Ta buga hannunta a cinya tace "mu zuba shege Ka fasa saboda yar shegen zaka ce zaka iya rabuwa da ni......
  Ya katsa Mata tsawa yace "idan kika Kara Kiran muhseena da yar shege na rantse Miki sai nayi Miki dukan kawo wuka anan ".
  Ganin yadda yake tsuma ya tabbatar ma da Haj murjanatu tabbas Yana iya sa Mata hannu a gaban makiyanta,hakan yasa ta fisgo Jakarta ta juya ta zubama Hajara da muhseena harara tace "zakuyi namakina zaku San kun shigo gona ta domin sai na maida rayuwar Ku abar kwatance".
Tana gama fadan hakan ta juya fuu cikin fusata da masifa ta fice batasan inda take wurga kafarta ba harta isa bakin titi ta ta tare me a daidaita sahu ta bashi umarnin ya kaita asibiti
Kallo daya zakayi mata Ka dauka daga turu ta fito .
  Tunda take a duniya bata taba wukakantuwa irin na yau ba , yau duk isa da izzar ta sun kare agaban makiyanta. Ji take kamar ta mutu ta huta da bakinciki.
  Fitarta Alh Mustapha labaran ya cigaba da neman gafara duk yayi wujuga wujuga.
 Muhseena ce ta Fara mikewa tayi masa kallon tsaf ta fice daga fakon tana fita Hajara ta bi bayanta , itama Haj saratu ta tashi ta fita.
 A hankali shima Taufiq ya zare jikinsa ya fice.
 Alh Mustapha ya kara fadawa cikin dimbin tashin hankali da damuwa. Ganin halin da ya Kara shiga ne yasa Alh yusuf lamido ya cigaba da kwantar masa da hankali domin iya nadama ya Gama ganinta a cikin fuskarsa.
Shi dai Mallam Umar babu abinda yake cewa sai kallonsa kawai da yake yi, domin gaba daya ji yake kamar ya tashi ya shake masa wuya saboda tsana.mugun mutum kawai Wanda ya jefa abar kaunarsa cikin mawuyacin hali.
  Daker Alh Yusuf lamido ya shawo Kansa ya iya tashi ya tafi Amma sai da ya rike Shi yayi masa rakiya har wajen motarsa saboda yadda jikinsa ya mutu daker yake iya tafiya, ga wani matsanancin ciwon Kai da ya addabe Shi gami da faduwar gaba.
 Ya dai shiga mota ya ya tafi cikin rikitaccen hali mara dadi . Kai ya Allah ya Raba mu da mummunar kaddara.
TO BE CONTINUE

DAGA TASKAR YAR GIDAN IMAM






NI MA MATARSA CE
                63


        Jin Haj murjanatu bata yi
 magana ba yasa ta cigaba da magana "Ni banga amfanin wannan boyewar da kika yi ba, na dauka tunda Allah ya biya Miki bukatarki mujaheeda ta Sami lafiya zaki dawo cikin mutunci, a gaskiya ya kamata ki dauki darasin rayuwa yadda na dauka domin ni gaba daya yanzu na dawo daga rakiyar duniya na fahimci aikin banza ce abinda Kuma arziki Bai Baka ba na rantse ràshin arziki bazai ba Ka ba, Naga haka acikin aurena yadda na dauka da sai da malamai zaka mallaki mijinka Ashe biyayya da girmamawa suke sa Ka mallaki miji idan kinga yadda mijina yake lallabani ke kyace karamar yarinya ce, to gara kema ki dawo ki Yagi rabonki abinda kika kasa yima aurenki yanzu kiyi ki aje izzarki ki yarda da abinda Allah ya kaddara kawai sai ki zauna lafiya".

  Ta Dan aje numfashi sannan ta cigaba da cewa.
   "Sannan kin taba tambayar mujaheeda shin bata son dawowa  ga mijinta? Sannan ta manta da rayuwar yarta ne? Ko Kuma ta daina son mijinta ne?. Ki gwada tambayar ta hakan domin Bana son ki shiga hakkinta, ina baki shawarar nan ne a matsayinki na aminiyata wacce Bana son ganin nakasu a rayuwarta,".

    Tana Gama fadin hakan ta kashe wayar.

  Ayayin da itama Haj murjanatu tayi kasa da wayar cikin sanyin .jiki.
   Ta yunkura ta tashi sosai ta zauna tana aje numfashi a hankali, maganganun Haj Nana sun shigeta sun Kuma tsaya Mata a zuciya, hakika ita kanta bata da wani kwanciyar hankali a garin da ba na ta ba, bata mu'amala da kowa bata da kowa daga ita sai yarta.
   Tun bayan Samun lafiyar mujaheeda Haj murjanatu itace ta  bada karfin guiwar su yi zaman su a Germany ko sa Sami saukin bakinciki da damuwar su, sai dai Kuma tsawon wannan shekarun ba su Sami kwanciyar hankalin da suke tunanin samu ba,sai azabar kashe kudi da suke yi domin tuni tattalin arzikin su yayi kasa kudadensu na cikin account dinsu duk sun kare sai dai daga kamfanininta ake turo Mata da kudaden bukatunsu, Anya kuwa lokaci Bai yi ba da zasu koma mahaifarsu.

   Hatta wacce take yi domin ta mujaheedan tun daga ranar da ta Sami kanta, ta fahimci ta canja matukar canjawa, ta sa ma kanta tawakkali sosai na duk abinda ya faru, domin ta tabbatar yadda ta leka lahira ta dawo duniya to komai na Allah ne idan Kuma tace zatayi jayayya da hukuncin ubangiji to hakika zata ga fitinar da tafi wacce ta shiga, hakanne yasa ko da ta dawo hayyacinta bata zafafama kanta ba illa godiya da tayi ma Allah da ya sa ta Sami lafiya, duk jinyar da ta cigaba da yi bayan fitarta daga coma kokarin cirema kanta duk damuwa da wani kishin banza tayi.
   Haka zalika kullum tana yawan Jin maganganun a cikin kwakwalwarta muryar muhseena Kuma take ji, kafin a sallameta daga asibitin ne wata Rana tayi ma likita tambayar me yasa take Jin maganganu acikin kwakwalwarta, likita yayi Mata bayani sosai Akan zai yuwu muryoyin da take ji irin muryoyin wadanda suke tare da itane alokacin da take cikin coma.

   Haj murjanatu ta tuna ranar da mujaheeda ta zuba Mata Ido tace "mummy Zainab ta taba zuwa ta duba ni a asibiti?". Ta girgiza Kai tace "Bata taba zuwa ta duba ki ba, madina ce a tare da ke kullum sai Kuma ni".
   Ko da zata cigaba da maganganu hanata tayi ta hanyar nuna Mata abar maganar.

  Haj murjanatu ta numfasa bayan ta dawo daga dogon tunaninta. Bata taba Jin kwadayin komawa gida Nigeria ba irin na wannan lokacin.

   A dai dai wannan lokacin itama mujaheeda tana cikin wani kayataccen lambu dake cikin hotel din da suke Zama.
   Kamar kullum nanne take Zama domin debe kewa, tana zaune tana kallon jama'ar da ke kaiwa da komawa Amma a badini tunani ne fal a ranta kamar ko yaushe.

     Tunanin fadwa ya fi komai ci Mata rai, shin awane Hali yarta take ciki? Shin meye matsayin ta a wajen Taufiq?. Shin ta yaya zata barma muhseena yarta da mijinta,?wace riba ta samu wannan bakaramin wauta tayi ba. Babu abinda zaa fasa ko da mutuwa tayi balle tana Raye.
  Shin shikenan tabar yarta da mijinta saboda kishi na banza da wofi Wanda babu wani riba da ta ci aciki.
  Wata zuciyar tace ",ya Zama dole ki koma kiyi yaki ki kwaci mijinki da yarki, wannan zaman ba Shi ba ne mafita".

  Ta runtse idanunta hawaye ya shiga fita mata.shin kanta aka Fara kishiya? Ko kanta zaa daina? Don me zata kashe rayuwarta saboda kishi.

  A wañnan yammacin dai mujaheeda ta yi tunani da naxarin da ta Dade batayi irinsa ba Kuma dukkan tunanin ya dora Akan yarda da komawa Nigeria domin yarta da Kuma mijinta.

           ***************
       Rayuwa dai tayi ma Àlh Mustapha labaran dadi baida sauran wata damuwa data wuce na dawowar Haj murjanatu da mujaheeda.

   Kyakkyawar alakace tsakaninsa da muhseena, har Durmi suna zuwa tare a duk lokacin da wani abu ya faru. Zuwa wannan lokacin duk jama'ar Durmi sun shaida Alh Mustapha labaran mahaifin muhseena ne masu Mata mugun Baki da bakin fenti duk bakunansu sunyi shuru.
   Musamman Mallam kallah Wanda Alh Mustapha labaran na shigowa garin zai bar duk abinda yake yi ya taho gidan mallam Umar domin ya kwashi gaisuwa don yasan ranar sai ya cika aljihunsa da kudi.
   Tabangaren Mallam Umar kuwa duk yadda ya so kin yin mu'amala da Alh Mustapha to abin ya ci tura domin Alh Mustapha labaran a matsayin dan'uwa  Kuma amini ya dauke Shi,babu abinda baya masa na alheri domin hatta gidan Shi ya gyara masa ya saya masa sabuwar mota ga abinci baya yankewa Shi yayi Taufiq yayi Ande Dije duniya sabuwa arzikin da take tunanin sai a mafarki zata Samu yau gashi a zahiri tana dangwalar arzikin Hajara da muhseena data ke daukar su  a banza, gaba daya bakinta ya mutu Hajara ta Sami yancinta a gidan sai abinda taga damar yi take yi, ita kanta bata taba tsammanin Allah zai kawo irin wannan ranar da zata mallaki yancinta ba Kuma a goge bakin fentin da akayi Mata.

    Acikin hakane muhseena ta kammala jarabawarta ta karshe  burinta ya cika.NNPC kaduna aka turata yin service dinta. Taufiq ya so a barta a jigawa Amma Alh Mustapha labaran ya nuna babu damuwa domin abokinsa Alh Sani a nnpc din yake aiki don haka zai Kula da ita yadda ya kamata ba zaa samu wata matsala ba.

   Amma duk da haka duk bayan sati biyu sai ta dawo jigawa, ko don fadwa da bata son ta zauna tare da kowa sai ita, hannun Haj saratu take barinta tunda ta Fara zuwa makaranta, Amma duk da haka hankalinsu na Kan juna.
             *************

     Sassanyar ranar wata talata ce misalin karfe uku da minti goma, jirgin su Mujaheeda ya sauka.

   Wani irin sanyi mujaheeda ta ji a ranta alokacin da kafarta ta taka kasar Nigeria, ta lumshe idanunta ta Bude tana yima Allah godiya .
   Shatar mota suka dauka zuwa gida.
 Cikin matukar saa sun tadda gidan abude adalilin dawowar Alh Mustapha Kenan daga kamfaninsa.
  Yana zaune a kujera me zaman mutum biyu Yana rike da Kofin lemo zai Kai bakinsa Kenan sallamar mujaheeda yasa ya tsaya cak gami da juyowa Yana murza idanunsa "mujaheeda kece?", Ta saki jakar hannunta ta taho da gudu , lokaci daya yayi wurgi da Kofin hannunsa ya ware hannayensa ya rungume mujaheeda Kam Yana yima Allah godiya.
  Tsayawa kawai Haj murjanatu tayi tana kallon irin murna da shaukin da Alh Mustapha ya shiga na ganin mujaheeda.

  Ya saketa ya shiga duddiba jikinta Yana fadin "Alhamdulillah mujaheeda kin warke, Allah na gode maka". Hawaye ya shiga fita daga idanunta hakika Basu kyauta ba zaman da sukayi a Germany na tsawon lokaci bai kamata ba.

   Ya zaunar da ita sannan ya dubi Haj murjanatu, tayi zaton ganin wulakanci da tozarci daga gare Shi , Amma sabanin hakan sai taga yayi murmushi yace "sannunku da zuwa".
  Ta zame ta zauna Akan kujera tana kallonsa.
 Ya dawo gefenta ya zauna Yana kallonta "me yasa Baki sanar da ni zaku dawo ba da naje da Kaina na dakko Ku, ko da yake bakya son nasan komai a game da Ku".

 Shuru kawai tayi duk jikinta yayi sanyi.
Ya tashi ya dawo kusa da mujaheeda yace "jeki kiyi wanka ki huta Zan sa a kawo abinci daga restaurant".
  Cikin faraa tace "To daddy".
Harta tashi ya dubeta yace "Bari nayima Taufiq albishir din dawowarki". Ta tsaya ta na kallonsa sannan tace " Daddy har yanzu Yana tunawa da ni ne".
  Yace "ta yaya Taufiq zai manta da ke mujaheeda? kece kika manta da Shi Amma Shi kullum kina cikin tunanin sa".
   Kai kawai ta jinjina ta juya ta shige cikin daki.
Daga bisani itama Haj murjanatu ta kasa jurar irin kallon da Alh Mustapha yake yi Mata hakan ne yasa itama ta tashi ta shige nata dakin Wanda  tayi mamakin yadda ta Sami dakin tsaf tsaf a gyare domin dai tasan babu wata me aiki daya da ta bari a gidan kafin tafiyarsu duk ta sallame su.

    Cikin kankanin lokaci gaba daya labarin dawowar Haj murjanatu da mujaheeda ya iske duk wanda yakamata ya iske ta hanyar Alh Mustapha labaran.
  Taufiq ya Fara sanarmawa, zahiri yayi matukar jindadi da murnar dawowarsu, domin dai ko kadan bai kullacesu da ràshin dawowar su ba domin yasan cewa bacin rai ne ya haddasa hakan.
   Sosai yayi kewar mujaheeda, ya na Kuma cike da dokin son ganinta domin tayi jinyar da ya cire rai da ita.
  Muhseena ta dago fararen idanunta ta dubi Taufiq Yana ta faraa da murna sosai ta dan numfasa tace "kana murnar dawowar anti mujaheeda sosai ko?", Ta Karasa maganar a raunane, ya juyo ya dubeta sannan ya tashi ya dawo inda take ya zauna sosai gami da kwanto da Kansa a Saman kafadarta, tasa hannu daya ta tura cikin gashinsa tana daddanna gashin.
   "Kina da damuwa ne Akan hakan".
Ta girgiza Kai tace "Ko daya, yaya Taufiq Nima ina murnar dawowarsu, Amma Kuma ina fargabar abubuwa da dama". Ya Kara kwantar da Kansa Yana lumshe idanunsa.
  Yace "fargabar me kike yi"
Ta Dan numfasa sannan tace "ina fargabar Kada anti mujaheeda ta ki amsata bayan tasan dangantakar da ke tsakaninmu na hada Miji da Kuma hada iyaye".
  Ya girgiza Kai sannan ya dago yasa hannayensa biyu ya rungumeta Yana sunsinar kamshin jikinta.
  Yace "ko hakan ya kasance ba zai canja komai ba a game da matsayin mu, domin ke jinin jikina ce don haka rabo da ke tamkar rabo da Raina ne".
   Ta saba Jin irin wadannan kalaman masu sanyaya rai daga gare Shi.
  Don haka ta gyara zamanta tace "Bana kishin anti mujaheeda hakanne yasa a kullum nake adduar Allah ya daidaita tsakaninmu domin bazan taba yarda ace akaina aurenku ya samu matsala ba, don haka don Allah kayi iya kokarinka domin anti mujaheeda ta dawo gareka".
     Ya lumshe idanunsa,bai taba ganin mace mara son Kai irin muhseena ba, wannan halin nata Yana daya daga cikin dalilan da suke Kara Sawa yake matukar kaunarta.
  Ya Bude idonsa suka hada Ido yace "in sha Allah zanyi iya kokarina".
  Tace "idan zaka dubata Zan bika".
Ya girgiza Kai Yana kallon cikin jikinta watanni biyar har ya Fara turo Riga yace "ki huta yau kinga kin jigata dazu Aman da kikayi", tace "Lafiya lau nake yaya Taufiq, ai na gaya maka dalilin yin aman nawa, kamshin turarenka ne da bana so yanzu".
Yayi murmushi yace "To ai na fitar da su daga gidan". Gaba daya sukayi dariya.

    Karfe biyar da rabi Taufiq da muhseena suka isa gidan Alh Mustapha labaran. Dukkansu  a falo suka taddasu a zaune Alh Mustapha labaran Yana kusa da mujaheeda labari suke yi sosai. Shigowar su muhseena gaba daya sai mujaheeda ta kidime, idanunta suka sauka akan cikin muhseena wani irin bakinciki ya tokare zuciyarta, tayi saurin dauke kallonta daga muhseena ta dawo da Shi Kan Taufiq inda take Jin numfashin ta na saurin fita , duk yadda ta so da ta tsani Taufiq ta kasa domin dai wani irin matsanancin sonsa ne ya ke yawo acikin jininta, tana son daina kallonsa Amma idonta ya kasa Shi kawai take son gani kamar yadda shima ya zuba Mata Ido duk da irin ramar da tayi to fa sai yaga ta Kara masa matukar kyau.

   Kai tsaye muhseena gun mujaheeda ta wuce ta rungumeta tana tambayar ta jikinta.
  Mujaheeda ta yi kokarin daidaita tunaninta , hakan yasa ta amsa duk gaisuwar muhseena ba tare da nuna wata alama ta damuwa ko tsanarta ba.ita Kam Haj murjanatu ko yaya ta dubi muhseena Jin wani bacin rai take tana Kara jinjina cewa wai muhseena YAR Alh Mustapha ce.
   Har muhseenan ta tashi ta dawo gaban Haj murjanatu ta durkusa har kasa ta gaisheta , yadda Alh Mustapha ya Kura Mata Ido domin ganin irin karbar da zata ma muhseena yasa ta yi kokarin boye komai ta amsa Mata sannan ta Kara da cewa ta tashi ta hau kujera.
  Hakan ko ba karamin mamaki yaba muhseena da Alh Mustapha labaran ba, tambayar da suke ta yima kansu itace "shin Haj murjanatu ta shiryu ne ta Kuma karbi muhseena a matsayin kaddararta ko kuwa wani sabon wasan zaa Fara?.

     Taufiq ma ya gaida Haj murjanatu yadda yayi zaton zata amsa yaga sabanin hakan domin ta amsa a mutunce.

  Alh Mustapha ya Kula da irin yadda Taufiq da mujaheeda ke kallon juna,don haka ya dubi mujaheeda yace "mujaheeda ki tashi Ku Koma wancan falon ke da Taufiq Ku gaisa".
   Ya karasa maganar Yana kallon muhseena itama Shi take kallo , ta idonsa yake lallashinta ta Kuma fahimci hakan don haka ta jinjina masa Kai gami da yin dan murmushi sannan tayi kasa da kanta, har su mujaheeda suka wuce ta gabanta bata dago kanta ba, duk da idon Taufiq na kanta ya so su hada Ido don ya fahimci yadda take ji amma hakan bai samu ba.

    Suna shiga falon mujaheeda zata wuce ta zauna akan kujera Kenan, Taufiq yasa hannu ya janyota gaba daya zuwa faffadan kirjinsa, tun tana kicikicin kwace kanta harta sallama ta koma ta kwantar da kanta Shi Kuma ya sa hannayensa biyu ya Kara rungumeta ya runtse idonsa sosai cikin wata kasalalliyar murya yace "Mujaheeda me yasa kika guje ni?, Me yasa kika ki dawowa bayan kin Sami lafiya?".
  Cikin kuka tace "saboda Baka Sona yaya Taufiq Ka Sami makwafina, baka bukatata a rayuwar Ka".
  Ya dago da fuskarta ya manna bakinsa a nata hakan yas dole ta hadiye sauran kalaman nata, bata Hana Shi ba domin itama tasan tayi matukar rashinsa.
  Gaba daya Kan kujera suka zube, ya zare bakinsa a nata ya kura Mata Ido tsawon lokaci suna kallon juna, sannan yace "waye ya gaya Miki Bana son ki? Waye ya gaya Miki bana bukatarki, na rantse Miki mujaheeda ina sonki na Kuma fi kowa bukatarki, ina son kiyi min uziri ban auri muhseena don Bana sonki ba ko don ina son na wulakantaki, kaddara CE kawai tafi karfin mu Wanda nake son kema ki yarda da wannan kaddarar don Allah mujaheeda mu bar maganar nan tun da kin Riga ayanxu kinsan matsayin muhseena agareki bayan abokiyar zamanki ita din yar'uwarki ce, sannan mahaifiyarta tana sonki kamar ita ta haifeki nasan an sanar da ke komai itace wacce ta yi rainonki wacce baki son ta tafi ko'ina ta barki".
  Kuka me karfi ta Kara fashewa da Shi tace "naji komai, don Allah Ka daina tunatar da ni".
 Ya Kara matseta Yana shafar ko'ina a jikinta, "ina tunatar da ke ne saboda ki Kara yarda da cewa komai hukuncin Allah ne, mujaheeda ki dawo gare ni".
  Ta kasa tabuka komai daya wuce fitar da hawaye.
 Taufiq ya cigaba da sumbatar ko'ina a jikinta, tun bata maida masa martani harta Fara mayar masa "ki dawo gare ni mu cigaba da rayuwa".
  Mujaheeda batasan lokacin da tace "Zan dawo gareka yaya Taufiq domin bazan iya rayuwa babu Kai ba,na fusata da Kai Amma ina ganinka komai ya fita araina".
   Abinda Taufiq yake son ji kenan , tana Gama fadin haka ya zare jikinsa daga nata ya janyo hannunta ya rike idanunsa jajir yace "Alhamdulillah nagode mujaheeda ko yanzu ki zo mu tafi gidan mu mu shimfida sabuwar rayuwa".
  Ta girgiza Kai tace "muhseena fa?" 
 .ya girgiza Kai yace "Baki da matsala da ita tafi kowa son ki dawo dakinki a matsayin yaya uwa daya uba daya take kallonki nasan muhseena ba zata taba baki matsala ba,".
  Ta yi kasa da kanta zuwa wasu dakikai sannan tace "shikenan, zanyi magana da mummy idan nasa ranar dawowa sai na sanar da Kai".
  Kafin yayi magana tace "ina fadwa?, Me yasa baku taho da ita ba?".
  Yace "zasu taho tare da Haj don tana gidan mu jiya Haj ta tafi da ita".
 Ta dago tace "Ta yi girma ko?".
Ya zaro wayarsa a aljihu ya fito da hotonta ya nuna Mata. Ta Kara kyau ga girma da tayi sosai.
   Haka Taufiq ya cigaba da lallashi da nuna ma mujaheeda muhimmancinta a gare Shi , har sai da ta ji ta aminta sosai da maganganunsa, zuciyarta Kuma ta natsu da ta koma gidansa.
  Sun Dade Basu fito daga falon ba , tun Alh Mustapha na Jan muhseena da hira domin kauda mata shiga damuwa har ya Rasa abinda zai fada. Ita kanta muhseena ta Kula kwarai da dalilin da yasa Alh Mustapha ke kokarin dauke hankalinta don haka nunawa tayi babu komai, amma ta rasa dalilin da yasa can kasan zuciyarta take Jin zafi da Kunar kebewar Taufiq da mujaheeda.
  Ita ko Haj murjanatu dadi ne ya cika zuciyarta domin ko banza dai Taufiq ya nunama muhseena Yana son mujaheeda tunda har gashi ya manta da ita alokacin da yayi tozali da mujaheeda.
  Wannan Kuma shine Karon farko da ta ji , mujaheeda ba zata bar ma muhseena Taufiq ba, zata koma dakinta tayi rayuwa yadda take so , domin muhseena bata isa ta Hana mujaheeda jindadin rayuwa ba.

     Daga bisani dai Taufiq ya fito yayi sallama dasu Haj murjanatu suka dauki hanyar gida Shi da muhseena. Har sun Dan yi nisa muhseena ta Kara kallonsa ranta cike da kuna tace "Ka gyara maballin rigarka a balle yake, sannan idan mun ISA gida kayi saurin canja rigar jikinka domin janbakin matarka ya bata rigar".
  Duk da ya Sami faduwar gaba sai ya dake, yayi kamar baiji.abinda tace ba. Domin ya Kula kwarai da fushin dake makale a fuskarta.
 Kai kishi na wahalar da Mata, komai dai kauda Kan mace Akan  mijinta sai tayi kishi matukar tana sonsa.
  A ranar Haj saratu da Alh yusuf lamido da fadwa da Haj bilki da haulat suka je gidansu mujaheeda suka dubata. Kam mujaheeda ta rike fadwa tana Jin kaunarta har cikin bargonta tayi rashinta sosai, don ko da suka tashi tafiya nan suka bar Mata fadwa.
   Kwanaki uku da dawowar mujaheeda kullum Taufiq sai ya je gidan su, fadwa Kuma na gunta ta hanata dawowa. 

  Rana ta hudu shima can gidan Taufiq ya uni, damuwa sosai muhseena take yi ko kadan bata son kishin mujaheeda yayi tasiri a zuciyarta, domin ta Fara Sama ranta cewa Taufiq ya koma ga mujaheeda. Uni Kuna ranta yake yi, tana tunanin abubuwa da dama Sam bata son shaidan yayi galaba akanta.

   Sha daya saura minti goma na dare, Taufiq ya dawo, muhseena na kwance a Kan doguwar kujera innalillahi wainna'ilaihirrajiun kawai take ta maimatawa acikin zuciyarta.
   Cikin dakushewar murya muhseena tace "sannu da dawowa". Ya zauna a gefenta yasa hannu ya Fara shafan Saman bayanta domin ya fahimci sautin damuwa acikin muryarta "yauwa tauraruwata".
  Ta lumshe Ido ta Bude hakan nan  ta ji hawaye ya na kokarin fita Mata.
   "Ya anti mujaheeda"
Bai amsa ba illa cigaba da shafata da yake yi , bata damu da ràshin amsawar tasa ba ta cigaba da cewa "yaushe zata dawo dakinta".

  Taufiq duk ya fahimci halinda ta ciki ya Kuma fahimci dalilin tambayoyin da take masa. Cikin sanyin murya yace, "muhseena tashi muyi magana".ta makale kafada , ya jinjina Kai Yana kokarin dagata "aah tashi muyi magana tashi".
  Ya mikar da ita ya zaunar ya rike hannunta sosai Yana murzawa, yace "kalleni". Ta dago da idanunta masu fitar da hawaye tana kallonsa Amma ta kasa jurar hakan don haka ta runtse idanunta hawaye Yana fita. Cikin sanyin guiwa ya sa harshe ya shiga  dauke hawayen daya bayan daya , tsigar jikinta ya cigaba da tashi lokaci daya Kuma kaunarsa Yana Kara ratsa dukkan zuciyarta.
  "Kiyi hakuri, ki daina kuka ki tsaya ki fahimce ni".
  Ta Bude idonta tace 'ina jinka me kake son na fahimta?".
Ya gyara zamansa har yanzu hannunsa yana cikin nata ya Fara magana cikin sanyin murya, "Kada kiyi zaton ina yin duk abinda nake yi ne saboda na bata Miki rai, ko don na nuna Miki Ina son mujaheeda " ya girgiza Kai "ko daya ba haka bane, ina yi ne saboda na cika alkawarin da kikasa na Miki akan nayi duk yadda zanyi mujaheeda ta dawo gare ni, hakanne yasa nake lallabata tare da nuna Mata muhimmancinta agare ni, Kuma ta amince zata dawo nan da kwanaki biyu".
  Muhseena ta danyi murmushi sannan tace "kana son anti mujaheeda?".
  Duk da tambayar tayi masa nauyi , hakan Bai hanata gaya Mata magana ta gaskiya ba, inda ya dubeta yace "bazanyi Miki karya ba, ina son mujaheeda sosai". Taji maganar kamar cakawar wuka a kirjinta, tayi saurin boye damuwarta sannan  tace "ina fatan zakayi adalci a tsakaninmu, Kada Ka wulakanta ni domin wacce kake so ta dawo gareka".
  Ya janyota da karfi Kan kirjinsa ya kwantar , maganarta tayi masa zafi, yace "kina zaton Bana sonki ne, don nace ina son mujaheeda, muhseena ina sonki son da ban taba yi ma wata mace ba ina jinki a cikin zuciyata da jinina".
  Hannunta tasa ta rungume Shi sosai sonsa na cigaba da zafafa a cikin ranta. Shima rungumeta yayi tsam tsam, Yana cewa "nayi Miki uziri nasan kishi na ne a ranki Wanda ban isa na Hana ki ba domin halittace , sai dai ina rokonki Kada ki zafafa ma kanki kishi irin na mujaheeda, Wanda itama kullum naje ina yi Mata nasihar ta rage kishi Kuma Alhamdulillah tun dasowarta na fahimci kishinta ya ragu sai dai Wanda baa rasa ba, Wanda kullum na je ina kokarin cire Mata kishinki domin ina shaawar Ku zauna lafiya".
   A hankali tace "Allah yasa mu zauna lafiyan "
  "Amin ya Allah, Shi yasa ba zaku zauna gida daya ba zaki cigaba da Zama a gidanki itama zata cigaba da Zama a gidanta , hakanne zaisa Ku zauna lafiya don idanun Ku ba zasu ga komai ba balle zuciya ta so su".
Ta yarda da maganarsa hakan ya fi, saura Kuma ta yi yaki da shaidan akan abinda yake son tusa Mata arai.
  Ta Kuma sa aranta ranar da mujaheeda zata dawo ranar zata koma Kaduna ba Kuma zata dawo ba har sai ta Gama service din ta gaba daya Wanda watanni uku ya rage.

   Tabangaren mujaheeda kuwa zafi da kushin muhseena sun ragu sosai a zuciyarta, tun daga ranar da Haj madina ta sanar da ita irin kokarin da muhseena tayi Akan jinyarta  Wanda kullum sai ta zo asibitin ta Kula da ita har tabar kasar.
  Sosai take ganin kima da mutuncinta, ita kanta Haj murjanatu jikinta yayi sanyi da Jin abinda bata sani ba.
  Hakanne ma yasa da mujaheeda ta yanke shawarar komawa dakinta bata shiga cikin hukuncinta ba, sai ma karfafa Mata guiwa tayi kamar yadda Alh Mustapha ya karfafa Mata , 
  Aranar ita kanta Haj murjanatu tayi mamakin yadda Alh Mustapha ya nuna lalle mujaheeda yarsa ce, Wanda tayi zaton zaiyi son rai ya nuna muhseena yake so amma a maganganunsa ya tabbatar mata matsayin mujaheeda da muhseena daya awajensa.

   Kuma da Kansa ya dauki mujaheeda ya maidata dakinta. Ya Kuma sa Taufiq ya kawo muhseena ya zaunar da su yayi musu nasiha me tsuma zuciya, ya Kuma gaya musu su aje alakar kishi dake tsakaninsu su dauki alakar ya da kanwa da rayuwa ta kaddaro musu, ya tunanar da SU dukansu yayansa ne. Ya Dade sosai a gidan da Kuma ya tashi tafiya tunda dare yayi cewa yayi Taufiq yayi zamansa Shi zai aje muhseena a gida. Duk da ransa Bai so ba haka ya daure ya karbi hukuncin surukinsa.
  Ko a motar tun fitar su gidan, Alh Mustapha labaran yake cigaba da yima muhseena nasiha ya Kara da cewa "kwanciyar hankalinki shine nawa domin Bani da wani abu da nake so sama da ke ina sonki ina jinki acikin bargona Bana son damuwarki don haka ki kwantar da hankalinkinki Zama da kishiya ba wani abu bane idan kika dauka da zafi yafi wuta zafi idan kika dauka da sanyi yafi kankara sanyi, illa iyaka ki rike ibada da addua da Kuma biyayya ga mijinki ki yarda shine shugabanki Kuma ibada kike yi , sannan ki zauna da mujaheeda da zuciya daya ki so ta ki kaunace ta to komai zai zo Miki da sauki".

  Muhseena ta dauki nasihar mahaifinta sosai. Bayan ya ajeta a gida.
 Har tayi Shirin kwanciya wayar Taufiq ta tsaidata ta dauka cike da faraa tace "Ango kasha kamshi" yayi dariya yace 'Kin sha magani" ta amsa "Na sha", "Kin kashe AC KO, kinsan dai matsalar da yske janyo Miki, Kuma Bana nan" tace "Kada Ka damu duk abinda Ka Saba yi min nayi da Kaina nagode da kulawa", ya gyada Kai yace "ko nasa Haulat ta zo ta kwana da ke ne" ta girgiza Kai tace "aah babu damuwa, sai Allah ya kaimu goben" yace "to Allah ya kaimu" . Daganan suka aje wayar.
  Yadda muhseena tayi zaton zata Dade bata iya bacci ba saboda ràshin Taufiq, ba haka bane domin tana Gama addu'onin baccinta bacci ya kwasheta.

    Ta so komawa Kaduna washegari Amma Taufiq ya ki har sai ranar litinin da kanshi ya dauketa zuwa Kaduna bayan ya kaita tayi sallama da mujaheeda.ta jidadin yadda taga fadwa ta sake da mahaifiyarta sosai sai dai Kuma ganin muhseena zata tafi yasa tayi ta kuka daker mujaheeda ta lallasheta tayi shuru bayan tafiyarta.
  Irin rukon da muhseena tayi ma fadwa kadai ya isa yasa mujaheeda ta so muhseena,kamar yadda take kokarin yi a yanzu domin da karfi da ya ji tana ta turama kanta kaunar muhseena, irin soyayyar da take Mata tun farkon zuwanta gidanta Shi take kokarin dawowa da Shi, domin dai ta tabbatar da cewa muhseena bata da tunanin raba ta da mijinta.
    
    Washegarin komawar muhseena Kaduna, mallam Umar da Hajara suka iso jigawa takanas domin duba mujaheeda. Hajara cike take da dokin ganin mujaheeda domin dai har yanzu kaunarta na nan makale acikin zuciyarta.
   Haj. Saratu ce tayi musu rakiya har gidan mujaheedan, yayin da Taufiq ya bar komai ya baro office ya dawo gida domin gaisawa da surukansa. Tun zuwansu gidan sabbin Yan aikin mujaheeda suke dawainiya da su, ita ko mujaheeda duk da karancin shekarun ta alokacin da tayi rayuwa da Hajara to fa ta dan tuna wasu abubuwan da dama, haka zalika sosai take ganin girman Hajara musamman yadda ta zauna tayi ta Mata nasiha gami da nuna Mata muhimmancinta a gareta, ta Kara da cewa "Nasan ba zaki iya tuna irin shakuwa da soyayya me karfi da ke tsakanina da ke a wancan lokacin ko bandaki Zan shiga sai kin tsaya bakin kofa kina jiran fitowa ta.......?.
   Mujaheeda ta numfasa tace "Zan iya tuna wasu , wasu Kuma na manta, amma tun kafin zuwanki daddy ya tunatar da ni hakan, cikin abinda ya tunatar da ni harda alakata da muhseena ta bangarenki". Cikin nauyin baki Hajara tace "hakane ba zamu ja da kaddara ba domin yin Allah ne, Alh Mustapha shine mahaifin muhseena Shi yasa nake son Ku hada Kai don Allah Kada Ku kalli junanku a matsayin kishiyoyi don da ina da ikon canja wannan kaddarar na Zama karkashin Miji daya ke da muhseena hakika da na canja, Amma bamu da yadda zamuyi".

  Mujaheeda dai ta natsu tana sauraron maganganun Hajara, bayan ta Gama itama Haj saratu ta dora da nata, Taufiq dai Yana gefe ransa yayi fari kal, irin nasihohin da sukema mujaheeda Yana yi masa dadi kwarai.
  Daga karshe shima Mallam Umar ya dora da na Shi, duk abinda suka fada gaskiya ne mujaheeda Kuma tayi alkawarin yin amfani da duk abinda suka ce. Da zasu tafi rafar dubu daya mujaheeda ta ba Hajara nera dubu dari,ta amsa gami da sa Mata albarka.
    Kwanan su Hajara biyu a jigawa suka koma Durmi, Amma kafin su koma sai da Mallam Umar ya Kai Hajara gidan Alh Mustapha suka gaisa da Haj murjanatu tare da yi Mata murnar Samun lafiyar mujaheeda. Yadda Hajara ta dauka Haj murjanatu zata karbeta sabanin hakan ta gani domin duk da fuskarta babu yabo babu fallasa to fa bata wulakanta su ba, har suka bar gidan Hajara na cike da mamakin hakan gami da fatan Allah yasa ta shiryu ne ta dawo hanya madaidaiciya.
    
             ************
     Kamar yadda muhseena ta kudurta a ranta ba zata Kara komawa jigawa ba sai ta kammala bautar kasarta, haka ta dage duk iya yadda Taufiq ya so ta dawo ta ki dawowa acewarta ta ba su damar cin amarcinsu ne, Har fushi sai da Taufiq ya yi amma ta ki dawowa sai dai kullum ta tura masa sakon ban hakuri da neman yafiya.
   Dole shine hakurinsa ya Gaza ya sanar da mujaheeda zai je aiki na kwanaki biyu .
 Daganan Kaduna ya wuce. Ko da ya je duk yadda ya so da ya nuna Mata ransa ya baci sakamakon kin dawowarta gida ya kasa saima tausaya Mata da yayi sakamakon ganin yadda cikinta yayi girma sosai, sai dai duk da haka ya kasa hakura da ita sai da ya dauketa daga gidan Alh Sani gidan abokin Alh Mustapha inda take Zama ya kaita hotel din da ya kama, anan ya lafarma Kansa da duk wata sha'awarta da ta addabe Shi, ita kanta duk da jikinta yayi nauyi bai hanata kokartawa ta Fara ta masa rai ba yadda yakamata.
  Kwana biyu yayi a Kaduna sannan ya dawo jigawa.

     Yau litinin aranar mujaheeda ta koma office, kamfani na nan inda ta barshi babu abinda ya sauya Àlh jibrin ne ke yin yadda ya so Yana ta cin karensa babu babbaka.
    Sai dai tana shigowa kamfanin Alh jibrin ya kidime , nan da nan ya hada files ya Kai Kan tebur dinta ya sanar da ita Dama Yana Shirin yin tafiya , ga duk abinda zata bukata nan , yayi Mata Kuma fatan alheri tare da tayata murnar Samun lafiyar ta. Daganan a gaggauce ya bar office din. Haka sauran ma'aikatan suka yi ta shigowa suna yi mata murnar Samun lafiya.


     A gigice mujaheeda ta shigo falon Haj murjanatu tana kwala Mata Kira, da sauri Haj murjanatu ta fito daga cikin daki, tana fadin "mujaheeda menene Lafiya?".
   Mujaheeda ta fito da takarda ta Mika Mata sannan ta fada Kan kujera tana aje numfashi. Da sauri Haj murjanatu ta bude takardar ta Fara dubawa, itama a hankali takardar ta sulale a hannunta ta Fadi kasa.
  Ta dubeta Baki na rawa "mujaheeda ina duk aka Kai wadannan kudaden", mujaheeda ta dubeta Ido jajir tace "Duk iya binciken da nayi manager shine ya tattaresu ya handame, tsawon wannan shekarun da mukayi. Bama kasar babu wani abu daya na cigaba da kamfanin ya samu, da yawan ma'aikatan ma wasu an koresu saboda ba'a iya yin albashi , an daina shigo da Kaya hakan yasa babu wasu Kaya da kamfani yake sarrafawa balle har afitar da su a samu riba, kayan aiki da Muke ajewa a ma'adanar kamfani babu komai, murtala ya tabbatar min da cewa manager jibrin shine ya ke fita dasu Yana sayar ma da sauran kamfanoni irin namu Shi yasa yanzu baa maganar kamfanin mu domin duk abinda Muke sarrafawa mu fita dashi sauran kamfanonin irinsa suke yi, babu ko sisii a asusun kamfanin, babu dai komai sai ginin kamfanin da Kayan aikin ciki amma bamu da kudin cigaba da Kula da kamfanin , sai dai a rufe Shi.".

   Haj murjanatu ta fada Kan kujera turus, tana fadin " jibrin yayi mana illa, innalillahi wainna'ilaihirrajiun,na yarda da jibrin na bashi duk amanata me yasa zai min haka, na rantse yanzu zansa yansanda su kamo Shi duk abinda ya ci sai ya dawo da Shi". Mujaheeda ta girgiza Kai tace "ban shigo gidan nan ba sai da na Gama bincike akan Alh jibrin, baya cikin jigawa domin tun shigata kamfani ya tafi ya ce Dama zaiyi tafiya ,sai yanzu na gane dalilin tafiyar tasa iyalansa ya dauka suka bar jigawa, mummy ban san inda suka koma ba, bansan ta yadda zamu iya kama Shi ba".

    Iya gigita da tashin hankali Haj murjanatu ta shiga, domin wannan kamfanin kusan Rabin dukiyarsu Yana ciki. Nan da nan ta shiga sambatu, mujaheeda ta dawo kusa da ita ta zauna tana tausarta tare da karfafa Mata guiwar za'aga Alh jibrin. Amma ina Haj murjanatu ta riga ta gigice, a haka Alh Mustapha ya shigo ya taddata cikin mawuyacin Hali, mujaheeda ce ta iya yi masa bayanin duk abinda yafaru.
  Shima ya jinjina al'amarin ya Kuma samu damuwa kwarai, nan da nan ya fito da wayarsa ya Kira dpo din yankinsu ya sanar da Shi duk abinda ke faruwa ya Kuma bukaci taimakonsa akan yasa a Sanya Ido akan Alh jibrin a binciko inda ya gudu da iyalinsa ,dpo ya jajanta sannan yayi alkawarin taimakonsa.

    "Suma sauran kamfanonin ya kamata a bincike su asan Suma wane Hali suke ciki". Mujaheeda tace "hakane daddy, domin jikina yayi sanyi duniya babu amana mutane duk sun gurbace, son duniya yayi musu yawa kowa so yake yayi arziki ba tare da ya wahala ba, Burinsu wani yayi musu wahalar su su kwashe kudin , shin ina son Kai da son rai zai kaimu ne"?.

   Alh Mustapha labaran ya 'Allah dai yasa mu fi karfin zuciyar mu, yanzu yamma tayi Amma gobe Zan aje duk abinda nake Zan je duk sauran kamfanonin na zauna da manajojin, na ji inda aka kwana". Mujaheeda tace "hakan yayi daddy Allah ya kaimu goben". Daganan ya koma wajen Haj murjanatu da ta dauke wuta ta rasa inda kanta yake komai sai juya Mata yake akai. Haka Alh Mustapha ya dinga kwantar Mata da hankali tare da nuna Mata sai inda karfinsa ya kare, ta kwantar da hankalinta. Amma har mujaheeda tabar gidan Haj murjanatu bata dawo dai dai ba.

     Washegari tun safe Alh Mustapha labaran ya fita zuwa sauran kamfanoni hudu na Haj murjanatu domin sanin halin da ake ciki. Ita kuma mujaheeda ta je kamfaninta ta hada ma'aikatan tayi musu bayanin komai Wanda hakanne zaisa ta rufe kamfanin a yau har sai zuwa lokacin  da komai zai daidaita.hankalin ma'aikatan yayi mummunan tashi sai dai babu yadda zasu yi domin babu yadda zaayi su dinga yin aiki babu albashi. Tana gamawa da kamfaninta gidansu ta wuce.
   Hankalin Haj murjanatu idan yayi dubu ya tashi ko abinci daker mujaheeda ta matsa Mata ta ci. Hankalin ta bai Kara tashi ba sai da Alh Mustapha labaran ya dawo da nashi labarin.
  Ko wanne kamfani ga file dinsa nan na inda akayi da kudinsa, kusan duk kamfanonin kudaden da suka dinga turama Haj murjanatu zamanin zamansu a Germany da kudaden jinyar mujaheeda da kudin aikin zuciya da akayi Mata  duk sune suka karya kamfanonin Suma maganar da ake yanzu babu kudi a kamfanonin ma'aikata na bin albashin watanni hudu wasu uku , babu Kuma kayan da zaa dinga sarrafawa ana fitarwa, duk a bayanan manajojin sunce babu wata hanya da zasuyi magana da Haj murjanatu balle su Gaya Mata halin da ake ciki, tunda ta kirasu sau daya ta Basu dollar account dinta ta sanar dasu duk bayan wata daya ga adadin kudin da zasu dinga sa wa a cikin account din shikenan Basu Kara ji daga gareta ba sai dai sun cigaba da cika umarninta.

DAGA TASKAR YAR GIDAN IMAM

NI MA MATARSA CE
                  64

        Tashin hankali Wanda ba'asa masa rana, gaba daya neman yawun bakinta Haj murjanatu tayi ta rasa, sai wani daci da makaki da zuciyarta take yi, tayi ma Àlh Mustapha labaran kuri da idanunta.
   Ya jinjina Kai ya dubeta sosai yace "ki kwantar da hankalinki, dukkan bayanan da manajojinki suka min da Wanda na karanta a cikin files din nan gaba daya na gamsu da Shi, ba su cuceki ba kamar da jibrin ya cutar da ke, don haka hakuri ki Sami natsuwa".
   Ta dube Shi Ido cike da hawaye tace "ta yaya Zan kwantar da hankalina, bayan asirina Yana neman tonuwa a duniya, Alh idan na rasa kudi na rasa komai domin kudin nan sune ni,". Ya girgiza Kai yace "ki daina fadin haka domin kudi basu bane komai, kiyi tawakkali , don yanzu ma naba manajojinki umarnin su sallami ma'aikatan su rufe kamfanin batun bashiin da suke bi Kuma in sha Allah idan Allah ya buda duk zaa biya su ".
  Mujaheeda ta goge hawayenta tace "gara da kasa a rufe din daddy, Nima na rufe nawa, Allah ya bamu mafita"ya dubeta yace "Amin". Daganan ya juya wajen Haj murjanatu irin tsantsar damuwar da take ciki yasa ya cigaba da bata hakuri gami da bata misalai Kala Kala bai gushe Yana bata baki ba har sai da ta sakko ta dawo hayyacinta ta Kuma tabbatar da bata da wata mafita da ya wuce yin hakuri tare da fauwalama Allah komai sosai tsoron Allah yakamata domin ta Kara tabbatar da shine yake tsara komai yadda ya ga Dama talakawan da take rainawa haka Allah ya tsara musu rayuwarsu Kuma ko yaushe ya ga Dama zai iya azurtasu kamar yadda ya azurtata gashi Kuma lokaci daya ya talautata, komai daga gare Shi yake.

...faruwar wannan al'amarin gaba daya ya Kara canja Haj murjanatu, ta Kara fahimtar duk abinda Allah ya kaddara ma bawa ba Shi da yadda zaiyi, 
   Alh jibrin dai Yayi batan dabo domin har yanzu babu labarinsa, duk binciken da ake yi har yanzu babu labarinsa.

  Muhseena ta kammala bautar kasarta da wata daya ta haihu, ta haifo danta sankacece kyakkyawa inda ya ci sunan Alh Yusuf Lamido, suna kiransa da Hanif.
  Mujaheeda da ita akayi komai na suna gwanin ban sha'awa, kwarai da gaske mujaheeda ta canja, Shi Kansa Taufiq ya yarda da canjawarta, zaman lafiya ya wanzu a gidan Taufiq kowacce rige rigen faranta ransa take yi, zamansu da ban da ban ya taimaka wajen Samun zaman lafiyarsu.

    Ma'aikatar da muhseena tayi bautar kasa NNPC ,sakamakon hazakar da ta nuna da Kuma hanyar da Alh Sani yayi Mata suka dauketa aiki,Samun aiki a irin wannan wajen ba abu bane na wasa sai mai rabo hakanne yasa murna awajen muhseena da Taufiq da duk wani masoyinta baa magana domin dai yanzu muhseena ta kerema saa.
   Sai dai Nisan da muhseena zata yima Taufiq shine damuwarsa amma babu yadda zaiyi tunda cigaba ne ta samu.
   Wata hudu bayan haihuwar muhseena ta tattara ta koma Kaduna , kudaden da wajen ajikinta suka bata domin ta kama gida da sauran bukatu sune ta hada gaba daya ta sayi gida a Kadunan.
   Duk sati Taufiq ke kokarin zuwa Kaduna nan yake yin weekend dinsa, sau tari wani zubin Yana zuwa da fadwa Wanda ba dan makaranta ba da  tayi zamanta a Kadunan kasancewar yadda kullum zai koma da kuka ake rabuwa.
              **********
   Rayuwa ta Mika lokaci Yana ta tafiya , alokacin da Allah ya azurta muhseena alokacin Allah ya talauta Haj murjanatu da mujaheeda. Domin har zuwa wannan lokacin baasan inda Alh jibrin yake ba, Yan ragowar kudaden da suke cikin asusun ajiyarsu yau da gobe sun sa duk sun kare.Rayuwa ta Fara yima Haj murjanatu wahala abinda tafi karfinsa Yanzu yafi karfinta. Yanzu gaba daya hidimarta ta koma hannun Alh Mustapha Wanda da take ganin tafi karfin ta tsaya yayi Mata hidimar rayuwarta duk da Shi Allah ya dorama yayi Mata, a dole yanzu ta yarda da cewa itace matar shine Mijin wannan gadarar da izzar duk ta sauke su domin babu abinda zatayi gadarar da Shi tunda ba tafi karfin komai ba, kullum cikin damuwa da tashin hankali take Shi Kuma Alh Mustapha Yana tausarta Wanda ba don asara da tayi ba da yayi ma Allah godiya da yasa ta rasa komai domin hakan ne yayi sanadiyyar da ya mallaki matarsa yanzu yasan cewa Yana da Mata , tunda zai ce ayi ayi agidansa kamar yadda zaice a bari a bari, ya Kuma samu damar yin cafane kamar kowane magidanci sabanin da da sai dai adafa a bashi babu me cewa asayo kaza, ko raayi yayi ya sayo baya burgeta don ba lalle bane ayi amfani da Shi,. Unguwa bata tambayarsa amma yanzu Saita tambaya, Yan aiki duk shine yake biyansu Shi yasa yake iko kamar kowane magidanci.
  Itama can mujaheeda tuni ta Gama yarda da cewa mijinta shine gatanta tana binsa sauda Kafa, tsakaninta da muhseena Kuma babu komai sai zaman lafiya. Ayayinda Alh yusuf lamido yake jaddadama Taufiq kullum Kada ya sake ko da wasa ya wulakanta mujaheeda ko ya ci zarafinta domin tana nan amatsayin yar gaban goshinsa , Shi yasa yafi kowa jindadi da murnar gyaruwar halayen mujaheeda da mahaifiyarta.

    Tabangaren muhseena kuwa rayuwa sai Alhamdulillah domin ta mallaki duk abinda take mafarkin mallaka a rayuwa, kudi kima da darajar da take ganin bata da su a da ,ayanxu duk ta hadasu.

    Acikin shekaru uku da Fara aikinta ta cika burika da yawa a rayuwarta.
  Ta sayama mahaifanta gida me kyau a Kaduna. Su Hajara da hajja Ande sun bar Durmi sun dawo Kaduna cikin gata, hajja Ande kwana tayi ranar tana kuka tare da Sama muhseena albarka, abinda bata taba tunanin zai faru ba yau shine yake faruwa, muhseena da take ganinta wulakantacciya itace ta Zama gatansu , itace komai na su itace abin alfarinsu itace sanyin idaniyarsu.
   A ranar har kasa ta durkusa tana yima muhseena godiya tare da neman afuwarta, ta durkusa ta dagota tace "haba Ande kinfi karfin haka a wajena na rantse babu abinda bazan Miki ba domin Bani da kamarku, ki manta da faruwar komai Ki jidadinki kakata".gaba daya suka sa dariya. Mallam Umar ya dubeta yace "Allah yayi Miki albarka muhseena Dama ni nasan sai nayi alfari da ke a duniya kafin Allah ya dauki raina, Allah yayi Miki albarka Allah yasa kifi haka".
   Muhseena ta zuba masa Ido kaunarsa na Kara kamata tace "Babana burina kullum na faranta ranka in dai akwai albarkarka in sha Allah cigaba yanzu aka Fara gani".
  Yayi murmushi yace "Allah yayi Miki albarka Allah ya dafa Miki".
Itama Hajara kukan dadi tayi tayi tana sa Mata albarka duk wañnan katon gidan ace na sune a gari kamar Kaduna hakika haihuwar muhseena tayi Mata Rana, duk wani bakinciki data taba yi yau ya kau.

    Garin Durmi asibiti daya ne har yanzu , muhseena tasa aka gina katon asibiti, duk inda Ka bi garin Durmi maganar muhseena ake yi , kowa so yake ma ya ganta , Amma tana daga gefe sai ruwan alhairai take ta yi musu, ga asibiti ga ma'aikata da aka diba ga ruwa data sa aka janyo musu. Hatta megari sai da ya nemeta da manyan fadarsa SU kayi ta Mata godiya , megari yaji kunyar abubuwan da suka faru abaya Bai ma Kara yin nadama ba sai da muhseena ta biya masa kujerar aikin hajji da Shi da Mallam kallah, domin ba zata taba manta cewa megari shine silar farincikinta domin shine yayi sanadiyyar aurenta da Taufiq Wanda hakan ya Zama alheri a rayuwarta Mallam kallah Kuma duk da mahaifiyarta wahala ta ci a gidansa to ba zata taba manta cewa ya taimakama mahaifiyarta da wajen Zama ba alokacin da kowa ya ki ta.
   Wai aiko bakin mallam kallah har kunne Shi da matarsa itama tana adduar Allah yasa wata rana itama ta biya Mata .

  Har Kaduna megari da Mallam kallah suka je sukayi Mata godiya, mallam kallah kallon gidanta kawai yake Yana fadin "ikon Allah tikisa Zainabu kece da wannan gida Allah abin tsoro".
   Ta Basu kudi masu yawa na mota suka tafi cike da al'ajabi da tsoron Allah,domin ganin abinda basu taba mafarkin faruwa ba Ya faru. Duk inda kabi kauyen Durmi maganar muhseena ake yi da irin cigaba da canjin da ta samu a rayuwarta.
   A wannan lokacin ta kare dire danta na biyu Wanda ya ci sunan Mallam Umar ana kiransa da Ansar, tsakaninsu wata uku da mujaheeda itama ta haifo yarta mace inda aka sa Mata Hajara ta ci sunan mahaifiyar muhseena Wanda hakan ba karamin jindadi Hajara da muhseena sukayi ba, domin komai na sunan Hajara muhseena ta dauke bayan sha Tara na arziki da ta saukema mujaheeda da Hajara wacce zaa dinga kiranta da Aleena.

  Hadin Kai sosai mujaheeda da muhseena suke yi Basu yarda shaidan ya shiga tsakaninsu ba domin ko shaidan ya shiga tsakaninsu sun Sami matsala to da sauri suke gyara matsalar basa bari abin yayi nisa.
    Dole mujaheeda ta nemi aiki da takardunta saboda abubuwa duk sun canja, ta fahimci dole ta tashi ta nemi na kanta abinda ba'a taba nuna Mata ba saboda Haj murjanatu ta manta ba kowane abu bane yake tabbata  kamar yadda ayanzu rayuwar ta canja musu.
    Amma Alh Mustapha labaran da Kansa ya dauketa aiki a kamfaninsa tunda ta dage sai ta nemi aiki.
   Haj murjanatu ta na matukar jindadin yadda Alh Mustapha bai wofintar da su ba duk da rashi da ya samesu , hakan ya tabbatar Mata da cewa domin Allah Alh Mustapha yake tare da su, tunda talaucinsu da Samun yarsa ta jini baisa ya juya musu baya ba , Alokacin ne ta ji zuciyarta ta aminta da ta yafe masa duk abinda ya yi Mata, ta Kuma yafe masa, alokacin da ta gaya masa ta yafe masa, ji yayi kamar an cire masa Kaya, ya dubeta sosai yace "Na dade da son Jin wannan maganar daga bakinki ,ban Samun  kwanciyar hankali matukar na tuna har yanzu baki yafe min ba,to nagode Allah ya yafe min ". Hankalin sa ya Kara kwanciya kwarai da gaske.

   Taufiq cike yake da alfari da muhseena kullum Kuma kaunarta tana Kara Samun matsuguni acikin zuciyarsa. Gidajensa sun Zama biyu Kaduna da jigawa duk Kuma da tazarar da ke tsakani Bai taba gazawa ko fashin zuwa wajen muhseena ba,Sam ma ya daina ganin nisan wajen.Haka zalika shima tabangaren aikinsa Yana ta Samun cigaba domin ya Zama shugaban shashin kasuwanci na kamfanin duk wata kwangilar  hanya da za'a bayar ta hannunsa yake zuwa arziki ya ci uban na da sai hamdala kawai.

   Wannan shekarar mallam Umar da Hajara da Ande dije suka sauke farali muhseena ta biya musu. Ande dije dai ko bayan dawowarsu hakanan Saita rushe da kukan dadi ita kanta cike take da kakabin wai ta sauke farali Kuma ta dalilin muhseena tsakani da Allah taga ishara ita Kam yanzu ko cewa muhseena da Hajara sukayi ta kwanta su takata su wuce to zata kwanta ta gamsu da cewa muhseena itace gatanta.

   Wata shekarar tana zagayowa ta biyama mallam Hamza wanzami mahaifin Kabir, domin dai har kasa ta nade ba zata taba mantawa da Kabir ba domin Yana cikin tarihin rayuwarta. Alokacin da labarin biyan makkan ya Sami Mallam Hamza wanzami rasa inda zaisa Kansa yayi saboda tsananin kunya da nadamar abubuwan da suka faru a baya, Dama tun kafin faruwar hakan yayi nadama saboda irin abin arzikin da muhseena take yi a garin da irin yadda Allah ya daukakata a matsayin ta na wacce suke kyamata yau gashi tafi wasu dubban yayan da aka Haifa ta hanyar aure.

    Shima har Kaduna ya je ya nemi gidanta ya je yayi Mata godiya kwarai da gaske nan ma bata barshi haka ba sai da ta bashi kudi masu kauri, lalle arzikin bawa Yana kashinsa Mallam Hamza wanzami ya tsinke da lamarin duniya.
   Muhseena bata San inda Kabir ya Sami number ta ba, wata ranar laraba tana zaune a office kiransa ya shigo wayarta, sallama kawai yayi ta ji wani abu ya tsirga a cikin jijiyoyin jikinta, "kin gane me magana".
Ta numfasa a hankali tace 'ko cikin wane Hali nake Kabir idan naji muryarka ai Zan gane".
    Jikin Kabir ya Kara yin sanyi kaunar muhseena da duk tsawon shekarun nan yake dakonsa ya shiga dawowa cikin zuciyarsa, cikin taushin murya yace "Baki manta da muryata ba muhseena duk tsawon shekarun nan ". Ta girgiza Kai tace "Ta yaya Zan manta, ya gida ya aiki ". A sanyaye yace "duk lafiya lau",daganan ya cigaba da yi Mata godiya, tace "Don nayi ma mahaifinka wani abu bai kamata kayi min godiya ba, domin har yanzu ban manta irin hidima da kayi da ni arayuwata ba, Kaine ke sayama mahaifiyata magani Kai ke bamu abinda zamu ci agidan mu, Kai Ka Fara nuna min akwai jindadi a duniya shin ta yaya Zan manta da Kai ko alhairinka agare ni,". Kamar Wanda ya Fado daka Kan gado Kabir yace cikin tsumuwa da maganganun muhseena "ina sonki,har abada bazan taba son wata yadda nake sonki ba tsawon wannan shekarun har yanzu son ki na nan acikin zuciyata babu abinda ya canja".tausayin Kabir yayi matukar kamata yadda yake maganar kadai ya tabbatar Mata da gaske har yanzu tana cikin ransa.wanda ita ayanzu mutuncinsa da kimarsa kawai take gani amma babu sonsa aranta ta tattara duk taba Taufiq, tace "ya sunan matarka?", Ya numfasa yace "sunanta Habiba, bayan rabuwar mu na kasa hakura da ke hakan yasa na kasa Kara yin soyayya da ko wace mace a dalilin hakan kawuna Alh Musa da mahaifana suka yanke shawarar hada ni aure da yar kawu musan Habiba, ba nj da wani zabi da ya wuce na yarda da zabinsu domin dai nasan har abada babu ranar da Zan gabatar da wata mace a matsayin wacce nake so, yar mu daya da ita Zainab".
  Muhseena ta aje numfashi da karfi tace "Zainab?".
   Yace "eh, sunanki nasa Mata"
Tace "don Allah Kada Ka bari tasan sunan wacece kasama yarta, don Bana son ta tsaneta".
  Yayi murmushi yace "Aita sani, Habiba ta bambamta da sauran Mata bata da irin wannan kishin na kwatance, ta San sunanki nasa domin tasan komai akanki, kin San me?" 
   Ta girgiza Kai kamar yana ganinta tace "aah"
Yace "wani lokacin har tsokana ta take tace zo nan muhseena masoyiyar mijina ".
  Muhseena tace da sauri "itama muhseena ake kiranta".
 Ya gyada Kai yace "haka Muke kiranta", tace"Allah yayi Mata albarka, don Allah Kabir Ka hadani zumunci da Habiba, Ka nemi ni ta whatapp da number nan Ka tura min hotunan ya ta ina son na ganta".
  Yace "in sha Allah duk zanyi hakan, yaranki nawa yanzu"tace "yarana biyu Yusuf (Hanif) da Umar (Ansar), da nace na Gama haihuwa amma yanzu Nima in sha Allah zanyi maka takwara".
 Gaba daya suka sa dariya sun Dade suna hira kafin suyi sallama.

  Wannan shine asalin Fara zumuncin muhseena da matar Kabir Habiba, zumunci suke sosai tare da yayansu.
    
             *************

    Duk zuwa wannan shekarun Alh Mustapha labaran bai taba gajiyawa ba wajen neman Alh jibrin.
   Cikin ikon Allah yansanda sun Sami nasarar cafke Alh jibrin a wani yammaci da ya shigo garin jigawa ba tare da Sanin cewa yansanda suna saye da Ido Akan shi.an kamo Shi  zuwa hedkwatar yansanda, dpo Yana Kiran Alh Mustapha labaran ya sanar da Shi Bai Jira komai ba ya dauki Haj murjanatu suka tafi.  
       Haj murjanatu ta tsinema Alh jibrin yadda yakamata ta Kuma tabbatar masa da cewa duk abinda ya ci nata sai ya fito da Shi, Alh Mustapha yace "wannan Kuma aikin hukuma ne, ki barsu suyi aikinsu". Ya waiwaya wajen dpo yace ",yallabai duk abinda ya dace ayi don Allah ayi duk kudaden da ya nade na kamfani ya dawo da su ko Sisi Kada yayi ciwon Kai". Dpo yace "in sha Allah Kada kadamu zamuyi iya bakin kokarin mu akan hakan". Daganan suka dawo gida suka bar Alh jibrin tsare a wajen yansanda cikin tuhuma.
  Kwarai da gaske mujaheeda ma ta jidadin kama Alh jibrin da akayi, domin ita harma ta cire rai duba da irin tsawon lokacin da aka diba ba tare da an ganshi ba.

 Yansanda sun Sami nasarar bincikar bayan barinsa jigawa Katsina ya koma da Zama inda kudaden da ya sata ya shiga kasuwanci hakanne yasa kudaden Basu durkushe ba, ayanxu Kuma wulakanta da tijarar da ya gani a wajen yansanda Wanda suke Shirin tura Shi kotu yasa ya amince da zai dawo da kudaden duk da yasan lokacin durkushewarsa yayi domin ba kananan kudade ya samu a kamfanin ba. Dama abinda suke bukata Kenan ya dawo da kudin tunda Kuma ya dawo da su Alh Mustapha da Kansa ya ce a sake Shi . Haka aka sake Shi ya koma cikin talauci tunda bai godema abinda Allah ya azurta Shi da Shi ba sai ya ci amana, ga  shinan hatta na shin da ya raina ya rasa domin sun shige cikin kudaden da ya dawo ma da Haj murjanatu.
   Alhamdulillah tuni Haj murjanatu ta farfado da kamfaninta , mujaheeda ta koma office dinta,talaucin da ya zamar musu ishara ya yaye arzikinsu ya dawo,sai dai Haj murjanatu tayi alkawarin ba Zata Kara komawa halinta na baya ba wanna izzar ta barta har abada.

  Haka rayuwa ta cigaba da tafiya Allah Yana tsara komai yadda ya ga dama, abinda mutum bai taba tunanin faruwar sa ba shine ya ke ta faruwa domin Allah ya nuna ma kowa cewa shine me yin yadda ya so. Muhseena ta Sami daukaka ta Zama abin alfarin kowa, tabangaren wajen aikinta ma ta Sami daukaka domin hazakarta yasa tana ta Samun cigaba.
  Hajara tayi fes idan ba labari aka baka ba ba zaka taba yarda da cewa ta taba dandanar dacin talauci ba. Daga ita har mallam Umar da Hajja Ande kullum cikin sa ma muhseena albarka su keyi. Haka Alh Mustapha labaran shima kullum cikin yabo da sa ma yayansa albarka yake musamman a bangaren zaman aurensu da suka ba mara da kunya suka rike junansu da zuciya daya cikin gaskiya da amana. 

  Ayayin da adalcin da Taufiq yake kwatantawa tsakanin muhseena da mujaheeda ya sa gidansa ya Kara Samun zaman lafiya da kwanciyar hankali , ga yawaitar arziki.

    Duk da irin daukakar da muhseena ta samu a wajen aikinta, bata taba fifita aikinta akan aurenta ba, duk runtsi bata taba tauye hakkin mijinta ba duk abinda yake so Shi take yi , tana daya daga cikin Mata masu biyayyar aure da zaa iya yin kwatance da su.

  Shi yasa har yanzu Taufiq bsi hango wata kamarta ba,
  
Muhseena ta Sami hutun karshen shekara hakan yasa ta kwashe yayanta Hanif da Ansar da yayan mujaheeda fadwa da Aleena sai yarinyar Kabir muhseena suka daga zuwa Dubai domin ta samu ta huta,
  A Daren ranar da ta dauka a Dubai suna waya da Taufiq Wanda yake Jin kamar ya kutso ya shigo ta cikin wayar "kin kwashe yayanki kin tafi kin bar yayanki cikin kewa".
  Tayi murmushi tace "yaya Taufiq kaima kasan idan akwai abinda ke min kuna a raina bai wuce nisantarka ba, amma ina danne wasu abubuwan da yawa saboda Kada na kawo matsala tsakanina da anti mujaheeda, domin tabbas nasan kishi idan na taho tare da Kai dole sai ta ji babu dadi sai shaidan ya buga gangarsa ni Kuma Bana fatan ace nice silar Samun Wata baraka a tsakaninmu".
  Ya lumshe Ido ya bude yace "Allah na gode maka da Ka ni'imta ni da Samun abokiyar rayuwa irinki muhseena, ina sonki ina sonki". Tayi murmushi tace "Ni ma ina sonka yayana, Kai wani bangare ne na rayuwata, awajena baka da tamka", ya aje numfashi me dauke da sakonni Kala Kala yace "har na Fara kewarki ke din ta dabance lalle Allah yayi gaskiya da baisa namiji yayi adalci a cikin soyayyar matansa ba, saboda yadda nake Jin sonki araina na rantse tamkar babu wasu Mata a duniya ban taba son wata mace yadda nake sonki ba".
   Cikin  jindadin maganganun sa tace " Shi yasa kullum nake gaya maka Ka dinga kokarin boye wannan soyayyar agaban anti mujaheeda domin hakan zai iya dasa Mata damuwa ni Kuma Bana son hakan".
  Yace "ina kokarin boyewa sosai muhseena " tace "Allah ya barmu tare " "Amin ya Allah gimbiyata". Daga karshe su kayi sallama 
  Tayi kasa da wayar gami da  daga hannayenta biyu tana yi ma Allah godiya, "Allah Alhamdulillah nagode maka da dukkan ni',imominka gare ni nagode Alhamdulillah Alhamdulillah Alhamdulillah".
  Ta maida hannayenta kasa idanunta cike da hawaye tace idan ba Allah ba waye zai maka wannan, na Sami budin arziki, na Sami daukaka, na Zama abar sha'awa kowa na so ya San ni yayi mu'amala da ni, ga iyayena na cikin daula da kwanciyar hankali, ga Kuma miji daya tamkar da dubu da Allah ya ba ni , duk burika na na rayuwa Allah ya cika min.
  Ta goge hawayenta, tana fatan cigaba da rayuwa a yadda take yin ta yanzu.

         ALHAMDULILLAH
SAI MUN HADU A SABON LITTAFINA MAI SUNA, A MACE KO A RAYE.
     DAGA TASKAR YAR GIDAN IMAM.
               MRS ALIYU MAYARE
